ABIS Pro admin console: update bundle

Adds the Operators, Coupons (with bulk tasks), Campaigns and Settings modules,
shared LocationPicker / SwitchCard / ChipGroup components, and the routes in src/app/modules.tsx.

Apply from the project root:  node apply-bundle.mjs bundle.txt
Check first without changing: node apply-bundle.mjs --dry-run bundle.txt

Files: 70 written, 2 deleted (they moved to src/components/ui).

import {

} from '@ant-design/icons';
import type { ComponentType, ReactNode } from 'react';
import { matchPath } from 'react-router-dom';

/**
 * Every module (page) of the admin portal, in ONE place.
 * The sidebar, the router and the header title all read from this file.
 *
 * To add a new module: add one entry below. Nothing else needs to change.
 */

export type AppModule = {
  /** URL path and menu key. 'orders' → /orders */
  key: string;
  /** Text in the sidebar and in the header title */
  label: string;
  icon: ReactNode;
  /** Loads the page only when it's opened (keeps the first download small) */
  loadPage: () => Promise<{ default: ComponentType }>;
};

export type ModuleGroup = {
  label: string;
  modules: AppModule[];
};

export const dashboardModule: AppModule = {
  key: 'dashboard',
  label: 'Dashboard',
  icon: <DashboardOutlined />,
  loadPage: () => import('@/features/dashboard/pages/DashboardPage'),
};

export const allPages: AppModule[] = [
  dashboardModule,
  ...moduleGroups.flatMap((group) => group.modules),
  settingsModule,
  profilePage,
  createOutletPage,
  createAppPage,
  editAppPage,
  bannersPage,
  createBannerPage,
  manageCatalogPage,
  masterMappingPage,
  createGalleryPage,

  createMediaItemPage,

  createOperatorPage,
  editOperatorPage,
  createCouponPage,
  editCouponPage,
  couponTasksPage,
  newCouponTaskPage,
  couponTaskDetailsPage,
  // 'campaigns/new' must come before 'campaigns/:campaignId', or the header would say "Campaign details".
  newCampaignPage,
  campaignDetailsPage,
  forceUpdateUsersPage,
  nonServiceableUsersPage,
  userTasksPage,
  newUserTaskPage,
  userTaskDetailsPage,
];

const pageTitles = new Map(allPages.map((page) => [page.key, page.label]));

/** '/orders/123' → 'orders' */
export function getModuleKey(pathname: string): string {
  return pathname.split('/')[1] ?? '';
}

/**
 * '/orders' → 'Orders', '/outlets/new' → 'Create outlet', '/apps/42/edit' → 'Edit app'.
 * Looks for a page that matches the full address first, then falls back to the module
 * ('/orders/123' → 'Orders').
 */
export function getPageTitle(pathname: string): string {
  const page = allPages.find((candidate) => matchPath(`/${candidate.key}`, pathname));
  return page?.label ?? pageTitles.get(getModuleKey(pathname)) ?? '';
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/ChipGroup.tsx =====
import { Checkbox, type GetProps } from 'antd';
import './ChipGroup.css';

type ChipGroupProps = GetProps<typeof Checkbox.Group>;

/**
 * A group of choices drawn as pills that turn on and off. They are still real checkboxes,
 * so keyboard and screen readers work, and a disabled form disables them too.
 */
export function ChipGroup({ className, ...props }: ChipGroupProps) {
  return <Checkbox.Group className={`chip-group${className ? ` ${className}` : ''}`} {...props} />;
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/LocationPicker.tsx =====
import { AimOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { App, Button, Flex, Typography } from 'antd';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CircleMarker, MapContainer, TileLayer, useMap, useMapEvents } from 'react-leaflet';
import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
import { env } from '@/config/env';
import { colors } from '@/theme';
import './LocationPicker.css';

export type Coordinates = { lat: number; lng: number };

/** Where the map opens when no location is set yet (Raipur). */
const DEFAULT_CENTER: Coordinates = { lat: 21.2514, lng: 81.6296 };
const DEFAULT_ZOOM = 12;
const PICKED_ZOOM = 16;

/** About 10 cm of precision: more decimals add no value. */
function round(value: number): number {
  return Math.round(value * 1_000_000) / 1_000_000;
}

function ClickToPick({ onPick }: { onPick: (position: Coordinates) => void }) {
  useMapEvents({
    click: (event) => onPick({ lat: round(event.latlng.lat), lng: round(event.latlng.lng) }),
  });
  return null;
}

/** Moves the map when the coordinates are typed in by hand. */
function FollowPosition({ lat, lng }: { lat: number; lng: number }) {
  const map = useMap();
  useEffect(() => {
    map.panTo({ lat, lng });
  }, [map, lat, lng]);
  return null;
}

type LocationPickerProps = {
  /** The current point, or undefined when none is set. */
  position?: Coordinates;
  onPick: (position: Coordinates) => void;
  /** Short instruction above the map. */
  hint?: string;
};

/**
 * Map for choosing an exact location: click the map, or use the browser's current
 * location. Put latitude/longitude inputs next to it: they remain the
 * keyboard-accessible way to set the same values.
 */
export function LocationPicker({
  position,
  onPick,
  hint = 'Click the map to set the exact position.',
}: LocationPickerProps) {
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const [isLocating, setIsLocating] = useState(false);

  const useCurrentLocation = () => {
    if (!('geolocation' in navigator)) {
      void message.error('Your browser cannot share its location.');
      return;
    }
    setIsLocating(true);
    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
      ({ coords }) => {
        setIsLocating(false);
        onPick({ lat: round(coords.latitude), lng: round(coords.longitude) });
      },
      () => {
        setIsLocating(false);
        void message.error('Could not get your location. Allow location access or pick the point on the map.');
      },
      { enableHighAccuracy: true, timeout: 10_000 },
    );
  };

  return (
    <div className="location-picker">
      <Flex align="center" justify="space-between" gap={8} wrap className="location-picker__bar">
        <Typography.Text className="location-picker__hint">{hint}</Typography.Text>
        <Button size="small" icon={<AimOutlined />} loading={isLocating} onClick={useCurrentLocation}>
          Use my location
        </Button>
      </Flex>
      <MapContainer
        className="location-picker__map"
        center={position ?? DEFAULT_CENTER}
        zoom={position ? PICKED_ZOOM : DEFAULT_ZOOM}
        scrollWheelZoom={false}
      >
        <TileLayer url={env.VITE_MAP_TILE_URL} attribution={env.VITE_MAP_TILE_ATTRIBUTION} />
        <ClickToPick onPick={onPick} />
        {position && (
          <>
            <FollowPosition lat={position.lat} lng={position.lng} />
            <CircleMarker
              center={position}
              radius={9}
              pathOptions={{ color: colors.primary, fillColor: colors.accent, fillOpacity: 1, weight: 3 }}
            />
          </>
        )}
      </MapContainer>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/components/ui/SwitchCard.tsx =====
import { Form, Switch } from 'antd';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import './SwitchCard.css';

type SwitchCardProps = {
  /** The true/false form field the switch controls, for example 'isActive' or ['invite', 'enabled']. */
  name: string | (string | number)[];
  title: string;
  hint: ReactNode;
  /** Extra fields shown only while the switch is on. */
  children?: ReactNode;
};

/**
 * An on/off option drawn as a card: title and hint on the left, switch on the right.
 * Fields that only matter when the option is on are shown under it, so long forms stay short.
 * Use it inside a <Form>.
 */
export function SwitchCard({ name, title, hint, children }: SwitchCardProps) {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance();
  const isOn = Form.useWatch(name, form) === true;
  const id = `switch-${[name].flat().join('-')}`;

  return (
    <div className={`switch-option${isOn ? ' switch-option--on' : ''}`}>
      <div className="switch-option__head">
        <div className="switch-option__text">
          <label htmlFor={id} className="switch-option__title">
            {title}
          </label>
          <span className="switch-option__hint">{hint}</span>
        </div>
        <Form.Item name={name} valuePropName="checked" noStyle>
          <Switch id={id} />
        </Form.Item>
      </div>
      {children && isOn && <div className="switch-option__body">{children}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/components/ui/SwitchCard.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/api/campaigns-api.ts =====
import { fakeNetworkDelay, mockCampaigns, samplePhones } from '@/features/campaigns/api/mock-campaigns';
import type { CampaignPayload } from '@/features/campaigns/schemas/campaign.schema';
import type { Campaign, CampaignId, CampaignSummary } from '@/features/campaigns/types';

/**
 * SAMPLE: these functions work on the sample data in `mock-campaigns.ts` so the whole flow
 * can be tried. Each one has a TODO showing the real call. When the API is ready, only the
 * `api` files change: the pages keep working as they are.
 */

function toSummary(campaign: Campaign): CampaignSummary {
  const { id, name, title, status, media, totalUsers, successCount, failCount, scheduledAt, createdAt } = campaign;
  return { id, name, title, status, media, totalUsers, successCount, failCount, scheduledAt, createdAt };
}

/**
 * Every campaign, newest first. The list doesn't need the phone numbers, so the server should leave them out.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<CampaignSummary[]>('/campaigns');
 *       return data;
 * If there are many thousands of campaigns, load them in pages instead.
 */
export async function listCampaigns(): Promise<CampaignSummary[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return mockCampaigns.map(toSummary);
}

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<Campaign>(`/campaigns/${encodeURIComponent(campaignId)}`);
 *       return data;
 * (encodeURIComponent keeps a strange id in the address bar from changing the request path.)
 */
export async function getCampaign(campaignId: CampaignId): Promise<Campaign> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const campaign = mockCampaigns.find((item) => item.id === campaignId);
  if (!campaign) throw new Error('Campaign not found');
  return structuredClone(campaign);
}

export type CreateCampaignInput = {
  payload: CampaignPayload;
  /** New picture chosen in the form (push only). */
  picture?: File;
  /** Picture of the campaign this one was copied from, kept when no new picture is chosen. */
  pictureUrl?: string;
};

/**
 * Creates a campaign. Without a scheduled time the server starts sending right away.
 * The server must check the picture's type and size again, and who may send to all users.
 *
 * TODO: const body = new FormData();
 *       body.append('campaign', JSON.stringify({ ...payload, pictureUrl }));
 *       if (picture) body.append('picture', picture);
 *       const { data } = await apiClient.post<Campaign>('/campaigns', body);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function createCampaign({ payload, picture, pictureUrl }: CreateCampaignInput): Promise<Campaign> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const now = new Date().toISOString();
  const isScheduled = payload.scheduledAt !== undefined;
  const targetPhones =
    payload.audience === 'SPECIFIC' ? payload.phoneNumbers : samplePhones(120, mockCampaigns.length + 1);

  const campaign: Campaign = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: payload.name,
    title: payload.title,
    message: payload.message,
    media: payload.media,
    appId: payload.appId,
    segment: payload.segment,
    audience: payload.audience,
    // The sample keeps the picture in this browser tab only. The real server returns its own link.
    pictureUrl: payload.media === 'PUSH' ? (picture ? URL.createObjectURL(picture) : pictureUrl) : undefined,
    status: isScheduled ? 'SCHEDULED' : 'PROCESSING',
    scheduledAt: payload.scheduledAt ?? now,
    createdAt: now,
    updatedAt: now,
    totalUsers: isScheduled ? 0 : targetPhones.length,
    matchingDevices: 0,
    successCount: 0,
    failCount: 0,
    targetPhones: isScheduled ? [] : targetPhones,
    failedPhones: [],
  };
  mockCampaigns.unshift(campaign);
  return structuredClone(campaign);
}

import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import type { Campaign } from '@/features/campaigns/types';

/**
 * SAMPLE DATA: used until the campaigns API is connected.
 * Replace the functions in `campaigns-api.ts` with real calls, then delete this file.
 */

const FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS = 400;

/** Waits a little, like a real request would. */
export function fakeNetworkDelay(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS));
}

/** India time, like the old console. */
function ist(text: string): string {
  return dayjs(`${text}+05:30`).toISOString();
}

/** Made-up but valid-looking 10-digit mobile numbers, the same every time for the same seed. */
export function samplePhones(count: number, seed: number): string[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => {
    const n = (seed * 7_919 + index * 104_729) % 400_000_000;
    return String(6_000_000_000 + n * 9 + ((index * seed) % 9));
  });
}

type SampleRow = {
  name: string;
  total: number;
  success: number;
  fail: number;
  scheduled: string;
  created: string;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
};

const NEW_CUSTOMER_MESSAGE = 'Welcome to Abis! Use NEW25 for 25% OFF your first order. Fresh meat, delivered fast.';

function toCampaign(row: SampleRow, index: number): Campaign {
  const androidSuccess = index === 0 ? 486 : Math.round(row.success * 0.85);
  const androidFail = index === 0 ? 37 : Math.round(row.fail * 0.85);
  const targetPhones = samplePhones(row.total, index + 1);
  return {
    id: `cmp-${String(rows.length - index).padStart(3, '0')}`,
    name: row.name,
    title: row.title ?? (row.name.startsWith('Family') ? 'Family meal tonight? 🍗' : 'Fresh offers waiting for you'),
    message: row.message ?? (row.name.startsWith('Family') ? FAMILY_MESSAGE : MISS_YOU_MESSAGE),
    status: 'COMPLETED',
    media: 'PUSH',
    appId: 'app-001',
    segment: row.name.startsWith('Retention') ? 'DORMANT' : undefined,
    audience: 'ALL',
    totalUsers: row.total,
    matchingDevices: index === 0 ? 770 : Math.round(row.total * 0.55),
    successCount: row.success,
    failCount: row.fail,
    android: { success: androidSuccess, fail: androidFail },
    ios: { success: row.success - androidSuccess, fail: row.fail - androidFail },
    scheduledAt: ist(row.scheduled),
    createdAt: ist(row.created),
    updatedAt: ist(row.created),
    targetPhones,
    failedPhones: targetPhones.slice(0, row.fail),
  };
}

/** Campaigns that have not finished yet. Dates follow today, so the sample always has one of each. */
function upcomingCampaigns(): Campaign[] {
  const now = dayjs().second(0).millisecond(0);
  const base = {
    message: FAMILY_MESSAGE,
    appId: 'app-001',
    audience: 'ALL' as const,
    successCount: 0,
    failCount: 0,
    matchingDevices: 0,
    targetPhones: [],
    failedPhones: [],
  };
  return [
    {
      ...base,
      id: 'cmp-101',
      name: 'Weekend_offer_SMS',
      status: 'SCHEDULED',
      media: 'SMS',
      segment: 'LAPSING',
      totalUsers: 0,
      scheduledAt: now.add(2, 'day').hour(11).minute(0).toISOString(),
      createdAt: now.subtract(1, 'hour').toISOString(),
      updatedAt: now.subtract(1, 'hour').toISOString(),
    },
    {
      ...base,
      id: 'cmp-100',
      name: `Family_nonbuyer_${now.format('D_M_YY')}_set3`,
      title: 'Family meal tonight? 🍗',
      status: 'PROCESSING',
      media: 'PUSH',
      totalUsers: 41,
      matchingDevices: 23,
      successCount: 9,
      failCount: 1,
      scheduledAt: now.subtract(5, 'minute').toISOString(),
      createdAt: now.subtract(2, 'hour').toISOString(),
      updatedAt: now.subtract(5, 'minute').toISOString(),
    },
  ];
}

/** Newest first. */
export const mockCampaigns: Campaign[] = [...upcomingCampaigns(), ...rows.map(toCampaign)];

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/api/query-keys.ts =====
import type { CampaignId } from '@/features/campaigns/types';

/**
 * Names of the cached server data. Used when loading data and when refreshing it after a change,
 * so a typo in one place can't leave a list out of date.
 */
export const campaignQueryKeys = {
  /** The campaign list and every opened campaign. */
  all: ['campaigns'] as const,
  detail: (campaignId: CampaignId) => ['campaigns', campaignId] as const,
};

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/components/CampaignTags.tsx =====
import { BellOutlined, MessageOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { Tag } from 'antd';
import type { CampaignMedia, CampaignStatus } from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import { getMediaMeta, getStatusMeta } from '@/features/campaigns/format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

/** The status is always written out, so it never depends on the color alone. */
export function CampaignStatusTag({ status }: { status: CampaignStatus }) {
  const { label, color } = getStatusMeta(status);
  return (
    <Tag color={color} className="campaign-tag">
      {label}
    </Tag>
  );
}

export function MediaIcon({ media }: { media: CampaignMedia }) {
  return media === 'SMS' ? <MessageOutlined aria-hidden="true" /> : <BellOutlined aria-hidden="true" />;
}

export function CampaignMediaTag({ media }: { media: CampaignMedia }) {
  return (
    <Tag icon={<MediaIcon media={media} />} className="campaign-tag" variant="outlined">
      {media === 'PUSH' ? 'Push' : getMediaMeta(media).label}
    </Tag>
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/campaigns/components/CampaignTags.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/components/CampaignsTable.tsx =====
import { CopyOutlined, EyeOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { Button, Empty, Flex, Grid, Progress, Table, Tooltip, Typography, type TableColumnsType } from 'antd';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { CampaignMediaTag, CampaignStatusTag } from '@/features/campaigns/components/CampaignTags';
import { formatCampaignDate, getSuccessRate } from '@/features/campaigns/format';
import type { CampaignSummary } from '@/features/campaigns/types';
import { colors } from '@/theme';
import { formatCount, formatPercent } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

/** Height of the scrollable table body. Rows outside this area are not rendered (virtual scroll). */
const TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT = 560;

export type CampaignActions = {
  onView: (campaign: CampaignSummary) => void;
  onDuplicate: (campaign: CampaignSummary) => void;
};

type CampaignsTableProps = CampaignActions & {
  campaigns: CampaignSummary[];
  loading?: boolean;
};

function Delivery({ campaign }: { campaign: CampaignSummary }) {
  const rate = getSuccessRate(campaign.successCount, campaign.failCount);
  if (rate === undefined) return <span className="campaigns-muted">Not sent yet</span>;

  return (
    <Tooltip title={`${formatPercent(rate)} of sends worked`}>
      <div className="campaign-delivery">
        <div className="campaign-delivery__numbers">
          <span className="campaign-delivery__ok campaigns-number">✓ {formatCount(campaign.successCount)}</span>
          <span className="campaign-delivery__fail campaigns-number">✕ {formatCount(campaign.failCount)}</span>
        </div>
        <Progress
          percent={100}
          success={{ percent: Math.round(rate * 100), strokeColor: colors.positive }}
          strokeColor={colors.negativeSoft}
          showInfo={false}
          size="small"
          aria-label={`${formatPercent(rate)} delivered`}
        />
      </div>
    </Tooltip>
  );
}

function getColumns({ onView, onDuplicate }: CampaignActions, isMobile: boolean): TableColumnsType<CampaignSummary> {
  return [
    {
      title: 'Campaign',
      key: 'name',
      width: 300,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'left',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name),
      render: (_, campaign) => (
        <div className="campaign-name-cell">
          <Button
            type="link"
            className="campaign-name-cell__name"
            onClick={() => onView(campaign)}
            title={campaign.name}
          >
            {campaign.name}
          </Button>
          {campaign.title && (
            <Typography.Text className="campaign-name-cell__title" ellipsis={{ tooltip: campaign.title }}>
              {campaign.title}
            </Typography.Text>
          )}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Status',
      dataIndex: 'status',
      key: 'status',
      width: 120,
      render: (status: CampaignSummary['status']) => <CampaignStatusTag status={status} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'Media',
      dataIndex: 'media',
      key: 'media',
      width: 100,
      render: (media: CampaignSummary['media']) => <CampaignMediaTag media={media} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'Total users',
      dataIndex: 'totalUsers',
      key: 'totalUsers',
      width: 120,
      align: 'right',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.totalUsers - b.totalUsers,
      render: (total: number) => <span className="campaigns-number">{formatCount(total)}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Delivered / failed',
      key: 'delivery',
      width: 170,
      render: (_, campaign) => <Delivery campaign={campaign} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'Scheduled time',
      dataIndex: 'scheduledAt',
      key: 'scheduledAt',
      width: 190,
      sorter: (a, b) => dayjs(a.scheduledAt).valueOf() - dayjs(b.scheduledAt).valueOf(),
      render: (date: string) => formatCampaignDate(date),
    },
    {
      title: 'Created at',
      dataIndex: 'createdAt',
      key: 'createdAt',
      width: 190,
      sorter: (a, b) => dayjs(a.createdAt).valueOf() - dayjs(b.createdAt).valueOf(),
      defaultSortOrder: 'descend',
      render: (date: string) => <span className="campaigns-muted">{formatCampaignDate(date)}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Actions',
      key: 'actions',
      width: 130,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'right',
      render: (_, campaign) => (
        <Flex gap={4}>
          <Button
            size="small"
            icon={<EyeOutlined />}
            onClick={() => onView(campaign)}
            aria-label={`View ${campaign.name}`}
          >
            View
          </Button>
          <Tooltip title="Duplicate: start a new campaign with the same content">
            <Button
              size="small"
              type="text"
              icon={<CopyOutlined />}
              onClick={() => onDuplicate(campaign)}
              aria-label={`Duplicate ${campaign.name}`}
            />
          </Tooltip>
        </Flex>
      ),
    },
  ];
}

/**
 * The campaigns list. Uses virtual scrolling instead of page buttons: all rows are in one
 * scrollable list, but only the rows on screen are drawn, so it stays fast with many campaigns.
 */
export function CampaignsTable({ campaigns, loading = false, ...actions }: CampaignsTableProps) {
  // On a phone the Name and Actions columns scroll with the table: pinned, they would cover everything else.
  const isMobile = !Grid.useBreakpoint().md;
  const columns = getColumns(actions, isMobile);
  const tableWidth = columns.reduce((sum, column) => sum + Number(column.width ?? 0), 0);

  return (
    <Table<CampaignSummary>
      className="list-table campaigns-table"
      virtual
      rowKey="id"
      columns={columns}
      dataSource={campaigns}
      loading={loading}
      pagination={false}
      showSorterTooltip={false}
      scroll={{ x: tableWidth, y: TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT }}
      locale={{ emptyText: <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description="No campaigns found" /> }}
    />
  );
}

import { Card, Typography } from 'antd';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import type { CampaignMedia } from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

type MessagePreviewProps = {
  media: CampaignMedia;
  appName?: string;
  title?: string;
  message?: string;
  pictureUrl?: string;
  /** When it will be (or was) sent. Shown as the time on the lock screen. */
  sendAt?: string;
};

/**
 * Roughly how the message looks on a phone's lock screen. It is a guide only: each phone
 * shortens long texts and crops pictures in its own way.
 */
export function MessagePreview({ media, appName = 'Abis', title, message, pictureUrl, sendAt }: MessagePreviewProps) {
  const time = sendAt ? dayjs(sendAt) : dayjs();
  const isPush = media === 'PUSH';
  const sender = isPush ? appName : 'Messages';

  return (
    <Card className="message-preview" variant="borderless" size="small">
      <Typography.Text className="message-preview__title">Preview</Typography.Text>
      <div className="message-preview__screen" aria-label="Message preview">
        <span className="message-preview__clock">{time.format('h:mm')}</span>
        <div className="notification">
          <div className="notification__head">
            <span className="notification__app-icon" aria-hidden="true">
              {sender.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
            </span>
            <span>{sender}</span>
            <span className="notification__time">{time.format('D MMM')}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="notification__body">
            {isPush && <span className="notification__title">{title || 'Notification title'}</span>}
            <span className="notification__text">
              {message || <span className="message-preview__empty">Your message appears here</span>}
            </span>
          </div>
          {isPush && pictureUrl && <img className="notification__picture" src={pictureUrl} alt="Campaign picture" />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Card>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/components/PhoneListCard.tsx =====
import { CopyOutlined, DownloadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { App, Button, Card, Empty, Flex, Tag, Typography } from 'antd';
import { downloadTextFile, toCsv } from '@/lib/download';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

type PhoneListCardProps = {
  title: string;
  description: string;
  phones: string[];
  /** File name for the CSV download, without ".csv". */
  fileName: string;
  emptyText: string;
};

/**
 * A long list of phone numbers in a scrollable box, with Copy and Download buttons,
 * so a list of thousands of numbers doesn't stretch the page.
 */
export function PhoneListCard({ title, description, phones, fileName, emptyText }: PhoneListCardProps) {
  const { message } = App.useApp();

  const copy = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(phones.join(', ')).then(
      () => void message.success(`${formatCount(phones.length)} numbers copied.`),
      () => void message.error('Could not copy. Use Download instead.'),
    );
  };

  const download = () => downloadTextFile(`${fileName}.csv`, toCsv([['Phone'], ...phones.map((phone) => [phone])]));

  return (
    <Card
      className="campaign-card"
      variant="borderless"
      title={
        <Flex align="center" gap={8}>
          {title}
          <Tag className="campaign-count">{formatCount(phones.length)}</Tag>
        </Flex>
      }
      extra={
        phones.length > 0 && (
          <Flex gap={8}>
            <Button size="small" icon={<CopyOutlined />} onClick={copy}>
              Copy
            </Button>
            <Button size="small" icon={<DownloadOutlined />} onClick={download}>
              Download CSV
            </Button>
          </Flex>
        )
      }
    >
      <Typography.Paragraph className="campaigns-muted">{description}</Typography.Paragraph>
      {phones.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="phone-list" tabIndex={0} aria-label={title}>
          {phones.join(', ')}
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={emptyText} />
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/constants.ts =====
/**
 * Choices offered by the campaign form. Labels are what admins see; values are what the API stores.
 * TODO: Check the values against the backend before going live.
 */

export const CAMPAIGN_MEDIA = ['SMS', 'PUSH'] as const;
export type CampaignMedia = (typeof CAMPAIGN_MEDIA)[number];

export const CAMPAIGN_MEDIA_META = new Map<CampaignMedia, { label: string; hint: string }>([
  ['SMS', { label: 'SMS', hint: 'A text message to the phone number. No title or picture.' }],
  ['PUSH', { label: 'Push notification', hint: 'A notification in the app, with a title and an optional picture.' }],
]);

export const CAMPAIGN_STATUSES = ['SCHEDULED', 'PROCESSING', 'COMPLETED', 'FAILED'] as const;
export type CampaignStatus = (typeof CAMPAIGN_STATUSES)[number];

export const CAMPAIGN_STATUS_META = new Map<CampaignStatus, { label: string; color: string }>([
  ['SCHEDULED', { label: 'Scheduled', color: 'processing' }],
  ['PROCESSING', { label: 'Sending', color: 'gold' }],
  ['COMPLETED', { label: 'Completed', color: 'success' }],
  ['FAILED', { label: 'Failed', color: 'error' }],
]);

/** Who receives the campaign: everyone (optionally narrowed by segment), or a pasted list of phone numbers. */
export const CAMPAIGN_AUDIENCES = ['ALL', 'SPECIFIC'] as const;
export type CampaignAudience = (typeof CAMPAIGN_AUDIENCES)[number];

/** One SMS holds 160 plain characters, or 70 when it has emoji or symbols such as ₹. Longer texts are split. */
export const SMS_PART_LENGTH = { plain: 160, plainMulti: 153, unicode: 70, unicodeMulti: 67 } as const;
===== END FILE: src/features/campaigns/constants.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/format.ts =====
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import {
  CAMPAIGN_MEDIA_META,
  CAMPAIGN_STATUS_META,
  SMS_PART_LENGTH,
  type CampaignMedia,
  type CampaignStatus,
} from '@/features/campaigns/constants';

export function getStatusMeta(status: CampaignStatus): { label: string; color: string } {
  return CAMPAIGN_STATUS_META.get(status) ?? { label: status, color: 'default' };
}

export function getMediaMeta(media: CampaignMedia): { label: string; hint: string } {
  return CAMPAIGN_MEDIA_META.get(media) ?? { label: media, hint: '' };
}

/** '2026-08-18T01:30:00Z' → '18 Aug 2026, 7:00 AM' */
export function formatCampaignDate(isoDate: string): string {
  return dayjs(isoDate).format('DD MMM YYYY, h:mm A');
}

/** With seconds, for the details page. */
export function formatCampaignDateTime(isoDate: string): string {
  return dayjs(isoDate).format('DD MMM YYYY, h:mm:ss A');
}

/** Share of sends that worked, from 0 to 1. Undefined while nothing was sent. */
export function getSuccessRate(success: number, fail: number): number | undefined {
  const attempts = success + fail;
  return attempts > 0 ? success / attempts : undefined;
}

// Plain SMS characters (GSM-7). Anything else, such as emoji or ₹, makes the SMS "unicode" and shorter.
const PLAIN_SMS = /^[A-Za-z0-9 \r\n@£$¥èéùìòÇØøÅåΔ_ΦΓΛΩΠΨΣΘΞÆæßÉ!"#¤%&'()*+,\-./:;<=>?¡ÄÖÑÜ§¿äöñüà^{}\\[~\]|€]*$/;

/** How many SMS a message is split into, which is what the operator charges for. */
export function countSmsParts(message: string): { parts: number; unicode: boolean } {
  const unicode = !PLAIN_SMS.test(message);
  const length = [...message].length;
  if (length === 0) return { parts: 0, unicode };
  const single = unicode ? SMS_PART_LENGTH.unicode : SMS_PART_LENGTH.plain;
  const multi = unicode ? SMS_PART_LENGTH.unicodeMulti : SMS_PART_LENGTH.plainMulti;
  return { parts: length <= single ? 1 : Math.ceil(length / multi), unicode };
}
===== END FILE: src/features/campaigns/format.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/pages/CampaignDetailsPage.tsx =====
import { AndroidFilled, AppleFilled, CopyOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  Alert,
  Button,
  Card,
  Descriptions,
  Progress,
  Result,
  Skeleton,
  Typography,
  type DescriptionsProps,
} from 'antd';
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PageHeader } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { listApps } from '@/features/apps/api/apps-api';
import { appQueryKeys } from '@/features/apps/api/query-keys';
import { getCampaign } from '@/features/campaigns/api/campaigns-api';
import { campaignQueryKeys } from '@/features/campaigns/api/query-keys';
import { CampaignMediaTag, CampaignStatusTag } from '@/features/campaigns/components/CampaignTags';
import { MessagePreview } from '@/features/campaigns/components/MessagePreview';
import { PhoneListCard } from '@/features/campaigns/components/PhoneListCard';
import { formatCampaignDateTime, getSuccessRate } from '@/features/campaigns/format';
import type { Campaign, PlatformResult } from '@/features/campaigns/types';
import { INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import { colors } from '@/theme';
import { formatCount, formatPercent } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

/** While the campaign is being sent, the page checks again this often (in milliseconds). */
const REFRESH_WHILE_SENDING_MS = 15_000;

const NOT_SET = <span className="campaigns-muted">-</span>;

function Metric({ label, value, tone }: { label: string; value: ReactNode; tone?: 'ok' | 'fail' }) {
  return (
    <Card className="campaign-metric" variant="borderless" size="small">
      <span className="campaign-metric__label">{label}</span>
      <span className={`campaign-metric__value${tone ? ` campaign-metric__value--${tone}` : ''}`}>{value}</span>
    </Card>
  );
}

function PlatformRow({ name, icon, result }: { name: string; icon: ReactNode; result?: PlatformResult }) {
  const rate = result ? getSuccessRate(result.success, result.fail) : undefined;
  return (
    <div className="platform-row">
      <span className="platform-row__name">
        {icon}
        {name}
      </span>
      <Progress
        percent={rate === undefined ? 0 : 100}
        success={{ percent: Math.round((rate ?? 0) * 100), strokeColor: colors.positive }}
        strokeColor={colors.negativeSoft}
        showInfo={false}
        aria-label={rate === undefined ? `${name}: nothing sent` : `${name}: ${formatPercent(rate)} delivered`}
      />
      <span className="platform-row__numbers">
        <span className="campaign-delivery__ok">{formatCount(result?.success ?? 0)} sent</span>
        {' · '}
        <span className="campaign-delivery__fail">{formatCount(result?.fail ?? 0)} failed</span>
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Only https links from our own storage are opened; anything else is shown as text. */
function PictureLink({ url }: { url: string }) {
  const isSafe = url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('blob:');
  return isSafe ? (
    <Typography.Link href={url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" className="campaign-link">
      {url}
    </Typography.Link>
  ) : (
    <span className="campaign-link">{url}</span>
  );
}

function CampaignDetails({ campaign }: { campaign: Campaign }) {
  const appsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: appQueryKeys.all, queryFn: listApps });
  const appName = appsQuery.data?.find((app) => app.id === campaign.appId)?.description;
  const rate = getSuccessRate(campaign.successCount, campaign.failCount);
  const isPush = campaign.media === 'PUSH';
  const isFinished = campaign.status === 'COMPLETED' || campaign.status === 'FAILED';

  const audience =
    campaign.audience === 'SPECIFIC'
      ? 'Specific users (pasted phone numbers)'
      : campaign.segment
        ? `All users in segment: ${INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS.get(campaign.segment)}`
        : 'All users';

  // Texts come from the team's own form, but are still shown as plain text only.
  const items: DescriptionsProps['items'] = [
    { key: 'status', label: 'Status', children: <CampaignStatusTag status={campaign.status} /> },
    { key: 'media', label: 'Media', children: <CampaignMediaTag media={campaign.media} /> },
    { key: 'app', label: 'App', children: appName ?? campaign.appId },
    { key: 'audience', label: 'Audience', children: audience },
    ...(isPush ? [{ key: 'title', label: 'Title', children: campaign.title ?? NOT_SET }] : []),
    { key: 'message', label: 'Message', children: <span className="campaign-message-text">{campaign.message}</span> },
    ...(isPush
      ? [
          {
            key: 'picture',
            label: 'Picture',
            children: campaign.pictureUrl ? <PictureLink url={campaign.pictureUrl} /> : NOT_SET,
          },
        ]
      : []),
    { key: 'scheduled', label: 'Schedule notification time', children: formatCampaignDateTime(campaign.scheduledAt) },
    { key: 'created', label: 'Created at', children: formatCampaignDateTime(campaign.createdAt) },
    { key: 'updated', label: 'Updated at', children: formatCampaignDateTime(campaign.updatedAt) },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="campaigns-page__stack">
      {campaign.status === 'SCHEDULED' && (
        <Alert
          type="info"
          showIcon
          title="This campaign has not been sent yet"
          description={`It will be sent on ${formatCampaignDateTime(campaign.scheduledAt)}. The results appear here after that.`}
        />
      )}
      {campaign.status === 'PROCESSING' && (
        <Alert
          type="warning"
          showIcon
          title="This campaign is being sent"
          description="The numbers below update by themselves until it is finished."
        />
      )}

      <div className="campaign-metrics">
        <Metric label="Matching users found" value={formatCount(campaign.totalUsers)} />
        <Metric label="Matching devices found" value={isPush ? formatCount(campaign.matchingDevices) : NOT_SET} />
        <Metric label="Delivered" value={formatCount(campaign.successCount)} tone="ok" />
        <Metric label="Failed" value={formatCount(campaign.failCount)} tone="fail" />
        <Metric label="Success rate" value={rate === undefined ? NOT_SET : formatPercent(rate)} />
      </div>

      <div className="campaign-details-layout">
        <div className="campaigns-page__stack">
          <Card className="campaign-card" variant="borderless" title="Campaign details">
            <Descriptions bordered column={1} size="small" items={items} styles={{ label: { width: 220 } }} />
          </Card>

          {isPush && (
            <Card className="campaign-card" variant="borderless" title="Delivery by platform">
              <PlatformRow name="Android" icon={<AndroidFilled aria-hidden="true" />} result={campaign.android} />
              <PlatformRow name="iOS" icon={<AppleFilled aria-hidden="true" />} result={campaign.ios} />
            </Card>
          )}

          <PhoneListCard
            title="Target audience"
            description="Every phone number this campaign was sent to."
            phones={campaign.targetPhones}
            fileName={`${campaign.name}-audience`}
            emptyText={isFinished ? 'No users matched' : 'Known once the campaign is sent'}
          />
          <PhoneListCard
            title="Failed user phone numbers"
            description="Numbers the campaign could not reach. You can send these again as a new campaign."
            phones={campaign.failedPhones}
            fileName={`${campaign.name}-failed`}
            emptyText={isFinished ? 'Every message was delivered' : 'Known once the campaign is sent'}
          />
        </div>

        <aside className="campaign-form-aside">
          <MessagePreview
            media={campaign.media}
            appName={appName}
            title={campaign.title}
            message={campaign.message}
            pictureUrl={campaign.pictureUrl}
            sendAt={campaign.scheduledAt}
          />
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The details and results of one campaign (/campaigns/:campaignId). */
export default function CampaignDetailsPage() {
  const { campaignId = '' } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const goBack = () => void navigate('/campaigns');

  const campaignQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: campaignQueryKeys.detail(campaignId),
    queryFn: () => getCampaign(campaignId),
    retry: false,
    refetchInterval: (query) => (query.state.data?.status === 'PROCESSING' ? REFRESH_WHILE_SENDING_MS : false),
  });

  if (campaignQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <Result
        status="404"
        title="Campaign not found"
        subTitle="It may have been removed, or the link is wrong."
        extra={
          <Button type="primary" onClick={goBack}>
            Back to campaigns
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  const campaign = campaignQuery.data;

  return (
    <div className="campaigns-page campaigns-page__stack">
      <PageHeader
        title={campaign?.name ?? 'Campaign details'}
        subtitle="What was sent, to whom, and how many messages arrived."
        backLabel="Back to campaigns"
        onBack={goBack}
        actions={
          campaign && (
            <Button
              icon={<CopyOutlined />}
              onClick={() => void navigate(`/campaigns/new?from=${encodeURIComponent(campaign.id)}`)}
            >
              Duplicate
            </Button>
          )
        }
      />
      {campaign ? <CampaignDetails campaign={campaign} /> : <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 10 }} />}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/pages/CampaignsPage.tsx =====
import { PlusOutlined, SearchOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Button, Input, Select } from 'antd';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ListPanel } from '@/components/ui/ListPanel';
import { LoadError } from '@/components/ui/LoadError';
import { listCampaigns } from '@/features/campaigns/api/campaigns-api';
import { campaignQueryKeys } from '@/features/campaigns/api/query-keys';
import { CampaignsTable } from '@/features/campaigns/components/CampaignsTable';
import {
  CAMPAIGN_MEDIA,
  CAMPAIGN_STATUSES,
  type CampaignMedia,
  type CampaignStatus,
} from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import { getStatusMeta } from '@/features/campaigns/format';
import type { CampaignSummary } from '@/features/campaigns/types';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

/** While a campaign is scheduled or sending, the list checks again this often (in milliseconds). */
const REFRESH_WHILE_SENDING_MS = 15_000;

type StatusFilter = 'all' | CampaignStatus;
type MediaFilter = 'all' | CampaignMedia;

const statusOptions: { value: StatusFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Any status' },
  ...CAMPAIGN_STATUSES.map((status) => ({ value: status, label: getStatusMeta(status).label })),
];

const mediaOptions: { value: MediaFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Any media' },
  ...CAMPAIGN_MEDIA.map((media) => ({ value: media, label: media === 'PUSH' ? 'Push' : media })),
];

function matchesSearch(campaign: CampaignSummary, search: string): boolean {
  if (!search) return true;
  return `${campaign.name} ${campaign.title ?? ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(search);
}

export default function CampaignsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>('all');
  const [mediaFilter, setMediaFilter] = useState<MediaFilter>('all');

  const campaignsQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: campaignQueryKeys.all,
    queryFn: listCampaigns,
    // Keeps the numbers of a campaign that is being sent up to date.
    refetchInterval: (query) =>
      query.state.data?.some((campaign) => campaign.status === 'PROCESSING') ? REFRESH_WHILE_SENDING_MS : false,
  });

  const campaigns = campaignsQuery.data ?? [];
  const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();
  const visibleCampaigns = campaigns.filter(
    (campaign) =>
      matchesSearch(campaign, normalizedSearch) &&
      (statusFilter === 'all' || campaign.status === statusFilter) &&
      (mediaFilter === 'all' || campaign.media === mediaFilter),
  );

  return (
    <div className="campaigns-page">
      <ListPanel
        title="All campaigns"
        count={campaignsQuery.data ? campaigns.length : undefined}
        subtitle="SMS and push notifications sent to your customers."
        toolbar={
          <>
            <Input
              allowClear
              placeholder="Search campaign name or title"
              prefix={<SearchOutlined />}
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              aria-label="Search campaigns"
            />
            <Select<StatusFilter>
              className="campaigns-toolbar__filter"
              value={statusFilter}
              options={statusOptions}
              onChange={setStatusFilter}
              aria-label="Filter by status"
            />
            <Select<MediaFilter>
              className="campaigns-toolbar__filter"
              value={mediaFilter}
              options={mediaOptions}
              onChange={setMediaFilter}
              aria-label="Filter by media"
            />
            <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/campaigns/new')}>
              Create campaign
            </Button>
          </>
        }
        footer={
          campaignsQuery.data &&
          `Showing ${formatCount(visibleCampaigns.length)} of ${formatCount(campaigns.length)} campaigns · Sample data`
        }
      >
        {campaignsQuery.isError ? (
          <LoadError what="campaigns" onRetry={() => void campaignsQuery.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <CampaignsTable
            campaigns={visibleCampaigns}
            loading={campaignsQuery.isPending}
            onView={(campaign) => void navigate(`/campaigns/${encodeURIComponent(campaign.id)}`)}
            onDuplicate={(campaign) => void navigate(`/campaigns/new?from=${encodeURIComponent(campaign.id)}`)}
          />
        )}
      </ListPanel>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/pages/NewCampaignPage.tsx =====
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Col, DatePicker, Form, Input, Radio, Result, Row, Select, Skeleton } from 'antd';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { FormActions, FormSection, PageHeader } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { PictureField } from '@/components/ui/PictureField';
import { getPictureFile, type PictureFieldValue } from '@/components/ui/picture-file';
import { listApps } from '@/features/apps/api/apps-api';
import { appQueryKeys } from '@/features/apps/api/query-keys';
import { createCampaign, getCampaign } from '@/features/campaigns/api/campaigns-api';
import { campaignQueryKeys } from '@/features/campaigns/api/query-keys';
import { MediaIcon } from '@/features/campaigns/components/CampaignTags';
import { MessagePreview } from '@/features/campaigns/components/MessagePreview';
import { CAMPAIGN_MEDIA, type CampaignAudience, type CampaignMedia } from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import { countSmsParts, getMediaMeta } from '@/features/campaigns/format';
import {
  campaignFields,
  campaignSchema,
  type CampaignFormInput,
  type CampaignPayload,
} from '@/features/campaigns/schemas/campaign.schema';
import type { Campaign } from '@/features/campaigns/types';
import { INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS, INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import { showZodErrorsInForm, zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/campaigns/campaigns.css';

const fields = campaignFields.shape;

type CampaignFormValues = CampaignFormInput & { pictureFile?: PictureFieldValue };

const DATE_TIME_FORMAT = 'DD MMM YYYY, h:mm A';

const segmentOptions = INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS.map((value) => ({ value, label: INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS.get(value) }));

const audienceChoices: { value: CampaignAudience; title: string; hint: string }[] = [
  { value: 'ALL', title: 'Send to all users', hint: 'Everyone who uses the app, or one segment of them.' },
  { value: 'SPECIFIC', title: 'Send to specific users', hint: 'Only the phone numbers you paste.' },
];

function toFormValues(source: Campaign | undefined): Partial<CampaignFormValues> {
  if (!source) return { media: 'SMS', audience: 'ALL', appId: 'app-001' };
  return {
    name: `${source.name} (copy)`,
    title: source.title,
    message: source.message,
    media: source.media,
    appId: source.appId,
    audience: source.audience,
    segment: source.segment,
    phoneNumbers: source.audience === 'SPECIFIC' ? source.targetPhones.join(', ') : undefined,
  };
}

/** Shows a just-chosen picture in the preview. The file is read in the browser only; nothing is uploaded. */
function useLocalPictureUrl(file: File | undefined): string | undefined {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<{ file: File; url: string }>();
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
      if (typeof reader.result === 'string') setPreview({ file, url: reader.result });
    };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    return () => reader.abort();
  }, [file]);
  // An old preview is never shown for a different (or removed) file.
  return preview && preview.file === file ? preview.url : undefined;
}

type NewCampaignFormProps = {
  /** The campaign being duplicated, if any. Its content fills the form. */
  source?: Campaign;
};

function NewCampaignForm({ source }: NewCampaignFormProps) {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<CampaignFormValues>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message, modal } = App.useApp();

  const appsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: appQueryKeys.all, queryFn: listApps });
  const appOptions = (appsQuery.data ?? []).map((app) => ({ value: app.id, label: app.description }));

  // Live values for the preview and for the fields that depend on other fields.
  const media: CampaignMedia = Form.useWatch('media', form) ?? 'SMS';
  const audience: CampaignAudience = Form.useWatch('audience', form) ?? 'ALL';
  const title: unknown = Form.useWatch('title', form);
  const text: unknown = Form.useWatch('message', form);
  const appId: unknown = Form.useWatch('appId', form);
  const scheduledAt: unknown = Form.useWatch('scheduledAt', form);
  const phoneText: unknown = Form.useWatch('phoneNumbers', form);
  const pictureFile: PictureFieldValue = Form.useWatch('pictureFile', form);
  const localPictureUrl = useLocalPictureUrl(getPictureFile(pictureFile));

  const isPush = media === 'PUSH';
  const messageText = typeof text === 'string' ? text : '';
  const sms = countSmsParts(messageText);
  const pastedCount = typeof phoneText === 'string' ? new Set(phoneText.split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean)).size : 0;
  const isScheduled = dayjs.isDayjs(scheduledAt) && scheduledAt.isValid();
  const appName = appOptions.find((option) => option.value === appId)?.label;

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: createCampaign,
    onSuccess: async (campaign) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: campaignQueryKeys.all });
      void message.success(
        campaign.status === 'SCHEDULED' ? `"${campaign.name}" was scheduled.` : `"${campaign.name}" is being sent.`,
      );
      void navigate(`/campaigns/${encodeURIComponent(campaign.id)}`);
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The campaign could not be created. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  const send = (payload: CampaignPayload, picture: File | undefined) => {
    mutation.mutate({ payload, picture, pictureUrl: picture ? undefined : source?.pictureUrl });
  };

  const handleFinish = (values: CampaignFormValues) => {
    const result = campaignSchema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) {
      showZodErrorsInForm(form, result.error);
      return;
    }
    const payload = result.data;
    const picture = payload.media === 'PUSH' ? getPictureFile(values.pictureFile) : undefined;
    const who =
      payload.audience === 'SPECIFIC'
        ? `${formatCount(payload.phoneNumbers.length)} phone numbers`
        : payload.segment
          ? `all ${INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS.get(payload.segment)?.toLowerCase()} users`
          : 'all users';

    // Messages can't be taken back once sent, so ask once more.
    modal.confirm({
      title: payload.scheduledAt ? 'Schedule this campaign?' : 'Send this campaign now?',
      content: payload.scheduledAt
        ? `It will be sent to ${who} on ${dayjs(payload.scheduledAt).format(DATE_TIME_FORMAT)}.`
        : `It will be sent to ${who} right away. Messages can't be taken back once sent.`,
      okText: payload.scheduledAt ? 'Schedule' : 'Send now',
      cancelText: 'Go back',
      onOk: () => send(payload, picture),
    });
  };

  const goBack = () => void navigate('/campaigns');

  return (
    <div className="campaigns-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={source ? 'Duplicate campaign' : 'New campaign'}
        subtitle={
          source
            ? `A copy of "${source.name}". Change what you need, then send or schedule it.`
            : 'Write an SMS or push notification and choose who receives it.'
        }
        backLabel="Back to campaigns"
        onBack={goBack}
      />

      <Form<CampaignFormValues>
        form={form}
        layout="vertical"
        initialValues={toFormValues(source)}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
        disabled={mutation.isPending}
        autoComplete="off"
      >
        <div className="campaign-form-layout">
          <div className="campaigns-page__stack">
            <FormSection title="Message" description="How it is sent and what it says.">
              <Form.Item label="Media" name="media" required rules={[zodRule(fields.media)]}>
                <Radio.Group className="choice-cards">
                  {CAMPAIGN_MEDIA.map((value) => (
                    <Radio key={value} value={value}>
                      <span className="choice-card__title">
                        <MediaIcon media={value} />
                        {getMediaMeta(value).label}
                      </span>
                      <span className="choice-card__hint">{getMediaMeta(value).hint}</span>
                    </Radio>
                  ))}
                </Radio.Group>
              </Form.Item>
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={isPush ? 12 : 24}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Campaign name"
                    name="name"
                    required
                    extra="For your team only. Customers don't see it."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.name)]}
                  >
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Family_nonbuyer_13_8_26_set3" maxLength={100} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                {isPush && (
                  <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                    <Form.Item
                      label="Title"
                      name="title"
                      required
                      extra="The bold first line of the notification."
                      rules={[zodRule(fields.title)]}
                    >
                      <Input placeholder="e.g. ❤️ We Miss You! Get 25% OFF" maxLength={100} showCount />
                    </Form.Item>
                  </Col>
                )}
                <Col span={24}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Message"
                    name="message"
                    required
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.message)]}
                    extra={
                      !isPush &&
                      messageText && (
                        <span className="campaign-sms-count">
                          {sms.parts} SMS {sms.parts === 1 ? 'part' : 'parts'} per person
                          {sms.unicode ? ' · Has emoji or symbols such as ₹, so each part holds 70 characters' : ''}
                        </span>
                      )
                    }
                  >
                    <Input.TextArea
                      placeholder="e.g. Use MISSYOU25 on orders above ₹499 & enjoy fresh, 100% Traceable Chicken. Order Today!"
                      autoSize={{ minRows: 3, maxRows: 8 }}
                      maxLength={1000}
                      showCount
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                {isPush && (
                  <Col span={24}>
                    <PictureField currentUrl={source?.pictureUrl} label="Picture (optional)" />
                  </Col>
                )}
              </Row>
            </FormSection>

            <FormSection title="Audience" description="Which app it is sent from and who receives it.">
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="App" name="appId" required rules={[zodRule(fields.appId)]}>
                    <Select
                      placeholder="Choose the app"
                      options={appOptions}
                      loading={appsQuery.isPending}
                      notFoundContent={appsQuery.isError ? 'Apps could not be loaded' : undefined}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col span={24}>
                  <Form.Item label="Send to" name="audience" required rules={[zodRule(fields.audience)]}>
                    <Radio.Group className="choice-cards">
                      {audienceChoices.map((choice) => (
                        <Radio key={choice.value} value={choice.value}>
                          <span className="choice-card__title">{choice.title}</span>
                          <span className="choice-card__hint">{choice.hint}</span>
                        </Radio>
                      ))}
                    </Radio.Group>
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                {audience === 'ALL' ? (
                  <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                    <Form.Item
                      label="Segment"
                      name="segment"
                      extra="Days since the customer's last order. Leave empty to send to everyone."
                      rules={[zodRule(fields.segment)]}
                    >
                      <Select allowClear placeholder="All users" options={segmentOptions} />
                    </Form.Item>
                  </Col>
                ) : (
                  <Col span={24}>
                    <Form.Item
                      label="Phone numbers"
                      name="phoneNumbers"
                      required
                      extra={`10-digit numbers separated by commas, spaces or new lines. ${
                        pastedCount > 0 ? `${formatCount(pastedCount)} different numbers.` : ''
                      }`}
                    >
                      <Input.TextArea
                        placeholder="e.g. 9876543210, 9123456780"
                        autoSize={{ minRows: 4, maxRows: 10 }}
                        spellCheck={false}
                      />
                    </Form.Item>
                  </Col>
                )}
              </Row>
            </FormSection>

            <FormSection title="Schedule" description="Send it right away, or pick a time.">
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Schedule notification time"
                    name="scheduledAt"
                    extra="Leave empty to send right away."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.scheduledAt)]}
                  >
                    <DatePicker
                      showTime={{ format: 'h:mm A', minuteStep: 5 }}
                      format={DATE_TIME_FORMAT}
                      placeholder="Send now"
                      disabledDate={(date) => date.isBefore(dayjs(), 'day')}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </FormSection>
          </div>

          <aside className="campaign-form-aside">
            <MessagePreview
              media={media}
              appName={appName}
              title={typeof title === 'string' ? title : undefined}
              message={messageText}
              pictureUrl={localPictureUrl ?? source?.pictureUrl}
              sendAt={isScheduled ? scheduledAt.toISOString() : undefined}
            />
          </aside>
        </div>

        <div className="campaign-form-actions">
          <FormActions>
            <Button onClick={goBack} disabled={false}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending}>
              {isScheduled ? 'Schedule campaign' : 'Start campaign'}
            </Button>
          </FormActions>
        </div>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}

function DuplicateCampaignView({ campaignId }: { campaignId: string }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const sourceQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: campaignQueryKeys.detail(campaignId),
    queryFn: () => getCampaign(campaignId),
    retry: false,
  });

  if (sourceQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <Result
        status="404"
        title="Campaign to copy not found"
        subTitle="It may have been removed, or the link is wrong. You can still start a new campaign."
        extra={
          <Button type="primary" onClick={() => void navigate('/campaigns/new', { replace: true })}>
            New campaign
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (sourceQuery.isPending) return <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 10 }} />;
  return <NewCampaignForm source={sourceQuery.data} />;
}

/** New campaign (/campaigns/new), or a copy of one (/campaigns/new?from=:campaignId). */
export default function NewCampaignPage() {
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const fromId = searchParams.get('from');
  // The key starts a fresh form when going from a copy to a blank form.
  return fromId ? <DuplicateCampaignView key={fromId} campaignId={fromId} /> : <NewCampaignForm key="blank" />;
}

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/schemas/campaign.schema.ts =====
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { CAMPAIGN_AUDIENCES, CAMPAIGN_MEDIA } from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import { INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import { optionalDateTime, optionalText, requiredText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

/**
 * New campaign form rules. The form shows these messages field by field (see `zodRule`),
 * and `campaignSchema` turns the form values into the clean payload sent to the API.
 */

const PHONE_PATTERN = /^\d{10}$/;
const MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS = 50_000;

/** 'a, b\nc' → ['a', 'b', 'c']. Commas, spaces, semicolons and new lines all separate numbers. Each number once. */
function splitPhoneNumbers(text: string): string[] {
  return [...new Set(text.split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean))];
}

export const campaignFields = z.object({
  name: requiredText('Enter the campaign name', 100),
  title: optionalText(100),
  message: requiredText('Enter the message', 1000),
  media: z.enum(CAMPAIGN_MEDIA, { error: 'Choose how to send it' }),
  scheduledAt: optionalDateTime('Pick a valid date and time'),
  appId: requiredText('Choose the app', 64),
  segment: z
    .enum(INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS)
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? undefined),
  audience: z.enum(CAMPAIGN_AUDIENCES, { error: 'Choose who receives it' }),
  phoneNumbers: z
    .string()
    .nullish()
    .transform((text) => splitPhoneNumbers(text ?? '')),
});

type CampaignFieldValues = z.output<typeof campaignFields>;

function checkCampaign(values: CampaignFieldValues, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) {
  if (values.media === 'PUSH' && !values.title) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['title'], message: 'A push notification needs a title' });
  }
  if (values.scheduledAt && !dayjs(values.scheduledAt).isAfter(dayjs())) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['scheduledAt'], message: 'Pick a time in the future, or leave it empty' });
  }
  if (values.audience !== 'SPECIFIC') return;

  const invalid = values.phoneNumbers.filter((phone) => !PHONE_PATTERN.test(phone));
  let message: string | undefined;
  if (values.phoneNumbers.length === 0) {
    message = 'Enter at least one phone number';
  } else if (invalid.length > 0) {
    message = `These are not 10-digit phone numbers: ${invalid.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}${invalid.length > 5 ? ' …' : ''}`;
  } else if (values.phoneNumbers.length > MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS) {
    message = `Use ${MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS.toLocaleString('en-IN')} phone numbers or fewer`;
  }
  if (message) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['phoneNumbers'], message });
}

/** Only what matters for the chosen media and audience is sent. */
function toPayload(values: CampaignFieldValues) {
  const isPush = values.media === 'PUSH';
  const isSpecific = values.audience === 'SPECIFIC';
  return {
    name: values.name,
    title: isPush ? values.title : undefined,
    message: values.message,
    media: values.media,
    /** Missing means "send now". */
    scheduledAt: values.scheduledAt,
    appId: values.appId,
    audience: values.audience,
    segment: isSpecific ? undefined : values.segment,
    phoneNumbers: isSpecific ? values.phoneNumbers : [],
  };
}

export const campaignSchema = campaignFields.superRefine(checkCampaign).transform(toPayload);

/** What the form holds while the user is typing. */
export type CampaignFormInput = z.input<typeof campaignFields>;

/** The clean, validated payload for the API. The picture file is sent next to it. */
export type CampaignPayload = z.output<typeof campaignSchema>;
===== END FILE: src/features/campaigns/schemas/campaign.schema.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/campaigns/types.ts =====
import type { CampaignAudience, CampaignMedia, CampaignStatus } from '@/features/campaigns/constants';
import type { InactivitySegment } from '@/features/coupons/constants';

export type CampaignId = string;

/** A campaign in the list. Dates are ISO text such as "2026-08-18T07:00:00+05:30". */
export type CampaignSummary = {
  id: CampaignId;
  name: string;
  title?: string;
  status: CampaignStatus;
  media: CampaignMedia;
  /** Users who matched the audience. */
  totalUsers: number;
  successCount: number;
  failCount: number;
  scheduledAt: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

/** Delivery results per platform. Only push campaigns have them. */
export type PlatformResult = { success: number; fail: number };

/** A campaign with everything the details page shows. */
export type Campaign = CampaignSummary & {
  message: string;
  appId: string;
  segment?: InactivitySegment;
  audience: CampaignAudience;
  pictureUrl?: string;
  updatedAt: string;
  matchingDevices: number;
  android?: PlatformResult;
  ios?: PlatformResult;
  /** Phone numbers the campaign was sent to. */
  targetPhones: string[];
  /** Phone numbers it could not reach. */
  failedPhones: string[];
};
===== END FILE: src/features/campaigns/types.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/api/coupon-tasks-api.ts =====
import { fakeNetworkDelay, mockCoupons, mockCouponTasks } from '@/features/coupons/api/mock-coupons';
import type { TaskFile } from '@/features/users/api/tasks-api';
import type { TaskPayload } from '@/features/users/schemas/task.schema';
import type { UserTask } from '@/features/users/types';
import { toCsv } from '@/lib/download';

/**
 * Bulk import/export of coupons. Works like the users' tasks (same task shape, same CSV checks),
 * only the addresses differ.
 *
 * SAMPLE: works on the sample data in `mock-coupons.ts`. Each function has a TODO showing the real call.
 */

export type CouponTask = UserTask;

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<CouponTask[]>('/coupons/tasks');
 *       return data;
 */
export async function listCouponTasks(): Promise<CouponTask[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return structuredClone(mockCouponTasks);
}

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<CouponTask>(`/coupons/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(taskId)}`);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function getCouponTask(taskId: string): Promise<CouponTask> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const task = mockCouponTasks.find((entry) => entry.id === taskId);
  if (!task) throw new Error('Task not found');
  return structuredClone(task);
}

/**
 * Starts an import or an export. The CSV file (imports only) goes up as multipart form data.
 * The server must check the file's real content and size again, and every row it imports.
 *
 * TODO: const body = new FormData();
 *       body.append('type', payload.type);
 *       body.append('notificationEmail', payload.notificationEmail);
 *       if (payload.file) body.append('file', payload.file);
 *       const { data } = await apiClient.post<CouponTask>('/coupons/tasks', body);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function createCouponTask({ payload }: { payload: TaskPayload }): Promise<CouponTask> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const task: CouponTask = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    type: payload.type,
    state: 'PENDING',
    notificationEmail: payload.notificationEmail,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    hasFile: payload.type === 'import',
  };
  mockCouponTasks.unshift(task);
  return structuredClone(task);
}

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<string>(`/coupons/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(taskId)}/file`, {
 *         responseType: 'text',
 *       });
 *       return { fileName: `coupons-${taskId}.csv`, content: data };
 */
export async function downloadCouponTaskFile(taskId: string): Promise<TaskFile> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const task = mockCouponTasks.find((entry) => entry.id === taskId);
  if (!task?.hasFile) throw new Error('This task has no file');

  const rows = [
    ['Code', 'Type', 'Value', 'Value type', 'Start', 'End'],
    ...mockCoupons.map((coupon) => [
      coupon.code,
      coupon.type,
      coupon.value,
      coupon.valueType,
      coupon.startTime,
      coupon.endTime,
    ]),
  ];
  return { fileName: `coupons-${task.type}-${task.id}.csv`, content: toCsv(rows) };
}

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/api/coupons-api.ts =====
import { fakeNetworkDelay, mockCoupons } from '@/features/coupons/api/mock-coupons';
import type { CouponPayload } from '@/features/coupons/schemas/coupon.schema';
import type { Coupon, CouponId } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import { mockOutlets } from '@/features/outlets/api/mock-outlets';

export type OutletOption = { id: string; name: string };

/**
 * Outlets a coupon can be limited to.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<OutletOption[]>('/outlets', { params: { fields: 'id,name' } });
 *       return data;
 */
export async function listCouponOutletOptions(): Promise<OutletOption[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return mockOutlets.map((outlet) => ({ id: outlet.id, name: outlet.name }));
}

/**
 * SAMPLE: these functions work on the sample data in `mock-coupons.ts` so the whole flow
 * can be tried. Each one has a TODO showing the real call. When the API is ready, only the
 * `api` files change: the pages keep working as they are.
 */

/**
 * Every coupon of the merchant.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<Coupon[]>('/coupons');
 *       return data;
 * If there are many thousands of coupons, search and page on the server instead.
 */
export async function listCoupons(): Promise<Coupon[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return structuredClone(mockCoupons);
}

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<Coupon>(`/coupons/${encodeURIComponent(couponId)}`);
 *       return data;
 * (encodeURIComponent keeps a strange id in the address bar from changing the request path.)
 */
export async function getCoupon(couponId: CouponId): Promise<Coupon> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const coupon = mockCoupons.find((item) => item.id === couponId);
  if (!coupon) throw new Error('Coupon not found');
  return structuredClone(coupon);
}

export type SaveCouponInput = {
  /** Missing when creating a new coupon. */
  couponId?: CouponId;
  payload: CouponPayload;
};

/**
 * Creates a coupon, or updates it when `couponId` is given.
 * The server must refuse a code that another coupon already has, and check every rule again.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = couponId
 *         ? await apiClient.put<Coupon>(`/coupons/${encodeURIComponent(couponId)}`, payload)
 *         : await apiClient.post<Coupon>('/coupons', payload);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function saveCoupon({ couponId, payload }: SaveCouponInput): Promise<Coupon> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const index = couponId ? mockCoupons.findIndex((item) => item.id === couponId) : -1;
  const existing = index >= 0 ? mockCoupons.at(index) : undefined;
  if (couponId && !existing) throw new Error('Coupon not found');

  if (mockCoupons.some((item) => item.code === payload.code && item.id !== existing?.id)) {
    throw new Error('Another coupon already has this code');
  }

  const saved: Coupon = {
    ...payload,
    id: existing?.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
    redemptions: existing?.redemptions ?? 0,
  };
  if (existing) {
    mockCoupons.splice(index, 1, saved);
  } else {
    mockCoupons.unshift(saved);
  }
  return structuredClone(saved);
}

/**
 * TODO: await apiClient.delete(`/coupons/${encodeURIComponent(couponId)}`);
 */
export async function deleteCoupon(couponId: CouponId): Promise<void> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const index = mockCoupons.findIndex((item) => item.id === couponId);
  if (index >= 0) mockCoupons.splice(index, 1);
}
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/api/coupons-api.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/api/mock-coupons.ts =====
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import type { CouponType, CouponValueType } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import type { Coupon } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import type { UserTask } from '@/features/users/types';

/**
 * SAMPLE DATA: used until the coupons API is connected.
 * Replace the functions in `coupons-api.ts` and `coupon-tasks-api.ts` with real calls, then delete this file.
 */

const FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS = 400;

/** Waits a little, like a real request would. */
export function fakeNetworkDelay(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS));
}

/** India time, like the old console. */
function ist(text: string): string {
  return dayjs(`${text}+05:30`).toISOString();
}

  start: string;
  end: string;
  minBill?: number;
  redemptions: number;
  sequence: number;
  isActive?: boolean;
  redemptionLimit?: number;
};

function toCoupon(row: SampleRow, index: number): Coupon {
  const isTargeted = row.type === 'ITEM_DISCOUNT' || row.type === 'CATEGORY_DISCOUNT' || row.type === 'FREE_ITEM';
  return {
    id: `cpn-${String(index + 1).padStart(3, '0')}`,
    name: `${row.code} offer`,
    label: row.valueType === 'FIXED' ? `₹${row.value} OFF` : `${row.value}% OFF`,
    instruction: row.minBill ? `Valid on orders above ₹${row.minBill}.` : undefined,
    code: row.code,
    sequence: row.sequence,
    type: row.type,
    valueType: row.valueType ?? 'PERCENT',
    value: row.value,
    maxDiscount: row.maxDiscount,
    minBillAmount: row.minBill,
    target: 'ORDER',
    targetIds: isTargeted ? ['cat-biryani', 'cat-kebabs'] : [],
    useReferenceId: false,
    applyOnMrp: false,
    isActive: row.isActive ?? true,
    startTime: ist(row.start),
    endTime: ist(row.end),
    redemptionLimit: row.redemptionLimit,
    redemptions: row.redemptions,
    perCustomerLimit: row.type === 'FIRST_TRANSACTION' ? 1 : undefined,
    isPrivate: false,
    allowedPhoneNumbers: [],
    inactivitySegments: row.code.startsWith('MISSYOU') ? ['LAPSING', 'DORMANT', 'CHURNED'] : [],
    platforms: [],
    daysOfWeek: [],
    deliveryDates: [],
    deliveryDayOffsets: [],
    slotRanges: [],
    outletIds: [],
  };
}

export const mockCoupons: Coupon[] = rows.map(toCoupon);

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/api/query-keys.ts =====
import type { CouponId } from '@/features/coupons/types';

/**
 * Names of the cached server data. Used when loading data and when refreshing it after a change,
 * so a typo in one place can't leave a list out of date.
 */
export const couponQueryKeys = {
  /** The coupon list and every opened coupon. */
  all: ['coupons'] as const,
  detail: (couponId: CouponId) => ['coupons', couponId] as const,
  outletOptions: ['coupons', 'outlet-options'] as const,
  /** The task list and every task's details. */
  tasks: ['coupon-tasks'] as const,
  task: (taskId: string) => ['coupon-tasks', taskId] as const,
};
