===== FILE: src/features/coupons/components/CouponsTable.tsx =====
import { Button, Empty, Grid, Table, Tag, Typography, type TableColumnsType } from 'antd';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { RowActions } from '@/components/ui/RowActions';
import {
  formatCouponDate,
  formatCouponValue,
  formatRedemptionLimit,
  getCouponStatus,
  getCouponStatusMeta,
  getCouponTypeLabel,
  getValueTypeLabel,
} from '@/features/coupons/format';
import type { Coupon } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import { formatCount, formatCurrency } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/coupons/coupons.css';

/** Height of the scrollable table body. Rows outside this area are not rendered (virtual scroll). */
const TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT = 560;

export type CouponActions = {
  onEdit: (coupon: Coupon) => void;
  onDelete: (coupon: Coupon) => void;
};

type CouponsTableProps = CouponActions & {
  coupons: Coupon[];
  loading?: boolean;
};

const DASH = <span className="coupons-muted">-</span>;

function getColumns({ onEdit, onDelete }: CouponActions, isMobile: boolean): TableColumnsType<Coupon> {
  return [
    {
      title: 'Code',
      key: 'code',
      width: 200,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'left',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.code.localeCompare(b.code),
      render: (_, coupon) => (
        <div className="coupon-code-cell">
          <Button className="coupon-code" onClick={() => onEdit(coupon)} title={`Edit ${coupon.code}`}>
            {coupon.code}
          </Button>
          <Typography.Text className="coupon-code-cell__name" ellipsis={{ tooltip: coupon.name }}>
            {coupon.name}
          </Typography.Text>
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Status',
      key: 'status',
      width: 110,
      render: (_, coupon) => {
        const { label, color } = getCouponStatusMeta(getCouponStatus(coupon));
        return (
          <Tag color={color} className="coupon-tag">
            {label}
          </Tag>
        );
      },
    },
    {
      title: 'Type',
      dataIndex: 'type',
      key: 'type',
      width: 170,
      render: (type: Coupon['type']) => getCouponTypeLabel(type),
    },
    {
      title: 'Value',
      key: 'value',
      width: 150,
      sorter: (a, b) => a.value - b.value,
      render: (_, coupon) => (
        <div className="coupon-value">
          <span className="coupon-value__amount" title={getValueTypeLabel(coupon.valueType)}>
            {formatCouponValue(coupon)}
          </span>
          {coupon.valueType === 'PERCENT' && coupon.maxDiscount ? (
            <span className="coupon-value__cap">up to {formatCurrency(coupon.maxDiscount)}</span>
          ) : (
            <span className="coupon-value__cap">{getValueTypeLabel(coupon.valueType)}</span>
          )}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Validity',
      key: 'validity',
      width: 240,
      sorter: (a, b) => dayjs(a.endTime).valueOf() - dayjs(b.endTime).valueOf(),
      render: (_, coupon) => (
        <div className="coupon-validity">
          <span>
            <span className="coupon-validity__label">From</span>
            {formatCouponDate(coupon.startTime)}
          </span>
          <span>
            <span className="coupon-validity__label">To</span>
            {formatCouponDate(coupon.endTime)}
          </span>
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Min bill',
      dataIndex: 'minBillAmount',
      key: 'minBillAmount',
      width: 110,
      align: 'right',
      render: (amount: number | undefined) =>
        amount === undefined ? DASH : <span className="coupons-number">{formatCurrency(amount)}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Redemptions',
      key: 'redemptions',
      width: 150,
      align: 'right',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.redemptions - b.redemptions,
      render: (_, coupon) => (
        <span className="coupons-number">
          {formatCount(coupon.redemptions)}
          <span className="coupons-muted"> / {formatRedemptionLimit(coupon.redemptionLimit)}</span>
        </span>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Sequence',
      dataIndex: 'sequence',
      key: 'sequence',
      width: 110,
      align: 'right',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.sequence - b.sequence,
      defaultSortOrder: 'descend',
      render: (sequence: number) => <span className="coupons-number">{sequence}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Actions',
      key: 'actions',
      width: 100,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'right',
      render: (_, coupon) => (
        <RowActions name={coupon.code} what="coupon" onEdit={() => onEdit(coupon)} onDelete={() => onDelete(coupon)} />
      ),
    },
  ];
}

/**
 * The coupons list. Uses virtual scrolling instead of page buttons: all rows are in one
 * scrollable list, but only the rows on screen are drawn, so it stays fast with many coupons.
 */
export function CouponsTable({ coupons, loading = false, ...actions }: CouponsTableProps) {
  // On a phone the Code and Actions columns scroll with the table: pinned, they would cover everything else.
  const isMobile = !Grid.useBreakpoint().md;
  const columns = getColumns(actions, isMobile);
  const tableWidth = columns.reduce((sum, column) => sum + Number(column.width ?? 0), 0);

  return (
    <Table<Coupon>
      className="list-table coupons-table"
      virtual
      rowKey="id"
      columns={columns}
      dataSource={coupons}
      loading={loading}
      pagination={false}
      showSorterTooltip={false}
      scroll={{ x: tableWidth, y: TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT }}
      locale={{ emptyText: <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description="No coupons found" /> }}
    />
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/components/CouponsTable.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/constants.ts =====
/**
 * Choices offered by the coupon form. Labels are what admins see; values are what the API stores.
 * TODO: Check the values against the backend before going live.
 */

export const COUPON_TYPES = [
  'SIMPLE',
  'FIRST_TRANSACTION',
  'MINIMUM_BILL_AMOUNT',
  'ONLINE_PAYMENT',
  'ITEM_DISCOUNT',
  'CATEGORY_DISCOUNT',
  'FREE_ITEM',
  'PRICE_RULE',
  'REFERRAL',
] as const;
export type CouponType = (typeof COUPON_TYPES)[number];

export const COUPON_TYPE_LABELS = new Map<CouponType, string>([
  ['SIMPLE', 'Simple'],
  ['FIRST_TRANSACTION', 'First Transaction'],
  ['MINIMUM_BILL_AMOUNT', 'Minimum Bill Amount'],
  ['ONLINE_PAYMENT', 'Online Payment'],
  ['ITEM_DISCOUNT', 'Item Discount'],
  ['CATEGORY_DISCOUNT', 'Category Discount'],
  ['FREE_ITEM', 'Free Item'],
  ['PRICE_RULE', 'Price Rule'],
  ['REFERRAL', 'Referral'],
]);

/** Coupon types that apply to chosen items or categories, so the form asks which ones. */
export const TARGETED_COUPON_TYPES = new Map<CouponType, { label: string; hint: string }>([
  ['ITEM_DISCOUNT', { label: 'Item IDs', hint: 'The items this discount applies to.' }],
  ['CATEGORY_DISCOUNT', { label: 'Category IDs', hint: 'The categories this discount applies to.' }],
  ['FREE_ITEM', { label: 'Free item IDs', hint: 'The items given free with this coupon.' }],
]);

export const COUPON_VALUE_TYPES = ['PERCENT', 'FIXED'] as const;
export type CouponValueType = (typeof COUPON_VALUE_TYPES)[number];

export const COUPON_VALUE_TYPE_LABELS = new Map<CouponValueType, string>([
  ['PERCENT', 'Percent Discount'],
  ['FIXED', 'Fixed Discount'],
]);

export const COUPON_TARGETS = ['ORDER'] as const;
export type CouponTarget = (typeof COUPON_TARGETS)[number];

export const COUPON_TARGET_LABELS = new Map<CouponTarget, string>([['ORDER', 'Order']]);

/** How long ago a customer last ordered. Users who never ordered are always eligible. */
export const INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS = ['ACTIVE', 'WARM', 'LAPSING', 'DORMANT', 'CHURNED'] as const;
export type InactivitySegment = (typeof INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS)[number];

export const INACTIVITY_SEGMENT_LABELS = new Map<InactivitySegment, string>([
  ['ACTIVE', 'Active (0-30 days)'],
  ['WARM', 'Warm (31-60 days)'],
  ['LAPSING', 'Lapsing (61-90 days)'],
  ['DORMANT', 'Dormant (91-180 days)'],
  ['CHURNED', 'Churned (181+ days)'],
]);

export const WEEK_DAYS = ['MON', 'TUE', 'WED', 'THU', 'FRI', 'SAT', 'SUN'] as const;
export type WeekDay = (typeof WEEK_DAYS)[number];

export const WEEK_DAY_LABELS = new Map<WeekDay, string>([
  ['MON', 'Monday'],
  ['TUE', 'Tuesday'],
  ['WED', 'Wednesday'],
  ['THU', 'Thursday'],
  ['FRI', 'Friday'],
  ['SAT', 'Saturday'],
  ['SUN', 'Sunday'],
]);

export const PLATFORMS = ['WEB', 'IOS', 'ANDROID'] as const;
export type Platform = (typeof PLATFORMS)[number];

export const PLATFORM_LABELS = new Map<Platform, string>([
  ['WEB', 'Web'],
  ['IOS', 'iOS'],
  ['ANDROID', 'Android'],
]);

/** Delivery day counted from the order day: 0 is today, 1 is tomorrow, up to 7 days ahead. */
export const DELIVERY_DAY_OFFSETS = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7] as const;

export function getDeliveryDayLabel(offset: number): string {
  if (offset === 0) return 'Today';
  if (offset === 1) return 'Tomorrow';
  return `T + ${offset}`;
}

/** How many checkout delivery slot ranges a coupon can have. */
export const MAX_SLOT_RANGES = 2;

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/format.ts =====
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { COUPON_TYPE_LABELS, COUPON_VALUE_TYPE_LABELS, type CouponType } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import type { Coupon } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import { formatCount, formatCurrency } from '@/utils/number-format';

export function getCouponTypeLabel(type: CouponType): string {
  return COUPON_TYPE_LABELS.get(type) ?? type;
}

export function getValueTypeLabel(valueType: Coupon['valueType']): string {
  return COUPON_VALUE_TYPE_LABELS.get(valueType) ?? valueType;
}

/** 20 + PERCENT → '20%', 50 + FIXED → '₹50' */
export function formatCouponValue(coupon: Pick<Coupon, 'value' | 'valueType'>): string {
  return coupon.valueType === 'PERCENT' ? `${coupon.value}%` : formatCurrency(coupon.value);
}

/** '2026-11-30T18:29:00Z' → '30 Nov 2026, 11:59 PM' */
export function formatCouponDate(isoDate: string): string {
  return dayjs(isoDate).format('DD MMM YYYY, h:mm A');
}

export function formatRedemptionLimit(limit: number | undefined): string {
  return limit === undefined ? 'Unlimited' : formatCount(limit);
}

/** Referral coupons are made for one customer each, so the list hides them unless asked. */
export function isReferralCoupon(coupon: Coupon): boolean {
  return coupon.type === 'REFERRAL';
}

export const COUPON_STATUSES = ['LIVE', 'SCHEDULED', 'EXPIRED', 'USED_UP', 'INACTIVE'] as const;
export type CouponStatus = (typeof COUPON_STATUSES)[number];

const statusMeta = new Map<CouponStatus, { label: string; color: string }>([
  ['LIVE', { label: 'Live', color: 'success' }],
  ['SCHEDULED', { label: 'Scheduled', color: 'processing' }],
  ['EXPIRED', { label: 'Expired', color: 'default' }],
  ['USED_UP', { label: 'Used up', color: 'warning' }],
  ['INACTIVE', { label: 'Inactive', color: 'error' }],
]);

export function getCouponStatusMeta(status: CouponStatus): { label: string; color: string } {
  return statusMeta.get(status) ?? { label: status, color: 'default' };
}

/** Whether customers can use the coupon right now, worked out from its switch, dates and redemptions. */
export function getCouponStatus(coupon: Coupon, now = dayjs()): CouponStatus {
  if (!coupon.isActive) return 'INACTIVE';
  if (now.isBefore(coupon.startTime)) return 'SCHEDULED';
  if (now.isAfter(coupon.endTime)) return 'EXPIRED';
  if (coupon.redemptionLimit !== undefined && coupon.redemptions >= coupon.redemptionLimit) return 'USED_UP';
  return 'LIVE';
}

  { key: 'discount', title: 'Discount' },

/** Only the page content scrolls (see AdminLayout.css), so the section list follows that area. */
function getScrollContainer(): HTMLElement | Window {
  return document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.admin-layout__content') ?? window;
}

function Section({ id, ...props }: PropsWithChildren<{ id: SectionKey; title: string; description: string }>) {
  return (
    <div id={sectionId(id)}>
      <FormSection {...props} />
    </div>
  );

/** "13:30" â†’ a dayjs value the time picker can show. */
function toTimeValue(time: TimeOfDay | undefined): Dayjs | undefined {
  if (!time) return undefined;
  const [hour = 0, minute = 0] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  return dayjs().hour(hour).minute(minute).second(0).millisecond(0);
}

function toFormValues(coupon: Coupon): CouponFormInput {
  const [slot1, slot2] = coupon.slotRanges;
  return {
    name: coupon.name,
    label: coupon.label,
    instruction: coupon.instruction,
    description: coupon.description,
    code: coupon.code,
    referenceId: coupon.referenceId,
    sequence: coupon.sequence,
    type: coupon.type,
    valueType: coupon.valueType,
    value: coupon.value,
    maxDiscount: coupon.maxDiscount,
    minBillAmount: coupon.minBillAmount,
    target: coupon.target,
    targetIds: coupon.targetIds,
    useReferenceId: coupon.useReferenceId,
    applyOnMrp: coupon.applyOnMrp,
    isActive: coupon.isActive,
    startTime: dayjs(coupon.startTime),
    endTime: dayjs(coupon.endTime),
    hasRedemptionLimit: coupon.redemptionLimit !== undefined,
    redemptionLimit: coupon.redemptionLimit,
    hasPerCustomerLimit: coupon.perCustomerLimit !== undefined,
    perCustomerLimit: coupon.perCustomerLimit,
    isPrivate: coupon.isPrivate,
    allowedPhoneNumbers: coupon.allowedPhoneNumbers.join(', '),
    inactivitySegments: coupon.inactivitySegments,
    platforms: coupon.platforms,
    daysOfWeek: coupon.daysOfWeek,
    applicationStart: toTimeValue(coupon.applicationTime?.start),
    applicationEnd: toTimeValue(coupon.applicationTime?.end),
    deliveryDates: coupon.deliveryDates.map((date) => dayjs(date)),
    deliveryDayOffsets: coupon.deliveryDayOffsets,
    slot1Start: toTimeValue(slot1?.start),
    slot1End: toTimeValue(slot1?.end),
    slot2Start: toTimeValue(slot2?.start),
    slot2End: toTimeValue(slot2?.end),
    restrictToOutlets: coupon.outletIds.length > 0,
    outletIds: coupon.outletIds,
  };
}

function getNewCouponValues(): Partial<CouponFormInput> {
  return {
    sequence: 0,
    type: 'SIMPLE',
    valueType: 'PERCENT',
    target: 'ORDER',
    isActive: true,
    startTime: dayjs().second(0).millisecond(0),
    targetIds: [],
    inactivitySegments: [],
    platforms: [],
    daysOfWeek: [],
    deliveryDayOffsets: [],
    outletIds: [],
  };
}

/** What customers will see, updated while the form is filled in. */
function CouponPreview({ coupon }: { coupon?: Coupon }) {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<CouponFormInput>();
  const code: unknown = Form.useWatch('code', form);
  const label: unknown = Form.useWatch('label', form);
  const value: unknown = Form.useWatch('value', form);
  const valueType: unknown = Form.useWatch('valueType', form);
  const maxDiscount: unknown = Form.useWatch('maxDiscount', form);
  const minBill: unknown = Form.useWatch('minBillAmount', form);

  const hasValue = typeof value === 'number' && value > 0;
  const isPercent = valueType === 'PERCENT';
  const valueText = hasValue
    ? `${formatCouponValue({ value, valueType: valueType as CouponValueType })} OFF`
    : 'Set a value';
  const notes = [
    isPercent && typeof maxDiscount === 'number' && maxDiscount > 0 ? `Up to ${formatCurrency(maxDiscount)}` : '',
    typeof minBill === 'number' && minBill > 0 ? `On orders above ${formatCurrency(minBill)}` : '',
  ].filter(Boolean);

  return (
    <div className="coupon-preview" aria-label="Coupon preview">
      <span className="coupon-preview__value">{valueText}</span>
      <span className="coupon-preview__label">
        {typeof label === 'string' && label.trim() ? label : 'Coupon label'}
      </span>
      <span className="coupon-preview__code">
        {typeof code === 'string' && code.trim() ? code.trim().toUpperCase() : 'CODE'}
      </span>
      {notes.length > 0 && <span className="coupon-preview__meta">{notes.join(' Â· ')}</span>}
      {coupon && (
        <span className="coupon-preview__meta">
          Now: {getCouponStatusMeta(getCouponStatus(coupon)).label} Â· Used {coupon.redemptions} times
        </span>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

type CouponFormViewProps = {
  /** The coupon being edited. Missing when creating a new coupon. */
  coupon?: Coupon;
};

function CouponFormView({ coupon }: CouponFormViewProps) {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<CouponFormInput>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const isEdit = coupon !== undefined;

  const type: CouponType | undefined = Form.useWatch('type', form);
  const valueType: CouponValueType | undefined = Form.useWatch('valueType', form);
  const isPrivate: boolean | undefined = Form.useWatch('isPrivate', form);
  const targeted = type ? TARGETED_COUPON_TYPES.get(type) : undefined;
  const isPercent = valueType !== 'FIXED';

  const outletsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: couponQueryKeys.outletOptions, queryFn: listCouponOutletOptions });
  const outletOptions = (outletsQuery.data ?? []).map((outlet) => ({ value: outlet.id, label: outlet.name }));

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: saveCoupon,
    onSuccess: async (saved) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: couponQueryKeys.all });
      void message.success(isEdit ? `Coupon "${saved.code}" was updated.` : `Coupon "${saved.code}" was created.`);
      void navigate('/coupons');
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error(
        `The coupon could not be ${isEdit ? 'updated' : 'created'}. Check that no other coupon has this code, then try again.`,
      );
    },
  });

  const handleFinish = (values: CouponFormInput) => {
    const result = couponSchema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) {
      showZodErrorsInForm(form, result.error);
      return;
    }
    mutation.mutate({ couponId: coupon?.id, payload: result.data });
  };

  const goBack = () => void navigate('/coupons');

  return (
    <div className="coupons-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={isEdit ? `Edit coupon ${coupon.code}` : 'Create new coupon'}
        subtitle={
          isEdit
            ? 'Change the discount, when it is valid and who can use it.'
            : 'Set the discount, when it is valid and who can use it. Only the Basics, Discount and Validity sections are required.'
        }
        backLabel="Back to coupons"
        onBack={goBack}
      />

      <Form<CouponFormInput>
        form={form}
        layout="vertical"
        initialValues={coupon ? toFormValues(coupon) : getNewCouponValues()}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
        disabled={mutation.isPending}
        autoComplete="off"
      >
        <div className="coupon-form-layout">
          <div className="coupons-page__stack">
            <Section id="basics" title="Basics" description="How the coupon is named and shown to customers.">
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Name" name="name" required rules={[zodRule(fields.name)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Diwali special" maxLength={100} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Label"
                    name="label"
                    tooltip="Short text on the coupon card, for example 20% OFF."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.label)]}
                  >
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. 20% OFF" maxLength={60} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={8}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Coupon code"
                    name="code"
                    required
                    extra="Customers type this at checkout. Saved in capital letters."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.code)]}
                    normalize={(value: string) => value.toUpperCase().replace(/\s/g, '')}
                  >
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. SPL20" maxLength={30} className="coupons-code-input" spellCheck={false} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={8}>
                  <Form.Item label="Reference ID" name="referenceId" rules={[zodRule(fields.referenceId)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="Coupon reference ID" maxLength={100} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={8}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Sequence"
                    name="sequence"
                    required
                    extra="Higher numbers appear first in the coupon list."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.sequence)]}
                  >
                    <InputNumber min={0} max={9999} precision={0} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Instruction" name="instruction" rules={[zodRule(fields.instruction)]}>
                    <Input.TextArea
                      placeholder="How to use it, e.g. Apply at checkout on orders above â‚¹199"
                      autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 5 }}
                      maxLength={500}
                      showCount
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Description" name="description" rules={[zodRule(fields.description)]}>
                    <Input.TextArea
                      placeholder="Terms and details shown to customers"
                      autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 5 }}
                      maxLength={1000}
                      showCount
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Section>

            <Section id="discount" title="Discount" description="What kind of coupon it is and how much it takes off.">
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Coupon type" name="type" required rules={[zodRule(fields.type)]}>
                    <Select options={typeOptions} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Coupon target" name="target" required rules={[zodRule(fields.target)]}>
                    <Select options={targetOptions} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Value type" name="valueType" required rules={[zodRule(fields.valueType)]}>
                    <Select options={valueTypeOptions} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Coupon value" name="value" required rules={[zodRule(fields.value)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      placeholder={isPercent ? 'e.g. 20' : 'e.g. 50'}
                      min={0}
                      max={isPercent ? 100 : 1_000_000}
                      step={isPercent ? 1 : 10}
                      prefix={isPercent ? undefined : 'â‚¹'}
                      suffix={isPercent ? '%' : undefined}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                {isPercent && (
                  <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                    <Form.Item
                      label="Max discount"
                      name="maxDiscount"
                      extra="The most a percent discount can take off. Leave empty for no cap."
                      rules={[zodRule(fields.maxDiscount)]}
                    >
                      <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 100" min={0} prefix="â‚¹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                    </Form.Item>
                  </Col>
                )}
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Minimum bill amount"
                    name="minBillAmount"
                    required={type === 'MINIMUM_BILL_AMOUNT'}
                    extra="The order must be at least this much. Leave empty for any amount."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.minBillAmount)]}
                  >
                    <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 199" min={0} prefix="â‚¹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                {targeted && (
                  <Col span={24}>
                    <Form.Item
                      label={targeted.label}
                      name="targetIds"
                      required
                      extra={`${targeted.hint} Type an ID and press Enter, or paste IDs separated by commas.`}
                      rules={[zodRule(fields.targetIds)]}
                    >
                      <Select
                        mode="tags"
                        placeholder="Type an ID and press Enter"
                        tokenSeparators={[',', ' ']}
                        open={false}
                      />
                    </Form.Item>
                  </Col>
                )}
              </Row>
              {targeted && (
                <SwitchCard
                  name="useReferenceId"
                  title="Use reference ID"
                  hint="Match the IDs above by the catalog's reference ID instead of the Presto ID."
                />
              )}
              <SwitchCard
                name="applyOnMrp"
                title="Apply coupon on MRP"
                hint="Work out the discount from the MRP instead of the base price."
              />
            </Section>

            <Section id="validity" title="Validity" description="When customers can use the coupon.">
              <SwitchCard
                name="isActive"
                title="Coupon active"
                hint="Turn off to pause the coupon at any time, even inside its dates."
              />
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Start time" name="startTime" required rules={[zodRule(fields.startTime)]}>
                    <DatePicker
                      showTime={{ format: TIME_FORMAT }}
                      format={DATE_TIME_FORMAT}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="End time" name="endTime" required rules={[zodRule(fields.endTime)]}>
                    <DatePicker
                      showTime={{ format: TIME_FORMAT, defaultOpenValue: dayjs().hour(23).minute(59) }}
                      format={DATE_TIME_FORMAT}
                      placeholder="Pick the end date"
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Section>

            <Section id="limits" title="Usage limits" description="How many times the coupon can be used. Optional.">
              <SwitchCard
                name="hasRedemptionLimit"
                title="Set redemption limit"
                hint="Stop the coupon after it has been used this many times in total. Off means unlimited."
              >
                <Form.Item
                  label="Total redemptions allowed"
                  name="redemptionLimit"
                  required
                  rules={[zodRule(fields.redemptionLimit)]}
                >
                  <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 500" min={1} precision={0} style={{ width: 220 }} />
                </Form.Item>
              </SwitchCard>
              <SwitchCard
                name="hasPerCustomerLimit"
                title="Limit usage per customer"
                hint="Based on the registered mobile number on completed orders. Separate from the total limit."
              >
                <Form.Item
                  label="Uses per customer"
                  name="perCustomerLimit"
                  required
                  rules={[zodRule(fields.perCustomerLimit)]}
                >
                  <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 1" min={1} precision={0} style={{ width: 220 }} />
                </Form.Item>
              </SwitchCard>
            </Section>

            <Section
              id="audience"
              title="Who can use it"
              description="Limit the coupon to some customers or platforms. Leave empty to allow everyone."
            >
              <SwitchCard
                name="isPrivate"
                title="Private"
                hint="Only the phone numbers below can use it, and it is hidden from the coupon list."
              />
              <Form.Item
                label="Restrict to these phone numbers"
                name="allowedPhoneNumbers"
                required={isPrivate === true}
                extra="10-digit numbers separated by commas, spaces or new lines."
              >
                <Input.TextArea
                  placeholder="e.g. 9876543210, 9123456780"
                  autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 8 }}
                  spellCheck={false}
                />
              </Form.Item>
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-subhead">Restrict by user inactivity</Typography.Text>
              <Form.Item
                name="inactivitySegments"
                extra="Days since the customer's last order. Leave all off to allow everyone. Customers who never ordered are always allowed."
              >
                <ChipGroup options={inactivityOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-subhead">Restrict by platform</Typography.Text>
              <Form.Item
                name="platforms"
                extra="Leave all off to allow every platform. Web is detected by source=WEB; apps must send os=ios or os=android at checkout."
              >
                <ChipGroup options={platformOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Section>

            <Section
              id="schedule"
              title="When it applies"
              description="Days and hours the coupon can be applied, in the merchant's time zone."
            >
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-subhead">Restrict by day of week</Typography.Text>
              <Form.Item name="daysOfWeek" extra="Leave all off to allow every day.">
                <ChipGroup options={weekDayOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Apply from (time of day)"
                    name="applicationStart"
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.applicationStart)]}
                  >
                    <TimePicker format={TIME_FORMAT} minuteStep={5} needConfirm={false} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                  <Form.Item
                    label="Apply until (time of day)"
                    name="applicationEnd"
                    extra="Leave both empty to allow any time. Based on when the coupon is applied, not the delivery slot."
                    rules={[zodRule(fields.applicationEnd)]}
                  >
                    <TimePicker format={TIME_FORMAT} minuteStep={5} needConfirm={false} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Section>

            <Section
              id="delivery"
              title="Delivery"
              description="Limit the coupon to some delivery dates, days or checkout slots. Leave empty to allow all."
            >
              <Form.Item
                label="Restrict by delivery date"
                name="deliveryDates"
                extra="Pick one or more dates. The coupon then applies only to orders delivered on those dates."
              >
                <DatePicker
                  multiple
                  format="DD MMM YYYY"
                  placeholder="Pick dates"
                  maxTagCount="responsive"
                  style={{ width: '100%' }}
                />
              </Form.Item>
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-subhead">Restrict by delivery day (relative)</Typography.Text>
              <Form.Item
                name="deliveryDayOffsets"
                extra="The discount applies only to items delivered on the chosen days. For example, Tomorrow only skips same-day items."
              >
                <ChipGroup options={deliveryDayOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-subhead">Delivery slot offer (checkout slot time)</Typography.Text>
              <Row gutter={16}>
                {(['slot1', 'slot2'] as const).map((slot, index) => (
                  <Col xs={24} md={12} key={slot}>
                    <Row gutter={8}>
                      <Col span={12}>
                        <Form.Item label={`Range ${index + 1} start`} name={`${slot}Start`}>
                          <TimePicker
                            format={TIME_FORMAT}
                            minuteStep={5}
                            needConfirm={false}
                            style={{ width: '100%' }}
                          />
                        </Form.Item>
                      </Col>
                      <Col span={12}>
                        <Form.Item label={`Range ${index + 1} end`} name={`${slot}End`}>
                          <TimePicker
                            format={TIME_FORMAT}
                            minuteStep={5}
                            needConfirm={false}
                            style={{ width: '100%' }}
                          />
                        </Form.Item>
                      </Col>
                    </Row>
                  </Col>
                ))}
              </Row>
              <Typography.Text className="coupons-muted">
                Leave both ranges empty to allow any slot. The discount applies only to items whose slot fits fully
                inside a range (for example 1:30 PM to 3:00 PM).
              </Typography.Text>
            </Section>

            <Section id="outlets" title="Outlets" description="Where the coupon can be used.">
              <SwitchCard
                name="restrictToOutlets"
                title="Restrict to specific outlets"
                hint="Off means the coupon works at every outlet."
              >
                <Form.Item label="Outlets" name="outletIds" required rules={[zodRule(fields.outletIds)]}>
                  <Select
                    mode="multiple"
                    allowClear
                    placeholder="Choose outlets"
                    options={outletOptions}
                    loading={outletsQuery.isPending}
                    showSearch={{ optionFilterProp: 'label' }}
                    notFoundContent={outletsQuery.isError ? 'Outlets could not be loaded' : undefined}
                  />
                </Form.Item>
              </SwitchCard>
            </Section>
          </div>

          <aside className="coupon-form-nav">
            <Card className="coupon-form-nav__card" variant="borderless" size="small">
              <Typography.Text className="coupon-form-nav__title">On this page</Typography.Text>
              <Anchor
                affix={false}
                replace
                getContainer={getScrollContainer}
                targetOffset={16}
                items={SECTIONS.map((section) => ({
                  key: section.key,
                  href: `#${sectionId(section.key)}`,
                  title: section.title,
                }))}
              />
            </Card>
            <CouponPreview coupon={coupon} />
          </aside>
        </div>

        <div className="coupon-form-actions">
          <FormActions>
            <Button onClick={goBack} disabled={false}>
              Cancel
            </Button>
            <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending}>
              {isEdit ? 'Save changes' : 'Create coupon'}
            </Button>
          </FormActions>
        </div>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}

function EditCouponView({ couponId }: { couponId: string }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const couponQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: couponQueryKeys.detail(couponId),
    queryFn: () => getCoupon(couponId),
    retry: false,
  });

  if (couponQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <Result
        status="404"
        title="Coupon not found"
        subTitle="It may have been deleted, or the link is wrong."
        extra={
          <Button type="primary" onClick={() => void navigate('/coupons')}>
            Back to coupons
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (couponQuery.isPending) {
    return <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 12 }} />;
  }

  return <CouponFormView coupon={couponQuery.data} />;
}

/** Create coupon (/coupons/new) and edit coupon (/coupons/:couponId/edit). */
export default function CouponFormPage() {
  const { couponId } = useParams();
  return couponId === undefined ? <CouponFormView /> : <EditCouponView couponId={couponId} />;
}

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/CouponTaskDetailsPage.tsx =====
import { DownloadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Card, Descriptions, Result, Skeleton, Typography, type DescriptionsProps } from 'antd';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { PageHeader } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { downloadCouponTaskFile, getCouponTask, type CouponTask } from '@/features/coupons/api/coupon-tasks-api';
import { couponQueryKeys } from '@/features/coupons/api/query-keys';
import { TaskStateTag, TaskTypeTag } from '@/features/users/components/TaskTags';
import { formatDateTimeWithSeconds } from '@/features/users/format';
import { downloadTextFile } from '@/lib/download';
import '@/features/coupons/coupons.css';
import '@/features/users/users.css';

const NOT_SET = <span className="coupons-muted">-</span>;

function TaskDetails({ task }: { task: CouponTask }) {
  const { message } = App.useApp();

  const downloadMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: downloadCouponTaskFile,
    onSuccess: (file) => downloadTextFile(file.fileName, file.content),
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The file could not be downloaded. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  // Error texts come from the server and can contain pieces of the uploaded file, so they are shown
  // as plain text only.
  const items: DescriptionsProps['items'] = [
    { key: 'type', label: 'Task type', children: <TaskTypeTag type={task.type} /> },
    { key: 'state', label: 'Status', children: <TaskStateTag state={task.state} /> },
    { key: 'createdAt', label: 'Created at', children: formatDateTimeWithSeconds(task.createdAt) },
    { key: 'email', label: 'Notification email', children: task.notificationEmail },
    {
      key: 'file',
      label: 'Coupons CSV',
      children: task.hasFile ? (
        <Button
          icon={<DownloadOutlined />}
          loading={downloadMutation.isPending}
          onClick={() => downloadMutation.mutate(task.id)}
        >
          Download
        </Button>
      ) : (
        NOT_SET
      ),
    },
    { key: 'error', label: 'Error message', children: task.errorMessage ?? NOT_SET },
    {
      key: 'errorRow',
      label: (
        <>
          Error row
          <Typography.Text className="task-details__hint">
            The row in the CSV file that caused this error
          </Typography.Text>
        </>
      ),
      children: task.errorRow ? <code className="task-details__row">{task.errorRow}</code> : NOT_SET,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <Card className="task-details" variant="borderless">
      <Descriptions bordered column={1} items={items} styles={{ label: { width: 260 } }} />
    </Card>
  );
}

/** The details of one coupon import or export task (/coupons/tasks/:taskId). */
export default function CouponTaskDetailsPage() {
  const { taskId = '' } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const goBack = () => void navigate('/coupons/tasks');

  const taskQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: couponQueryKeys.task(taskId),
    queryFn: () => getCouponTask(taskId),
    retry: false,
  });

  if (taskQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <Result
        status="404"
        title="Task not found"
        subTitle="It may have been removed, or the link is wrong."
        extra={
          <Button type="primary" onClick={goBack}>
            Back to tasks
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="coupons-page coupons-page__stack">
      <PageHeader
        title="Coupon bulk update task"
        subtitle="What this import or export did, and its file."
        backLabel="Back to tasks"
        onBack={goBack}
      />
      {taskQuery.isPending ? <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 7 }} /> : <TaskDetails task={taskQuery.data} />}
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/CouponTasksPage.tsx =====
import { ArrowLeftOutlined, PlusOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { Button } from 'antd';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ListPanel } from '@/components/ui/ListPanel';
import { LoadError } from '@/components/ui/LoadError';
import { listCouponTasks } from '@/features/coupons/api/coupon-tasks-api';
import { couponQueryKeys } from '@/features/coupons/api/query-keys';
import { TasksTable } from '@/features/users/components/TasksTable';
import { isTaskRunning } from '@/features/users/format';
import '@/features/coupons/coupons.css';

/** While a task is still running, the list checks again this often (in milliseconds). */
const REFRESH_WHILE_RUNNING_MS = 10_000;

/** Bulk import / export tasks for coupons (/coupons/tasks). */
export default function CouponTasksPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const tasksQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: couponQueryKeys.tasks,
    queryFn: listCouponTasks,
    refetchInterval: (query) =>
      query.state.data?.some((task) => isTaskRunning(task.state)) ? REFRESH_WHILE_RUNNING_MS : false,
  });

  const tasks = tasksQuery.data ?? [];

  return (
    <div className="coupons-page">
      <ListPanel
        title="Bulk import / export tasks"
        count={tasksQuery.data ? tasks.length : undefined}
        subtitle="Bulk jobs that add or update coupons from a CSV file, or create a CSV file of coupons."
        toolbar={
          <>
            <Button icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/coupons')}>
              Back to coupons
            </Button>
            <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/coupons/tasks/new')}>
              Create new task
            </Button>
          </>
        }
        footer={tasksQuery.data && 'Sample data'}
      >
        {tasksQuery.isError ? (
          <LoadError what="tasks" onRetry={() => void tasksQuery.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <TasksTable
            tasks={tasks}
            loading={tasksQuery.isPending}
            onView={(task) => void navigate(`/coupons/tasks/${encodeURIComponent(task.id)}`)}
          />
        )}
      </ListPanel>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/CouponsPage.tsx =====
import { CloudSyncOutlined, PlusOutlined, SearchOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Checkbox, Input, Select } from 'antd';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ListPanel } from '@/components/ui/ListPanel';
import { LoadError } from '@/components/ui/LoadError';
import { deleteCoupon, listCoupons } from '@/features/coupons/api/coupons-api';
import { couponQueryKeys } from '@/features/coupons/api/query-keys';
import { CouponsTable } from '@/features/coupons/components/CouponsTable';
import {
  COUPON_STATUSES,
  getCouponStatus,
  getCouponStatusMeta,
  isReferralCoupon,
  type CouponStatus,
} from '@/features/coupons/format';
import type { Coupon } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/coupons/coupons.css';

type StatusFilter = 'all' | CouponStatus;

const statusOptions: { value: StatusFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Any status' },
  ...COUPON_STATUSES.map((status) => ({ value: status, label: getCouponStatusMeta(status).label })),
];

function matchesSearch(coupon: Coupon, search: string): boolean {
  if (!search) return true;
  return `${coupon.code} ${coupon.name}`.toLowerCase().includes(search);
}

export default function CouponsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>('all');
  const [excludeReferral, setExcludeReferral] = useState(true);

  const couponsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: couponQueryKeys.all, queryFn: listCoupons });

  const deleteMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (coupon: Coupon) => deleteCoupon(coupon.id),
    onSuccess: async (_, coupon) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: couponQueryKeys.all });
      void message.success(`Coupon "${coupon.code}" was deleted.`);
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The coupon could not be deleted. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  const coupons = couponsQuery.data ?? [];
  const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();
  const visibleCoupons = coupons.filter(
    (coupon) =>
      matchesSearch(coupon, normalizedSearch) &&
      !(excludeReferral && isReferralCoupon(coupon)) &&
      (statusFilter === 'all' || getCouponStatus(coupon) === statusFilter),
  );

  return (
    <div className="coupons-page">
      <ListPanel
        title="All coupons"
        count={couponsQuery.data ? coupons.length : undefined}
        subtitle="Discount codes customers can apply at checkout."
        toolbar={
          <>
            <Input
              allowClear
              placeholder="Search coupon code or name"
              prefix={<SearchOutlined />}
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              aria-label="Search coupons"
            />
            <Select<StatusFilter>
              className="coupons-toolbar__filter"
              value={statusFilter}
              options={statusOptions}
              onChange={setStatusFilter}
              aria-label="Filter by status"
            />
            <Checkbox
              className="coupons-toolbar__check"
              checked={excludeReferral}
              onChange={(event) => setExcludeReferral(event.target.checked)}
            >
              Exclude referral coupons
            </Checkbox>
            <Button icon={<CloudSyncOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/coupons/tasks')}>
              Bulk update tasks
            </Button>
            <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/coupons/new')}>
              Create coupon
            </Button>
          </>
        }
        footer={
          couponsQuery.data &&
          `Showing ${formatCount(visibleCoupons.length)} of ${formatCount(coupons.length)} coupons · Sample data`
        }
      >
        {couponsQuery.isError ? (
          <LoadError what="coupons" onRetry={() => void couponsQuery.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <CouponsTable
            coupons={visibleCoupons}
            loading={couponsQuery.isPending}
            onEdit={(coupon) => void navigate(`/coupons/${encodeURIComponent(coupon.id)}/edit`)}
            onDelete={(coupon) => deleteMutation.mutate(coupon)}
          />
        )}
      </ListPanel>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/CouponsPage.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/NewCouponTaskPage.tsx =====
import { UploadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Col, Form, Input, Radio, Row, Upload, type UploadFile, type UploadProps } from 'antd';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { FormActions, FormSection, PageHeader } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { createCouponTask } from '@/features/coupons/api/coupon-tasks-api';
import { couponQueryKeys } from '@/features/coupons/api/query-keys';
import { TASK_TYPES, type TaskType } from '@/features/users/constants';
import {
  csvFileSchema,
  MAX_CSV_SIZE_MB,
  taskFields,
  taskSchema,
  type TaskFormInput,
} from '@/features/users/schemas/task.schema';
import { showZodErrorsInForm, zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import '@/features/coupons/coupons.css';

const fields = taskFields.shape;

/** The form holds the chosen file as Ant Design's file list. It becomes a plain File on submit. */
type TaskFormValues = Omit<TaskFormInput, 'file'> & { file?: UploadFile[] };

const TYPE_OPTIONS = TASK_TYPES.map((type) => ({ value: type, label: type === 'import' ? 'Import' : 'Export' }));

const TYPE_HINTS = new Map<TaskType, string>([
  ['import', 'Adds or updates coupons in bulk from a CSV file.'],
  ['export', 'Creates a CSV file of every coupon. Download it from the task list when it is done.'],
]);

const initialValues: Partial<TaskFormValues> = { type: 'import' };

/** New coupon import / export task (/coupons/tasks/new). */
export default function NewCouponTaskPage() {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<TaskFormValues>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();

  const type: TaskType = Form.useWatch('type', form) ?? 'import';
  const goBack = () => void navigate('/coupons/tasks');

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: createCouponTask,
    onSuccess: async (task) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: couponQueryKeys.tasks });
      void message.success(`The task was created. We will email ${task.notificationEmail} when it is done.`);
      goBack();
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The task could not be created. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  // Checks the file the moment it is chosen, so a wrong file is refused before anything is sent.
  const beforeUpload: UploadProps['beforeUpload'] = (file) => {
    const result = csvFileSchema.safeParse(file);
    if (!result.success) {
      void message.error(result.error.issues[0]?.message ?? 'This file can not be used.');
      return Upload.LIST_IGNORE;
    }
    // Keep the file in the form; it is sent together with the rest of the form on submit.
    return false;
  };

  const handleFinish = (values: TaskFormValues) => {
    const result = taskSchema.safeParse({ ...values, file: values.file?.[0]?.originFileObj });
    if (!result.success) {
      showZodErrorsInForm(form, result.error);
      return;
    }
    mutation.mutate({ payload: result.data });
  };

  return (
    <div className="coupons-page coupons-page__stack">
      <PageHeader
        title="New bulk update task"
        subtitle="Import coupons from a CSV file, or export them to one."
        backLabel="Back to tasks"
        onBack={goBack}
      />

      <Form<TaskFormValues>
        form={form}
        layout="vertical"
        initialValues={initialValues}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
        disabled={mutation.isPending}
        className="coupons-page__stack"
      >
        <FormSection title="Task details" description="What the task does and who is told when it is finished.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Task type"
                name="type"
                required
                extra={TYPE_HINTS.get(type)}
                rules={[zodRule(fields.type)]}
              >
                <Radio.Group options={TYPE_OPTIONS} optionType="button" buttonStyle="solid" />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Email to be notified"
                name="notificationEmail"
                required
                extra="We email this address when the task is finished, saying whether it worked. Use an address you check regularly."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.notificationEmail)]}
              >
                <Input type="email" placeholder="Email" maxLength={254} autoCapitalize="off" spellCheck={false} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>

            {type === 'import' && (
              <Col xs={24} md={12}>
                <Form.Item
                  label="Coupon CSV file"
                  name="file"
                  required
                  valuePropName="fileList"
                  getValueFromEvent={(event: { fileList: UploadFile[] }) => event.fileList}
                  extra={`A .csv file, up to ${MAX_CSV_SIZE_MB} MB.`}
                >
                  <Upload accept=".csv,text/csv" beforeUpload={beforeUpload} maxCount={1}>
                    <Button icon={<UploadOutlined />}>Choose file</Button>
                  </Upload>
                </Form.Item>
              </Col>
            )}
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormActions>
          <Button onClick={goBack}>Cancel</Button>
          <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending}>
            Submit
          </Button>
        </FormActions>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/pages/NewCouponTaskPage.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/schemas/coupon.schema.ts =====
import dayjs, { type Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  COUPON_TARGETS,
  COUPON_TYPES,
  COUPON_VALUE_TYPES,
  DELIVERY_DAY_OFFSETS,
  INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS,
  PLATFORMS,
  TARGETED_COUPON_TYPES,
  WEEK_DAYS,
} from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import type { Coupon } from '@/features/coupons/types';
import { optionalAmount, optionalText, optionalWholeNumber, requiredDateTime, requiredText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

/**
 * Create/edit coupon form rules. The form shows these messages field by field (see `zodRule`),
 * and `couponSchema` turns the form values into the clean payload sent to the API.
 */

const CODE_PATTERN = /^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_-]*$/;
const PHONE_PATTERN = /^\d{10}$/;
const MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS = 5000;

function isValidDayjs(value: unknown): value is Dayjs {
  return dayjs.isDayjs(value) && value.isValid();
}

/** A time picked in a time picker. The payload gets "13:30"; empty becomes `undefined`. */
const optionalTimeOfDay = z
  .custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick a time' })
  .nullish()
  .transform((value) => value?.format('HH:mm'));

/** 'a, b\nc' → ['a', 'b', 'c']. Commas, spaces, semicolons and new lines all separate numbers. */
function splitPhoneNumbers(text: string): string[] {
  return [...new Set(text.split(/[\s,;]+/).filter(Boolean))];
}

function optionalList<T extends z.ZodType>(item: T, max: number, message: string) {
  return z
    .array(item)
    .max(max, message)
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? []);
}

/** Each field on its own. Rules that compare fields are in `couponSchema` below. */
export const couponFields = z.object({
  // Basics
  name: requiredText('Enter the coupon name', 100),
  label: optionalText(60),
  instruction: optionalText(500),
  description: optionalText(1000),
  code: requiredText('Enter the coupon code', 30)
    .toUpperCase()
    .pipe(
      z
        .string()
        .min(3, 'Use at least 3 characters')
        .regex(CODE_PATTERN, 'Use letters, numbers, - and _ only, with no spaces'),
    ),
  referenceId: optionalText(100),
  sequence: z.number({ error: 'Enter the sequence' }).int('Use a whole number').min(0, 'Use 0 or more').max(9999),

  // Discount
  type: z.enum(COUPON_TYPES, { error: 'Choose the coupon type' }),
  valueType: z.enum(COUPON_VALUE_TYPES, { error: 'Choose the value type' }),
  value: z.number({ error: 'Enter the coupon value' }).positive('Use more than 0').max(1_000_000),
  maxDiscount: optionalAmount(1_000_000),
  minBillAmount: optionalAmount(1_000_000),
  target: z.enum(COUPON_TARGETS, { error: 'Choose the coupon target' }),
  targetIds: optionalList(
    z.string().trim().min(1).max(64, 'IDs must be 64 characters or fewer'),
    500,
    'Add 500 IDs or fewer',
  ),
  useReferenceId: z.boolean().default(false),
  applyOnMrp: z.boolean().default(false),

  // Validity
  isActive: z.boolean().default(true),
  startTime: requiredDateTime('Pick the start date and time'),
  endTime: requiredDateTime('Pick the end date and time'),

  // Usage limits
  hasRedemptionLimit: z.boolean().default(false),
  redemptionLimit: optionalWholeNumber(10_000_000),
  hasPerCustomerLimit: z.boolean().default(false),
  perCustomerLimit: optionalWholeNumber(1000),

  // Who can use it
  isPrivate: z.boolean().default(false),
  allowedPhoneNumbers: z
    .string()
    .nullish()
    .transform((text) => splitPhoneNumbers(text ?? '')),
  inactivitySegments: optionalList(z.enum(INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS), INACTIVITY_SEGMENTS.length, 'Too many segments'),
  platforms: optionalList(z.enum(PLATFORMS), PLATFORMS.length, 'Too many platforms'),

  // When it applies
  daysOfWeek: optionalList(z.enum(WEEK_DAYS), WEEK_DAYS.length, 'Too many days'),
  applicationStart: optionalTimeOfDay,
  applicationEnd: optionalTimeOfDay,

  // Delivery
  deliveryDates: z
    .array(z.custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick a valid date' }))
    .max(60, 'Add 60 dates or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((dates) => [...new Set((dates ?? []).map((date) => date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')))].sort()),
  deliveryDayOffsets: optionalList(
    z.number().refine((value) => (DELIVERY_DAY_OFFSETS as readonly number[]).includes(value)),
    DELIVERY_DAY_OFFSETS.length,
    'Too many days',
  ),
  slot1Start: optionalTimeOfDay,
  slot1End: optionalTimeOfDay,
  slot2Start: optionalTimeOfDay,
  slot2End: optionalTimeOfDay,

  // Outlets
  restrictToOutlets: z.boolean().default(false),
  outletIds: optionalList(z.string().min(1), 1000, 'Choose 1000 outlets or fewer'),
});

type CouponFieldValues = z.output<typeof couponFields>;

/** Both ends of a time range, or neither. The end must come after the start. */
function checkTimeRange(
  ctx: z.RefinementCtx,
  start: string | undefined,
  end: string | undefined,
  [startPath, endPath]: [string, string],
) {
  if (start && !end) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [endPath], message: 'Pick the end time too' });
  if (end && !start) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [startPath], message: 'Pick the start time too' });
  if (start && end && end <= start) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [endPath], message: 'The end time must be after the start time' });
  }
}

function checkCoupon(values: CouponFieldValues, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) {
  if (values.valueType === 'PERCENT' && values.value > 100) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['value'], message: 'A percent discount can be 100 at most' });
  }
  if (values.type === 'MINIMUM_BILL_AMOUNT' && values.minBillAmount === undefined) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['minBillAmount'], message: 'This coupon type needs a minimum bill amount' });
  }
  if (TARGETED_COUPON_TYPES.has(values.type) && values.targetIds.length === 0) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['targetIds'], message: 'Add at least one ID' });
  }
  if (dayjs(values.endTime).isBefore(dayjs(values.startTime)) || values.endTime === values.startTime) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['endTime'], message: 'The end must be after the start' });
  }
  if (values.hasRedemptionLimit && !values.redemptionLimit) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['redemptionLimit'], message: 'Enter how many times it can be used' });
  }
  if (values.hasPerCustomerLimit && !values.perCustomerLimit) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['perCustomerLimit'], message: 'Enter the uses per customer' });
  }

  const invalidPhones = values.allowedPhoneNumbers.filter((phone) => !PHONE_PATTERN.test(phone));
  if (invalidPhones.length > 0) {
    const shown = invalidPhones.slice(0, 5).join(', ');
    ctx.addIssue({
      code: 'custom',
      path: ['allowedPhoneNumbers'],
      message: `These are not 10-digit phone numbers: ${shown}${invalidPhones.length > 5 ? ' …' : ''}`,
    });
  } else if (values.allowedPhoneNumbers.length > MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS) {
    ctx.addIssue({
      code: 'custom',
      path: ['allowedPhoneNumbers'],
      message: `Use ${MAX_PHONE_NUMBERS} phone numbers or fewer`,
    });
  } else if (values.isPrivate && values.allowedPhoneNumbers.length === 0) {
    ctx.addIssue({
      code: 'custom',
      path: ['allowedPhoneNumbers'],
      message: 'A private coupon needs at least one phone number',
    });
  }

  checkTimeRange(ctx, values.applicationStart, values.applicationEnd, ['applicationStart', 'applicationEnd']);
  checkTimeRange(ctx, values.slot1Start, values.slot1End, ['slot1Start', 'slot1End']);
  checkTimeRange(ctx, values.slot2Start, values.slot2End, ['slot2Start', 'slot2End']);

  if (values.restrictToOutlets && values.outletIds.length === 0) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['outletIds'], message: 'Choose at least one outlet' });
  }
}

/** The coupon as the API stores it, without what the server sets itself. */
export type CouponPayload = Omit<Coupon, 'id' | 'redemptions'>;

function toRange(start: string | undefined, end: string | undefined) {
  return start && end ? { start, end } : undefined;
}

/** Turns the form values into the API payload. Switched-off options send nothing for their fields. */
function toPayload(values: CouponFieldValues): CouponPayload {
  return {
    name: values.name,
    label: values.label,
    instruction: values.instruction,
    description: values.description,
    code: values.code,
    referenceId: values.referenceId,
    sequence: values.sequence,
    type: values.type,
    valueType: values.valueType,
    value: values.value,
    maxDiscount: values.valueType === 'PERCENT' ? values.maxDiscount : undefined,
    minBillAmount: values.minBillAmount,
    target: values.target,
    targetIds: TARGETED_COUPON_TYPES.has(values.type) ? values.targetIds : [],
    useReferenceId: values.useReferenceId,
    applyOnMrp: values.applyOnMrp,
    isActive: values.isActive,
    startTime: values.startTime,
    endTime: values.endTime,
    redemptionLimit: values.hasRedemptionLimit ? values.redemptionLimit : undefined,
    perCustomerLimit: values.hasPerCustomerLimit ? values.perCustomerLimit : undefined,
    isPrivate: values.isPrivate,
    allowedPhoneNumbers: values.allowedPhoneNumbers,
    inactivitySegments: values.inactivitySegments,
    platforms: values.platforms,
    daysOfWeek: values.daysOfWeek,
    applicationTime: toRange(values.applicationStart, values.applicationEnd),
    deliveryDates: values.deliveryDates,
    deliveryDayOffsets: values.deliveryDayOffsets,
    slotRanges: [toRange(values.slot1Start, values.slot1End), toRange(values.slot2Start, values.slot2End)].filter(
      (range) => range !== undefined,
    ),
    outletIds: values.restrictToOutlets ? values.outletIds : [],
  };
}

export const couponSchema = couponFields.superRefine(checkCoupon).transform(toPayload);

/** What the form holds while the user is typing. */
export type CouponFormInput = z.input<typeof couponFields>;
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/schemas/coupon.schema.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/coupons/types.ts =====
import type {
  CouponTarget,
  CouponType,
  CouponValueType,
  InactivitySegment,
  Platform,
  WeekDay,
} from '@/features/coupons/constants';

export type CouponId = string;

/** A time of day such as "13:30", in the merchant's time zone. */
export type TimeOfDay = string;

export type TimeRange = { start: TimeOfDay; end: TimeOfDay };

/** A coupon, as the API sends it. Dates are ISO text such as "2026-11-30T23:59:00+05:30". */
export type Coupon = {
  id: CouponId;

  // Basics
  name: string;
  label?: string;
  instruction?: string;
  description?: string;
  code: string;
  referenceId?: string;
  /** Higher numbers appear first in the customer's coupon list. */
  sequence: number;

  // Discount
  type: CouponType;
  valueType: CouponValueType;
  value: number;
  /** Cap on a percent discount, in rupees. */
  maxDiscount?: number;
  minBillAmount?: number;
  target: CouponTarget;
  /** Items or categories the coupon applies to (Item Discount, Category Discount, Free Item). */
  targetIds: string[];
  /** Match `targetIds` by the catalog's reference ID instead of the Presto ID. */
  useReferenceId: boolean;
  /** Discount on the MRP instead of the base price. */
  applyOnMrp: boolean;

  // Validity
  isActive: boolean;
  startTime: string;
  endTime: string;

  // Usage
  /** Missing means unlimited. */
  redemptionLimit?: number;
  /** How many times the coupon was used. Counted by the server. */
  redemptions: number;
  /** Uses per customer (by registered mobile number). Missing means no limit. */
  perCustomerLimit?: number;

  // Who can use it
  isPrivate: boolean;
  allowedPhoneNumbers: string[];
  inactivitySegments: InactivitySegment[];
  platforms: Platform[];

  // When it applies
  daysOfWeek: WeekDay[];
  applicationTime?: TimeRange;

  // Delivery
  /** Delivery dates as "2026-12-24". */
  deliveryDates: string[];
  /** 0 = today, 1 = tomorrow, ... */
  deliveryDayOffsets: number[];
  slotRanges: TimeRange[];

  /** Empty means every outlet. */
  outletIds: string[];
};
===== END FILE: src/features/coupons/types.ts =====

/** Waits a little, like a real request would. */

const CITIES = [
  { city: 'Raipur', state: 'Chhattisgarh', pincode: '492001', lat: 21.2514, lng: 81.6296 },
  { city: 'Bhilai', state: 'Chhattisgarh', pincode: '490020', lat: 21.1938, lng: 81.3509 },
  { city: 'Bengaluru', state: 'Karnataka', pincode: '560002', lat: 12.9716, lng: 77.5946 },
];

function buildOperator(index: number): Operator {
  const first = FIRST_NAMES.at(index % FIRST_NAMES.length) ?? 'RAHUL';
  const last = LAST_NAMES.at((index * 3) % LAST_NAMES.length) ?? 'KHAN';
  const place = CITIES.at(index % CITIES.length) ?? CITIES[0];
  const employeeNumber = String(10_029_010 + index * 137).padStart(10, '0');
  const hasAddress = index % 4 === 0;
  const isRated = index % 3 === 0;

  return {
    id: `op-${String(index + 100).padStart(4, '0')}`,
    friendlyId: `OP-${1100 + index}`,
    merchantId: DEMO_MERCHANT_ID,
    name: `${first} ${last}`,
    email: `${employeeNumber}@abispro.com`,
    phone: index % 5 === 0 ? undefined : String(7_000_000_000 + index * 48_271),
    role: OPERATOR_ROLES.at(index % 7 === 0 ? 2 : 1) ?? 'DELIVERY_PARTNER',
    department: index % 7 === 0 ? 'Operations' : 'Delivery',
    outletId: mockOutlets.at(index % mockOutlets.length)?.id,
    rating: isRated ? 3 + ((index * 7) % 5) / 2 : undefined,
    score: isRated ? Math.round(((index * 13) % 90) * 10) / 10 : 0,
    currentCycleCount: index % 6,
    trainedServiceIds: index % 2 === 0 ? ['delivery'] : [],
    isActive: index % 9 !== 0,
    status: OPERATOR_STATUSES.at(index % 11 === 0 ? 2 : index % 4 === 1 ? 1 : 0) ?? 'AVAILABLE',
    address: hasAddress
      ? {
          line1: `${10 + index}, Main Road`,
          city: place?.city,
          state: place?.state,
          country: 'India',
          pincode: place?.pincode,
        }
      : {},
    latitude: hasAddress ? place?.lat : undefined,
    longitude: hasAddress ? place?.lng : undefined,
  };
}

  ...knownOperators,
  ...Array.from({ length: 100 - knownOperators.length }, (_, index) => buildOperator(index)),
];

===== FILE: src/features/operators/api/operators-api.ts =====
import { DEMO_MERCHANT_ID, fakeNetworkDelay, mockOperators } from '@/features/operators/api/mock-operators';
import type { OperatorPayload } from '@/features/operators/schemas/operator.schema';
import type { Operator, OperatorId, OutletOption } from '@/features/operators/types';
import { mockOutlets } from '@/features/outlets/api/mock-outlets';

/**
 * SAMPLE: these functions work on the sample data in `mock-operators.ts` so the whole flow
 * can be tried. Each one has a TODO showing the real call. When the API is ready, only the
 * `api` files change: the pages keep working as they are.
 */

/**
 * Every operator of the merchant.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<Operator[]>('/operators');
 *       return data;
 * If there are many thousands of operators, load them in pages (query parameters) instead.
 */
export async function listOperators(): Promise<Operator[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return structuredClone(mockOperators);
}

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<Operator>(`/operators/${encodeURIComponent(operatorId)}`);
 *       return data;
 * (encodeURIComponent keeps a strange id in the address bar from changing the request path.)
 */
export async function getOperator(operatorId: OperatorId): Promise<Operator> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const operator = mockOperators.find((item) => item.id === operatorId);
  if (!operator) throw new Error('Operator not found');
  return structuredClone(operator);
}

/**
 * Outlets an operator can be assigned to.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<OutletOption[]>('/outlets', { params: { fields: 'id,name' } });
 *       return data;
 */
export async function listOutletOptions(): Promise<OutletOption[]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  return mockOutlets.map((outlet) => ({ id: outlet.id, name: outlet.name }));
}

export type SaveOperatorInput = {
  /** Missing when adding a new operator. */
  operatorId?: OperatorId;
  payload: OperatorPayload;
  /** New picture chosen in the form, if any. */
  picture?: File;
};

function toOperatorFields(payload: OperatorPayload) {
  const { addressLine1, addressLine2, city, state, country, pincode, password, ...rest } = payload;
  // The password goes to the server only. It is never kept with the operator's details.
  void password;
  return {
    ...rest,
    address: { line1: addressLine1, line2: addressLine2, city, state, country, pincode },
  };
}

/**
 * Adds an operator, or updates one when `operatorId` is given.
 * The server must refuse an email that another operator already has, hash the password,
 * and check the picture's type and size again.
 *
 * TODO: const body = new FormData();
 *       body.append('operator', JSON.stringify(payload));
 *       if (picture) body.append('picture', picture);
 *       const { data } = operatorId
 *         ? await apiClient.put<Operator>(`/operators/${encodeURIComponent(operatorId)}`, body)
 *         : await apiClient.post<Operator>('/operators', body);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function saveOperator({ operatorId, payload, picture }: SaveOperatorInput): Promise<Operator> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const index = operatorId ? mockOperators.findIndex((item) => item.id === operatorId) : -1;
  const existing = index >= 0 ? mockOperators.at(index) : undefined;
  if (operatorId && !existing) throw new Error('Operator not found');

  if (mockOperators.some((item) => item.email === payload.email && item.id !== existing?.id)) {
    throw new Error('Another operator already has this email');
  }

  const saved: Operator = {
    ...toOperatorFields(payload),
    id: existing?.id ?? crypto.randomUUID(),
    friendlyId: existing?.friendlyId ?? `OP-${2000 + mockOperators.length}`,
    merchantId: existing?.merchantId ?? DEMO_MERCHANT_ID,
    status: existing?.status ?? 'OFFLINE',
    // The sample keeps the picture in this browser tab only. The real server returns its own link.
    pictureUrl: picture ? URL.createObjectURL(picture) : existing?.pictureUrl,
  };

  if (existing) {
    mockOperators.splice(index, 1, saved);
  } else {
    mockOperators.unshift(saved);
  }
  return structuredClone(saved);
}

/**
 * TODO: await apiClient.delete(`/operators/${encodeURIComponent(operatorId)}`);
 */
export async function deleteOperator(operatorId: OperatorId): Promise<void> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const index = mockOperators.findIndex((item) => item.id === operatorId);
  if (index >= 0) mockOperators.splice(index, 1);
}

import type { OperatorId } from '@/features/operators/types';

/**
 * Names of the cached server data. Used when loading data and when refreshing it after a change,
 * so a typo in one place can't leave a list out of date.
 */
export const operatorQueryKeys = {
  /** Every operator query. Refreshing this refreshes the list and every opened operator. */
  all: ['operators'] as const,
  detail: (operatorId: OperatorId) => ['operators', operatorId] as const,
  outletOptions: ['operators', 'outlet-options'] as const,
};
