import { CheckCircleFilled, MinusCircleOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { Avatar, Button, Empty, Grid, Rate, Table, Tag, Typography, type TableColumnsType } from 'antd';
import type { OperatorStatus } from '@/features/operators/constants';
import { formatOperatorAddress, getInitials, getStatusMeta, isRealPhone } from '@/features/operators/format';
import type { Operator } from '@/features/operators/types';
import { RowActions } from '@/components/ui/RowActions';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/operators/operators.css';

/** Height of the scrollable table body. Rows outside this area are not rendered (virtual scroll). */
const TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT = 560;

export type OperatorActions = {
  onEdit: (operator: Operator) => void;
  onDelete: (operator: Operator) => void;
};

type OperatorsTableProps = OperatorActions & {
  operators: Operator[];
  loading?: boolean;
};

function Muted({ children = '-' }: { children?: string }) {
  return <span className="operators-muted">{children}</span>;
}

export function OperatorAvatar({ operator, size = 36 }: { operator: Operator; size?: number }) {
  return (
    <Avatar
      size={size}
      src={operator.pictureUrl}
      alt={operator.pictureUrl ? `Photo of ${operator.name}` : undefined}
      className={`operator-avatar${operator.isActive ? '' : ' operator-avatar--inactive'}`}
    >
      {getInitials(operator.name)}
    </Avatar>
  );
}

export function OperatorStatusTag({ status }: { status: OperatorStatus }) {
  const { label, color } = getStatusMeta(status);
  return (
    <Tag color={color} className="operator-status">
      {label}
    </Tag>
  );
}

function getColumns({ onEdit, onDelete }: OperatorActions, isMobile: boolean): TableColumnsType<Operator> {
  return [
    {
      title: 'Operator',
      key: 'name',
      width: 300,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'left',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: 'base' }),
      defaultSortOrder: 'ascend',
      render: (_, operator) => (
        <div className="operator-cell">
          <OperatorAvatar operator={operator} />
          <div className="operator-cell__text">
            <Button type="link" className="operator-cell__name" onClick={() => onEdit(operator)} title={operator.name}>
              {operator.name}
            </Button>
            <Typography.Text className="operator-cell__email" ellipsis={{ tooltip: operator.email }}>
              {operator.email}
            </Typography.Text>
          </div>
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Phone number',
      dataIndex: 'phone',
      key: 'phone',
      width: 140,
      render: (phone: string | undefined) =>
        isRealPhone(phone) ? <span className="operators-table__number">{phone}</span> : <Muted />,
    },
    {
      title: 'Address',
      dataIndex: 'address',
      key: 'address',
      width: 280,
      render: (address: Operator['address']) => {
        const text = formatOperatorAddress(address);
        return text ? (
          <Typography.Text ellipsis={{ tooltip: text }}>{text}</Typography.Text>
        ) : (
          <Muted>No address</Muted>
        );
      },
    },
    {
      title: 'Rating',
      dataIndex: 'rating',
      key: 'rating',
      width: 130,
      sorter: (a, b) => (a.rating ?? 0) - (b.rating ?? 0),
      render: (rating: number | undefined) =>
        rating ? (
          <Rate
            disabled
            allowHalf
            value={rating}
            className="operators-table__rating"
            aria-label={`Rated ${rating} out of 5`}
          />
        ) : (
          <Muted>Not rated</Muted>
        ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Status',
      dataIndex: 'status',
      key: 'status',
      width: 110,
      render: (status: OperatorStatus) => <OperatorStatusTag status={status} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'Active',
      dataIndex: 'isActive',
      key: 'isActive',
      width: 100,
      render: (isActive: boolean) =>
        isActive ? (
          <Tag color="success" icon={<CheckCircleFilled />} className="operator-status" variant="outlined">
            Yes
          </Tag>
        ) : (
          <Tag icon={<MinusCircleOutlined />} className="operator-status">
            No
          </Tag>
        ),
    },
    {
      title: 'Score',
      dataIndex: 'score',
      key: 'score',
      width: 90,
      align: 'right',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.score - b.score,
      render: (score: number) => <span className="operators-table__number">{score.toFixed(1)}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Cycle count',
      dataIndex: 'currentCycleCount',
      key: 'currentCycleCount',
      width: 120,
      align: 'right',
      sorter: (a, b) => a.currentCycleCount - b.currentCycleCount,
      render: (count: number) => <span className="operators-table__number">{formatCount(count)}</span>,
    },
    {
      title: 'Actions',
      key: 'actions',
      width: 100,
      fixed: isMobile ? undefined : 'right',
      render: (_, operator) => (
        <RowActions
          name={operator.name}
          what="operator"
          onEdit={() => onEdit(operator)}
          onDelete={() => onDelete(operator)}
        />
      ),
    },
  ];
}

/**
 * The operators list. Uses virtual scrolling instead of page buttons: all rows are in one
 * scrollable list, but only the rows on screen are drawn, so it stays fast with many operators.
 */
export function OperatorsTable({ operators, loading = false, ...actions }: OperatorsTableProps) {
  // On a phone the Name and Actions columns scroll with the table: pinned, they would cover everything else.
  const isMobile = !Grid.useBreakpoint().md;
  const columns = getColumns(actions, isMobile);
  const tableWidth = columns.reduce((sum, column) => sum + Number(column.width ?? 0), 0);

  return (
    <Table<Operator>
      className="list-table operators-table"
      virtual
      rowKey="id"
      columns={columns}
      dataSource={operators}
      loading={loading}
      pagination={false}
      showSorterTooltip={false}
      scroll={{ x: tableWidth, y: TABLE_BODY_HEIGHT }}
      locale={{ emptyText: <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description="No operators found" /> }}
    />
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/operators/components/OperatorsTable.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/operators/constants.ts =====
/**
 * TODO: Check these lists against the backend before going live. The form only offers these values,
 * so a role or status the server knows but this list doesn't would be lost on save.
 */

export const OPERATOR_ROLES = ['OPERATOR', 'DELIVERY_PARTNER', 'OUTLET_MANAGER', 'ADMIN'] as const;
export type OperatorRole = (typeof OPERATOR_ROLES)[number];

export const OPERATOR_ROLE_LABELS = new Map<OperatorRole, string>([
  ['OPERATOR', 'Operator'],
  ['DELIVERY_PARTNER', 'Delivery partner'],
  ['OUTLET_MANAGER', 'Outlet manager'],
  ['ADMIN', 'Admin'],
]);

/** Live state reported by the operator's app. */
export const OPERATOR_STATUSES = ['AVAILABLE', 'BUSY', 'OFFLINE'] as const;
export type OperatorStatus = (typeof OPERATOR_STATUSES)[number];

/** Label and tag color of each status. The text is always shown too, so color is never the only signal. */
export const OPERATOR_STATUS_META = new Map<OperatorStatus, { label: string; color: string }>([
  ['AVAILABLE', { label: 'Available', color: 'success' }],
  ['BUSY', { label: 'Busy', color: 'warning' }],
  ['OFFLINE', { label: 'Offline', color: 'default' }],
]);
===== END FILE: src/features/operators/constants.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/operators/format.ts =====
import {
  OPERATOR_ROLE_LABELS,
  OPERATOR_STATUS_META,
  type OperatorRole,
  type OperatorStatus,
} from '@/features/operators/constants';
import type { OperatorAddress } from '@/features/operators/types';

export function getRoleLabel(role: OperatorRole): string {
  return OPERATOR_ROLE_LABELS.get(role) ?? role;
}

export function getStatusMeta(status: OperatorStatus): { label: string; color: string } {
  return OPERATOR_STATUS_META.get(status) ?? { label: status, color: 'default' };
}

/** Joins the parts that are filled in. Returns '' when the operator has no address. */
export function formatOperatorAddress(address: OperatorAddress): string {
  return [address.line1, address.line2, address.city, address.state, address.country, address.pincode]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(', ');
}

/** 'Afzal Ahamad' → 'AA', 'ABIS Pro Testing' → 'AP'. Shown when the operator has no picture. */
export function getInitials(name: string): string {
  const words = name.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  return words
    .slice(0, 2)
    .map((word) => word.charAt(0).toUpperCase())
    .join('');
}

/** Placeholder numbers such as "0000000000" were saved when a phone was unknown: treat them as missing. */
export function isRealPhone(phone: string | undefined): phone is string {
  return Boolean(phone && !/^0+$/.test(phone));
}

===== FILE: src/features/operators/pages/OperatorFormPage.tsx =====
import { CloseOutlined, LockOutlined, MailOutlined, PhoneOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  App,
  Avatar,
  Button,
  Col,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Rate,
  Result,
  Row,
  Select,
  Skeleton,
  Switch,
  Typography,
} from 'antd';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { LocationPicker, type Coordinates } from '@/components/ui/LocationPicker';
import { FormActions, FormSection, PageHeader } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { PictureField } from '@/components/ui/PictureField';
import { getPictureFile, type PictureFieldValue } from '@/components/ui/picture-file';
import { DEMO_MERCHANT_ID } from '@/features/operators/api/mock-operators';
import { getOperator, listOutletOptions, saveOperator } from '@/features/operators/api/operators-api';
import { operatorQueryKeys } from '@/features/operators/api/query-keys';
import { OperatorStatusTag } from '@/features/operators/components/OperatorsTable';
import { OPERATOR_ROLES } from '@/features/operators/constants';
import { getInitials, getRoleLabel } from '@/features/operators/format';
import {
  createOperatorSchema,
  editOperatorSchema,
  type OperatorFormInput,
} from '@/features/operators/schemas/operator.schema';
import type { Operator } from '@/features/operators/types';
import { toJsonText } from '@/lib/form-values';
import { showZodErrorsInForm, zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import '@/features/operators/operators.css';

type OperatorFormValues = OperatorFormInput & {
  pictureFile?: PictureFieldValue;
};

const fields = editOperatorSchema.shape;

const roleOptions = OPERATOR_ROLES.map((role) => ({ value: role, label: getRoleLabel(role) }));

const newOperatorValues: Partial<OperatorFormValues> = {
  isActive: true,
  score: 0,
  currentCycleCount: 0,
  trainedServiceIds: [],
  country: 'India',
};

/** Partial: older operators may have no role yet, and the form asks for one before saving. */
function toFormValues(operator: Operator): Partial<OperatorFormValues> {
  return {
    name: operator.name,
    about: operator.about,
    phone: operator.phone,
    email: operator.email,
    password: '',
    role: operator.role,
    department: operator.department,
    outletId: operator.outletId,
    trainedServiceIds: operator.trainedServiceIds,
    isActive: operator.isActive,
    rating: operator.rating,
    score: operator.score,
    currentCycleCount: operator.currentCycleCount,
    addressLine1: operator.address.line1,
    addressLine2: operator.address.line2,
    city: operator.address.city,
    state: operator.address.state,
    country: operator.address.country,
    pincode: operator.address.pincode,
    latitude: operator.latitude,
    longitude: operator.longitude,
    referenceId: operator.referenceId,
    otherData: toJsonText(operator.otherData),
  };
}

function isCoordinate(value: unknown): value is number {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
}

type OperatorFormViewProps = {
  /** The operator being edited. Missing when adding a new operator. */
  operator?: Operator;
};

function OperatorFormView({ operator }: OperatorFormViewProps) {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<OperatorFormValues>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const isEdit = operator !== undefined;
  const schema = isEdit ? editOperatorSchema : createOperatorSchema;

  const outletsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: operatorQueryKeys.outletOptions, queryFn: listOutletOptions });
  const outletOptions = (outletsQuery.data ?? []).map((outlet) => ({ value: outlet.id, label: outlet.name }));

  // Live preview of the name and the map point while the form is being filled in.
  const name: unknown = Form.useWatch('name', form);
  const latitude: unknown = Form.useWatch('latitude', form);
  const longitude: unknown = Form.useWatch('longitude', form);
  const position = isCoordinate(latitude) && isCoordinate(longitude) ? { lat: latitude, lng: longitude } : undefined;
  const previewName = typeof name === 'string' && name.trim() ? name : (operator?.name ?? '');

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: saveOperator,
    onSuccess: async (saved) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: operatorQueryKeys.all });
      void message.success(isEdit ? `"${saved.name}" was updated.` : `"${saved.name}" was added.`);
      void navigate('/operators');
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error(
        `The operator could not be ${isEdit ? 'updated' : 'added'}. Check that no other operator has this email, then try again.`,
      );
    },
  });

  const handlePick = ({ lat, lng }: Coordinates) => {
    form.setFieldsValue({ latitude: lat, longitude: lng });
    void form.validateFields(['latitude', 'longitude']);
  };

  const clearLocation = () => {
    form.setFieldsValue({ latitude: undefined, longitude: undefined });
    void form.validateFields(['latitude', 'longitude']);
  };

  const handleFinish = (values: OperatorFormValues) => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) {
      showZodErrorsInForm(form, result.error);
      return;
    }
    mutation.mutate({
      operatorId: operator?.id,
      payload: result.data,
      picture: getPictureFile(values.pictureFile),
    });
  };

  const goBack = () => void navigate('/operators');

  return (
    <div className="operators-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={isEdit ? 'Edit operator' : 'Add operator'}
        subtitle={
          isEdit
            ? `Change ${operator.name}'s details, sign-in and assignment.`
            : 'Add an employee or operator who can sign in to the operator app.'
        }
        backLabel="Back to operators"
        onBack={goBack}
        actions={operator && <OperatorStatusTag status={operator.status} />}
      />

      <Form<OperatorFormValues>
        form={form}
        layout="vertical"
        initialValues={operator ? toFormValues(operator) : newOperatorValues}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
        disabled={mutation.isPending}
        className="operators-page__stack"
        // Stops the browser from filling in the admin's own email and password.
        autoComplete="off"
      >
        <FormSection title="Profile" description="Who the operator is. Customers may see the name and picture.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col span={24}>
              <div className="operator-form__profile">
                <Avatar size={72} src={operator?.pictureUrl} className="operator-avatar">
                  {getInitials(previewName) || '?'}
                </Avatar>
                <PictureField currentUrl={operator?.pictureUrl} />
              </div>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Name" name="name" required rules={[zodRule(fields.name)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Full name" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Phone number" name="phone" rules={[zodRule(fields.phone)]}>
                <Input
                  placeholder="e.g. 7767863628"
                  prefix={<PhoneOutlined />}
                  inputMode="tel"
                  autoComplete="off"
                  maxLength={20}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item label="About" name="about" rules={[zodRule(fields.about)]}>
                <Input.TextArea
                  placeholder="A short note about this employee"
                  autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 5 }}
                  maxLength={1000}
                  showCount
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection
          title="Sign-in"
          description="The operator signs in to the operator app with this email and password."
        >
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Email" name="email" required rules={[zodRule(fields.email)]}>
                <Input
                  placeholder="name@abispro.com"
                  prefix={<MailOutlined />}
                  inputMode="email"
                  autoComplete="off"
                  maxLength={254}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label={isEdit ? 'New password' : 'Password'}
                name="password"
                required={!isEdit}
                extra={isEdit ? 'Leave empty to keep the current password.' : 'At least 8 characters.'}
                rules={[zodRule(schema.shape.password)]}
              >
                <Input.Password
                  placeholder={isEdit ? 'Unchanged' : 'Password'}
                  prefix={<LockOutlined />}
                  autoComplete="new-password"
                  maxLength={128}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Work" description="The operator's role, team and where they work.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Role" name="role" required rules={[zodRule(fields.role)]}>
                <Select placeholder="Select role" options={roleOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Department" name="department" rules={[zodRule(fields.department)]}>
                <Input placeholder="e.g. Delivery" maxLength={80} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Outlet"
                name="outletId"
                extra="Leave empty for an operator who works across every outlet."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.outletId)]}
              >
                <Select
                  allowClear
                  placeholder="All outlets"
                  options={outletOptions}
                  loading={outletsQuery.isPending}
                  showSearch={{ optionFilterProp: 'label' }}
                  notFoundContent={outletsQuery.isError ? 'Outlets could not be loaded' : undefined}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Trained service IDs"
                name="trainedServiceIds"
                tooltip="Type a service ID and press Enter, or separate IDs with commas."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.trainedServiceIds)]}
              >
                <Select mode="tags" placeholder="e.g. delivery, cash-collection" tokenSeparators={[',']} open={false} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item
                name="isActive"
                valuePropName="checked"
                className="operator-switch-card"
                label={
                  <span>
                    Active
                    <span className="operator-switch-card__hint">
                      Inactive operators can't sign in and get no new orders.
                    </span>
                  </span>
                }
              >
                <Switch />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Performance" description="Rating, score and the number of orders in the current cycle.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Rating"
                name="rating"
                className="operator-rating"
                extra="Click a star again to clear it."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.rating)]}
              >
                <Rate allowHalf allowClear />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Score" name="score" required rules={[zodRule(fields.score)]}>
                <InputNumber step={0.1} precision={1} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Current cycle count"
                name="currentCycleCount"
                required
                rules={[zodRule(fields.currentCycleCount)]}
              >
                <InputNumber min={0} precision={0} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Address and location" description="Where the operator lives or is based. Optional.">
          <Row gutter={24}>
            <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Address line 1" name="addressLine1" rules={[zodRule(fields.addressLine1)]}>
                <Input placeholder="House, street" maxLength={200} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Form.Item label="Address line 2" name="addressLine2" rules={[zodRule(fields.addressLine2)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Area, landmark" maxLength={200} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="City" name="city" rules={[zodRule(fields.city)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Raipur" maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="State" name="state" rules={[zodRule(fields.state)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Chhattisgarh" maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Country" name="country" rules={[zodRule(fields.country)]}>
                    <Input maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Pin code" name="pincode" rules={[zodRule(fields.pincode)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. 492009" maxLength={10} inputMode="numeric" />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Col>

            <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
              <LocationPicker
                position={position}
                onPick={handlePick}
                hint="Click the map to set the operator's exact position."
              />
              <Row gutter={16} align="bottom">
                <Col xs={24} sm={10}>
                  <Form.Item label="Latitude" name="latitude" rules={[zodRule(fields.latitude)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      placeholder="e.g. 21.2459"
                      min={-90}
                      max={90}
                      step={0.0001}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={10}>
                  <Form.Item label="Longitude" name="longitude" rules={[zodRule(fields.longitude)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      placeholder="e.g. 81.6339"
                      min={-180}
                      max={180}
                      step={0.0001}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={4}>
                  <Form.Item>
                    <Button icon={<CloseOutlined />} onClick={clearLocation} disabled={!position} block>
                      Clear
                    </Button>
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Integrations" description="IDs used to connect this operator with other systems.">
          <Row gutter={16} className="operator-form__readonly">
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Reference ID" name="referenceId" rules={[zodRule(fields.referenceId)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Reference ID" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Merchant ID" tooltip="Set automatically from your merchant account.">
                <Input value={operator?.merchantId ?? DEMO_MERCHANT_ID} disabled aria-label="Merchant ID" />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Friendly ID" tooltip="A short ID the server gives every operator.">
                <Input
                  value={operator?.friendlyId}
                  placeholder="Given after saving"
                  disabled
                  aria-label="Friendly ID"
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Other data (JSON)"
                name="otherData"
                extra='Extra settings as a JSON object, for example {"shift": "morning"}'
                rules={[zodRule(fields.otherData)]}
              >
                <Input.TextArea
                  placeholder={'{\n  "key": "value"\n}'}
                  autoSize={{ minRows: 3, maxRows: 10 }}
                  className="operators-mono"
                  spellCheck={false}
                  maxLength={5000}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormActions>
          <Typography.Text className="operator-form__actions-note">
            {isEdit ? `Editing ${operator.friendlyId}` : 'Fields marked * are required.'}
          </Typography.Text>
          <Button onClick={goBack} disabled={false}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending}>
            {isEdit ? 'Save changes' : 'Add operator'}
          </Button>
        </FormActions>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}

function EditOperatorView({ operatorId }: { operatorId: string }) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const operatorQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: operatorQueryKeys.detail(operatorId),
    queryFn: () => getOperator(operatorId),
    retry: false,
  });

  if (operatorQuery.isError) {
    return (
      <Result
        status="404"
        title="Operator not found"
        subTitle="They may have been deleted, or the link is wrong."
        extra={
          <Button type="primary" onClick={() => void navigate('/operators')}>
            Back to operators
          </Button>
        }
      />
    );
  }

  if (operatorQuery.isPending) {
    return <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 10 }} />;
  }

  return <OperatorFormView operator={operatorQuery.data} />;
}

/** Add operator (/operators/new) and edit operator (/operators/:operatorId/edit). */
export default function OperatorFormPage() {
  const { operatorId } = useParams();
  return operatorId === undefined ? <OperatorFormView /> : <EditOperatorView operatorId={operatorId} />;
}
===== END FILE: src/features/operators/pages/OperatorFormPage.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/operators/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx =====
import { PlusOutlined, SearchOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Input, Select } from 'antd';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ListPanel } from '@/components/ui/ListPanel';
import { LoadError } from '@/components/ui/LoadError';
import { deleteOperator, listOperators } from '@/features/operators/api/operators-api';
import { operatorQueryKeys } from '@/features/operators/api/query-keys';
import { OperatorsTable } from '@/features/operators/components/OperatorsTable';
import { OPERATOR_STATUSES, type OperatorStatus } from '@/features/operators/constants';
import { getStatusMeta } from '@/features/operators/format';
import type { Operator } from '@/features/operators/types';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';
import '@/features/operators/operators.css';

type ActiveFilter = 'all' | 'active' | 'inactive';
type StatusFilter = 'all' | OperatorStatus;

const activeOptions: { value: ActiveFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Active and inactive' },
  { value: 'active', label: 'Active only' },
  { value: 'inactive', label: 'Inactive only' },
];

const statusOptions: { value: StatusFilter; label: string }[] = [
  { value: 'all', label: 'Any status' },
  ...OPERATOR_STATUSES.map((status) => ({ value: status, label: getStatusMeta(status).label })),
];

function matchesSearch(operator: Operator, search: string): boolean {
  if (!search) return true;
  return `${operator.name} ${operator.email} ${operator.phone ?? ''} ${operator.friendlyId}`
    .toLowerCase()
    .includes(search);
}

function matchesActive(operator: Operator, filter: ActiveFilter): boolean {
  if (filter === 'all') return true;
  return filter === 'active' ? operator.isActive : !operator.isActive;
}

export default function OperatorsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState<ActiveFilter>('all');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<StatusFilter>('all');
  const normalizedSearch = search.trim().toLowerCase();

  const operatorsQuery = useQuery({ queryKey: operatorQueryKeys.all, queryFn: listOperators });

  const deleteMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (operator: Operator) => deleteOperator(operator.id),
    onSuccess: async (_, operator) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: operatorQueryKeys.all });
      void message.success(`"${operator.name}" was deleted.`);
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The operator could not be deleted. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  const operators = operatorsQuery.data ?? [];
  const visibleOperators = operators.filter(
    (operator) =>
      matchesSearch(operator, normalizedSearch) &&
      matchesActive(operator, activeFilter) &&
      (statusFilter === 'all' || operator.status === statusFilter),
  );

  return (
    <div className="operators-page">
      <ListPanel
        title="All operators"
        count={operatorsQuery.data ? operators.length : undefined}
        subtitle="Employees and operators who handle orders and deliveries."
        toolbar={
          <>
            <Input
              allowClear
              placeholder="Search name, email, phone or ID"
              prefix={<SearchOutlined />}
              value={search}
              onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
              aria-label="Search operators"
            />
            <Select<StatusFilter>
              className="operators-toolbar__filter"
              value={statusFilter}
              options={statusOptions}
              onChange={setStatusFilter}
              aria-label="Filter by status"
            />
            <Select<ActiveFilter>
              className="operators-toolbar__filter"
              value={activeFilter}
              options={activeOptions}
              onChange={setActiveFilter}
              aria-label="Filter by active"
              popupMatchSelectWidth={false}
            />
            <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => void navigate('/operators/new')}>
              Add operator
            </Button>
          </>
        }
        footer={
          operatorsQuery.data &&
          `Showing ${formatCount(visibleOperators.length)} of ${formatCount(operators.length)} operators · Sample data`
        }
      >
        {operatorsQuery.isError ? (
          <LoadError what="operators" onRetry={() => void operatorsQuery.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <OperatorsTable
            operators={visibleOperators}
            loading={operatorsQuery.isPending}
            onEdit={(operator) => void navigate(`/operators/${encodeURIComponent(operator.id)}/edit`)}
            onDelete={(operator) => deleteMutation.mutate(operator)}
          />
        )}
      </ListPanel>
    </div>
  );
}
===== END FILE: src/features/operators/pages/OperatorsPage.tsx =====

===== FILE: src/features/operators/schemas/operator.schema.ts =====
import { z } from 'zod';
import { OPERATOR_ROLES } from '@/features/operators/constants';
import { optionalJsonObject, optionalText, requiredText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

/**
 * Add/edit operator form rules. The form shows these messages field by field (see `zodRule`),
 * and the same schema turns the form values into the clean payload sent to the API.
 */

const PHONE_PATTERN = /^\+?[0-9][0-9 -]{5,17}$/;
const PINCODE_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9 -]{2,9}$/;
const PASSWORD_MIN = 8;
const PASSWORD_MAX = 128;

/** Passwords are not trimmed: a space can be part of a password. */
const newPassword = z
  .string({ error: 'Enter a password' })
  .min(PASSWORD_MIN, `Use at least ${PASSWORD_MIN} characters`)
  .max(PASSWORD_MAX, `Use ${PASSWORD_MAX} characters or fewer`);

const fields = {
  // Profile
  name: requiredText('Enter the name', 100).pipe(z.string().min(2, 'Use at least 2 characters')),
  about: optionalText(1000),
  phone: optionalText(20).refine(
    (value) => !value || PHONE_PATTERN.test(value),
    'Phone numbers can contain only digits, spaces, + and -',
  ),

  // Account
  email: z
    .string({ error: 'Enter the email address' })
    .trim()
    .toLowerCase()
    .min(1, 'Enter the email address')
    .max(254, 'Use 254 characters or fewer')
    .pipe(z.email('Enter a valid email address')),

  // Work
  role: z.enum(OPERATOR_ROLES, { error: 'Choose a role' }),
  department: optionalText(80),
  outletId: optionalText(64),
  trainedServiceIds: z
    .array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(64, 'Service IDs must be 64 characters or fewer'))
    .max(100, 'Add 100 services or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? []),
  isActive: z.boolean().default(true),

  // Performance
  rating: z
    .number()
    .min(0, 'Use 0 to 5')
    .max(5, 'Use 0 to 5')
    .nullish()
    // The star picker gives 0 when cleared: that means "not rated".
    .transform((value) => value || undefined),
  score: z
    .number({ error: 'Enter the score' })
    .min(-1_000_000, 'Use -1,000,000 or more')
    .max(1_000_000, 'Use 1,000,000 or less'),
  currentCycleCount: z
    .number({ error: 'Enter the cycle count' })
    .int('Use a whole number')
    .min(0, 'Use 0 or more')
    .max(1_000_000, 'Use 1,000,000 or less'),

  // Address (all optional: many operators have none on file)
  addressLine1: optionalText(200),
  addressLine2: optionalText(200),
  city: optionalText(80),
  state: optionalText(80),
  country: optionalText(80),
  pincode: optionalText(10).refine((value) => !value || PINCODE_PATTERN.test(value), 'Enter a valid pin code'),
  latitude: z
    .number()
    .min(-90, 'Latitude must be between -90 and 90')
    .max(90, 'Latitude must be between -90 and 90')
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? undefined),
  longitude: z
    .number()
    .min(-180, 'Longitude must be between -180 and 180')
    .max(180, 'Longitude must be between -180 and 180')
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? undefined),

  // Integrations
  referenceId: optionalText(100),
  otherData: optionalJsonObject(),
};

type LocationValues = { latitude?: number; longitude?: number };

/** A location needs both numbers: one without the other is not a point on the map. */
function checkLocation(values: LocationValues, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) {
  if (values.latitude === undefined && values.longitude !== undefined) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['latitude'], message: 'Enter the latitude too, or clear the longitude' });
  }
  if (values.longitude === undefined && values.latitude !== undefined) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['longitude'], message: 'Enter the longitude too, or clear the latitude' });
  }
}

/** Adding an operator: the password is required. */
export const createOperatorSchema = z.object({ ...fields, password: newPassword }).superRefine(checkLocation);

/** Editing an operator: an empty password keeps the current one. */
export const editOperatorSchema = z
  .object({
    ...fields,
    password: z
      .union([z.literal(''), newPassword])
      .nullish()
      .transform((value) => value || undefined),
  })
  .superRefine(checkLocation);

/** What the form holds while the user is typing. */
export type OperatorFormInput = z.input<typeof editOperatorSchema>;

/** The clean, validated payload for the API. `password` is only there when it should change. */
export type OperatorPayload = z.output<typeof editOperatorSchema>;
===== END FILE: src/features/operators/schemas/operator.schema.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/operators/types.ts =====
import type { OperatorRole, OperatorStatus } from '@/features/operators/constants';

export type OperatorId = string;

export type OperatorAddress = {
  line1?: string;
  line2?: string;
  city?: string;
  state?: string;
  country?: string;
  pincode?: string;
};

/** An employee or operator, as the API sends it. The password is never sent back. */
export type Operator = {
  id: OperatorId;
  /** Short readable id given by the server, for example "OP-1042". */
  friendlyId: string;
  merchantId: string;
  name: string;
  about?: string;
  email: string;
  role?: OperatorRole;
  department?: string;
  /** Missing for operators who work across every outlet. */
  outletId?: string;
  phone?: string;
  /** 1 to 5. Missing until someone rates the operator. */
  rating?: number;
  score: number;
  currentCycleCount: number;
  trainedServiceIds: string[];
  otherData?: Record<string, unknown>;
  isActive: boolean;
  /** Set by the operator's app, not by this form. */
  status: OperatorStatus;
  pictureUrl?: string;
  address: OperatorAddress;
  latitude?: number;
  longitude?: number;
  referenceId?: string;
};

/** An outlet the operator can be assigned to. */
export type OutletOption = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
};

===== FILE: src/features/outlets/pages/CreateOutletPage.tsx =====
import { ArrowLeftOutlined, UploadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  App,
  Button,
  Card,
  Col,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Row,
  Select,
  Typography,
  Upload,
  type UploadFile,
  type UploadProps,
} from 'antd';
import type { PropsWithChildren } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { createOutlet } from '@/features/outlets/api/create-outlet';
import { DEMO_MERCHANT_ID } from '@/features/outlets/api/mock-outlets';
import { LocationPicker, type Coordinates } from '@/components/ui/LocationPicker';
import { outletSchema, type OutletFormInput } from '@/features/outlets/schemas/outlet.schema';
import { zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import './CreateOutletPage.css';

type OutletFormValues = OutletFormInput & {
  pictureFile?: UploadFile[];
};

const fields = outletSchema.shape;

const ACCEPTED_IMAGE_TYPES = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'];
const MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB = 2;

const initialValues: Partial<OutletFormValues> = {
  country: 'India',
  merchantId: DEMO_MERCHANT_ID,
  servingAreas: [],
  phoneNumbers: [],
};

function FormSection({ title, description, children }: PropsWithChildren<{ title: string; description: string }>) {
  return (
    <Card className="outlet-form__section" variant="borderless">
      <div className="outlet-form__section-head">
        <Typography.Title level={5} className="outlet-form__section-title">
          {title}
        </Typography.Title>
        <Typography.Text className="outlet-form__section-description">{description}</Typography.Text>
      </div>
      {children}
    </Card>
  );
}

function isCoordinate(value: unknown): value is number {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
}

export default function CreateOutletPage() {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<OutletFormValues>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { message } = App.useApp();

  const latitude: unknown = Form.useWatch('latitude', form);
  const longitude: unknown = Form.useWatch('longitude', form);
  const position = isCoordinate(latitude) && isCoordinate(longitude) ? { lat: latitude, lng: longitude } : undefined;

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: createOutlet,
    onSuccess: (outlet) => {
      void message.success(`"${outlet.name}" was created.`);
      void navigate('/outlets');
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error('The outlet could not be created. Please try again.');
    },
  });

  const handlePick = ({ lat, lng }: Coordinates) => {
    form.setFieldsValue({ latitude: lat, longitude: lng });
    void form.validateFields(['latitude', 'longitude']);
  };

  const beforePictureUpload: UploadProps['beforeUpload'] = (file) => {
    if (!ACCEPTED_IMAGE_TYPES.includes(file.type)) {
      void message.error('Use a JPG, PNG or WebP image.');
      return Upload.LIST_IGNORE;
    }
    if (file.size > MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB * 1024 * 1024) {
      void message.error(`The image must be ${MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB} MB or smaller.`);
      return Upload.LIST_IGNORE;
    }
    // Keep the file in the form; it is sent together with the outlet on submit.
    return false;
  };

  const handleFinish = (values: OutletFormValues) => {
    const outlet = outletSchema.parse(values);
    const picture = values.pictureFile?.[0]?.originFileObj;
    mutation.mutate({ outlet, picture });
  };

  const goBack = () => void navigate('/outlets');

  return (
    <div className="outlet-form-page">
      <div className="outlet-form-page__head">
        <Button type="text" icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={goBack} aria-label="Back to outlets" />
        <div>
          <Typography.Title level={4} className="outlet-form-page__title">
            Create outlet
          </Typography.Title>
          <Typography.Text className="outlet-form-page__subtitle">
            Add a new outlet with its address, delivery area and contact details.
          </Typography.Text>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Form<OutletFormValues>
        form={form}
        layout="vertical"
        initialValues={initialValues}
        onFinish={handleFinish}
        scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
        disabled={mutation.isPending}
        className="outlet-form"
      >
        <FormSection title="Basic details" description="How the outlet is named and listed.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Name" name="name" required rules={[zodRule(fields.name)]}>
                <Input placeholder="e.g. Shivanand Nagar Raipur" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Category" name="category" rules={[zodRule(fields.category)]}>
                <Input placeholder="e.g. Restaurant" maxLength={60} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item label="Description" name="description" rules={[zodRule(fields.description)]}>
                <Input.TextArea
                  placeholder="A short description shown to customers"
                  autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 5 }}
                  maxLength={500}
                  showCount
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Listing order"
                name="listingOrder"
                tooltip="Position of this outlet in lists. Lower numbers appear first."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.listingOrder)]}
              >
                <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 1" min={0} max={9999} precision={0} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Merchant ID"
                name="merchantId"
                tooltip="Set automatically from your merchant account."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.merchantId)]}
              >
                <Input readOnly disabled className="outlet-form__mono" />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Address and location" description="Where the outlet is, and its exact point on the map.">
          <Row gutter={24}>
            <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Address line 1" name="addressLine1" required rules={[zodRule(fields.addressLine1)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Building, street" maxLength={200} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Form.Item label="Address line 2" name="addressLine2" rules={[zodRule(fields.addressLine2)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Area, landmark (optional)" maxLength={200} />
              </Form.Item>
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="City" name="city" required rules={[zodRule(fields.city)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Raipur" maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="State" name="state" required rules={[zodRule(fields.state)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. Chhattisgarh" maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Country" name="country" required rules={[zodRule(fields.country)]}>
                    <Input maxLength={80} />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Pin code" name="pincode" required rules={[zodRule(fields.pincode)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="e.g. 492001" maxLength={10} inputMode="numeric" />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Col>

            <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
              <LocationPicker
                position={position}
                onPick={handlePick}
                hint="Click the map to set the outlet's exact position."
              />
              <Row gutter={16}>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Latitude" name="latitude" required rules={[zodRule(fields.latitude)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      placeholder="e.g. 21.2514"
                      min={-90}
                      max={90}
                      step={0.0001}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
                <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
                  <Form.Item label="Longitude" name="longitude" required rules={[zodRule(fields.longitude)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      placeholder="e.g. 81.6296"
                      min={-180}
                      max={180}
                      step={0.0001}
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                </Col>
              </Row>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Delivery and contact" description="Where the outlet delivers and how customers reach it.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Delivery radius"
                name="deliveryRadiusKm"
                required
                rules={[zodRule(fields.deliveryRadiusKm)]}
              >
                <InputNumber placeholder="e.g. 5" min={0} max={100} step={0.5} suffix="km" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Phone numbers"
                name="phoneNumbers"
                required
                tooltip="Type a number and press Enter. Add up to 5."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.phoneNumbers)]}
              >
                <Select mode="tags" placeholder="Type a number and press Enter" tokenSeparators={[',']} open={false} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Serving areas"
                name="servingAreas"
                tooltip="Type an area name and press Enter, or separate areas with commas."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.servingAreas)]}
              >
                <Select mode="tags" placeholder="e.g. Shankar Nagar, Telibandha" tokenSeparators={[',']} open={false} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Picture" description="Paste an image link or upload an image from your computer.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item label="Image link" name="pictureUrl" rules={[zodRule(fields.pictureUrl)]}>
                <Input placeholder="https://yourwebsite.com/image.jpg" inputMode="url" allowClear />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Or upload an image"
                name="pictureFile"
                valuePropName="fileList"
                getValueFromEvent={(event: { fileList: UploadFile[] }) => event.fileList}
                extra={`JPG, PNG or WebP, up to ${MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB} MB.`}
              >
                <Upload
                  accept={ACCEPTED_IMAGE_TYPES.join(',')}
                  beforeUpload={beforePictureUpload}
                  listType="picture"
                  maxCount={1}
                >
                  <Button icon={<UploadOutlined />}>Choose image</Button>
                </Upload>
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <FormSection title="Integrations" description="IDs used to connect this outlet with other systems.">
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Reference ID" name="referenceId" rules={[zodRule(fields.referenceId)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Reference ID" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item label="Vendor ID" name="vendorId" rules={[zodRule(fields.vendorId)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Vendor ID" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Accounting vendor ID"
                name="accountingVendorId"
                rules={[zodRule(fields.accountingVendorId)]}
              >
                <Input placeholder="Accounting vendor ID" maxLength={100} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={24}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Other data"
                name="otherData"
                tooltip="Extra settings as a JSON object."
                rules={[zodRule(fields.otherData)]}
              >
                <Input.TextArea
                  placeholder={'{\n  "key": "value"\n}'}
                  autoSize={{ minRows: 3, maxRows: 10 }}
                  className="outlet-form__mono"
                  spellCheck={false}
                />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </FormSection>

        <div className="outlet-form__actions">
          <Button onClick={goBack} disabled={false}>
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending}>
            Create outlet
          </Button>
        </div>
      </Form>
    </div>
  );
}

const FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS = 400;

/** Waits a little, like a real request would. */
export function fakeNetworkDelay(): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => window.setTimeout(resolve, FAKE_NETWORK_DELAY_MS));
}

    displayName: `${label(hour)} to ${label(hour + 2)}`,

    end: `${String(hour + 2).padStart(2, '0')}:00`,

import type { SettingsTabKey } from '@/features/settings/constants';

/**
 * Names of the cached server data. Used when loading data and when refreshing it after a change,
 * so a typo in one place can't leave a tab out of date.
 */
export const settingsQueryKeys = {
  all: ['settings'] as const,
  section: (section: SettingsTabKey) => ['settings', section] as const,
};

===== FILE: src/features/settings/api/settings-api.ts =====
import { fakeNetworkDelay, mockSettings } from '@/features/settings/api/mock-settings';
import type { SettingsTabKey } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import type { SettingsMap } from '@/features/settings/types';

/**
 * SAMPLE: these functions work on the sample data in `mock-settings.ts` so every tab can be tried.
 * Each one has a TODO showing the real call. Every tab is loaded and saved on its own,
 * so saving one tab never overwrites another.
 */

/**
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.get<SettingsMap[K]>(`/settings/${section}`);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function getSettings<K extends SettingsTabKey>(section: K): Promise<SettingsMap[K]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  const value = mockSettings.get(section);
  if (!value) throw new Error('Settings not found');
  return structuredClone(value) as SettingsMap[K];
}

export type SaveSettingsInput<K extends SettingsTabKey> = {
  section: K;
  value: SettingsMap[K];
};

/**
 * The server must check every value again, and allow this to admins only:
 * these settings change prices and sign-in for every customer.
 *
 * TODO: const { data } = await apiClient.put<SettingsMap[K]>(`/settings/${section}`, value);
 *       return data;
 */
export async function saveSettings<K extends SettingsTabKey>({
  section,
  value,
}: SaveSettingsInput<K>): Promise<SettingsMap[K]> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
  mockSettings.set(section, structuredClone(value));
  return structuredClone(value);
}

/**
 * Clears the booked-order counts of every delivery date slot, so all slots are open again.
 *
 * TODO: await apiClient.post('/settings/delivery/reset-slots');
 */
export async function resetDeliveryDateSlots(): Promise<void> {
  await fakeNetworkDelay();
}
===== END FILE: src/features/settings/api/settings-api.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/settings/components/BillFieldPicker.tsx =====
import { Radio, type RadioGroupProps } from 'antd';
import { BILL_AMOUNT_FIELD_META, BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import '@/features/settings/settings.css';

/**
 * Which total of the bill a rule is checked against, as three cards that each say what they mean.
 * Use it inside a Form.Item.
 */
export function BillFieldPicker(props: Omit<RadioGroupProps, 'options' | 'children'>) {
  return (
    <Radio.Group className="settings-choice-cards" {...props}>
      {BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS.map((field) => {
        const meta = BILL_AMOUNT_FIELD_META.get(field);
        return (
          <Radio key={field} value={field}>
            <span className="settings-choice-card__title">{meta?.label ?? field}</span>
            <span className="settings-choice-card__hint">{meta?.hint}</span>
          </Radio>
        );
      })}
    </Radio.Group>
  );
}

import { Col, Form, InputNumber, Row } from 'antd';

function RuleFields({ who }: { who: 'customer' | 'employee' }) {

      <Col xs={24} md={8}>

}

const toFormValues = (value: BillingSettings) => value;

/** Billing tab: the smallest order customers, and employees on their behalf, can place. */
export function BillingTab({ onDirtyChange }: TabProps) {

          hint="Employees can't place an order below this amount."
        >
          <RuleFields who="employee" />
        </SwitchCard>

  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/settings/components/DeliveryTab.tsx =====
import { DeleteOutlined, PlusOutlined, ReloadOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import {
  App,
  Button,
  Col,
  DatePicker,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Popconfirm,
  Radio,
  Row,
  Select,
  TimePicker,
  Tooltip,
  Typography,
} from 'antd';
import dayjs, { type Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { ChipGroup } from '@/components/ui/ChipGroup';
import { FormSection } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { SwitchCard } from '@/components/ui/SwitchCard';
import { resetDeliveryDateSlots } from '@/features/settings/api/settings-api';
import { BillFieldPicker } from '@/features/settings/components/BillFieldPicker';
import { SettingsForm } from '@/features/settings/components/SettingsForm';
import { DELIVERY_DATE_OFFSETS, getDeliveryDateLabel } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import { deliveryFields, deliverySchema, type DeliveryFormInput } from '@/features/settings/schemas/delivery.schema';
import { toTimeValue } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import type { DeliverySettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';

type TabProps = { onDirtyChange: (section: 'delivery', dirty: boolean) => void };

const TIME_FORMAT = 'h:mm A';

const dateOffsetOptions = DELIVERY_DATE_OFFSETS.map((value) => ({ value, label: getDeliveryDateLabel(value) }));

function timeValue(time: string): Dayjs {
  return toTimeValue(time) ?? dayjs().startOf('day');
}

function toFormValues(value: DeliverySettings): DeliveryFormInput {
  return {
    ...value,
    cutOffTime: toTimeValue(value.cutOffTime),
    liveOrderCutOffTime: toTimeValue(value.liveOrderCutOffTime),
    slots: value.slots.map((slot) => ({
      displayName: slot.displayName,
      time: [timeValue(slot.start), timeValue(slot.end)],
      maxOrders: slot.maxOrders,
      leadTimeMinutes: slot.leadTimeMinutes,
    })),
    nonDeliveryDates: value.nonDeliveryDates.map((date) => dayjs(date)),
  };
}

/** The delivery charge: one fixed amount, or an amount by distance. */
function ChargeFields() {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<DeliveryFormInput>();
  const mode = Form.useWatch('chargeMode', form);

  return (
    <>
      <Form.Item label="How is it charged?" name="chargeMode" required>
        <Radio.Group className="settings-choice-cards">
          <Radio value="FIXED">
            <span className="settings-choice-card__title">Fixed delivery charge</span>
            <span className="settings-choice-card__hint">The same charge for every order.</span>
          </Radio>
          <Radio value="KM">
            <span className="settings-choice-card__title">KM based delivery charge</span>
            <span className="settings-choice-card__hint">The charge depends on the distance to the customer.</span>
          </Radio>
        </Radio.Group>
      </Form.Item>

      {mode === 'KM' ? (
        <>
          <Typography.Text className="settings-subhead">Distance bands</Typography.Text>
          <Form.List name="distanceBands">
            {(rows, { add, remove }, { errors }) => (
              <>
                <div className="edit-list">
                  <div className="edit-list__row edit-list__head band-row">
                    <span>Up to (km)</span>
                    <span>Charge</span>
                    <span />
                  </div>
                  {rows.length === 0 && <div className="edit-list__empty">No bands yet. Add the first one.</div>}
                  {rows.map((row) => (
                    <div className="edit-list__row band-row" key={row.key}>
                      <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'upToKm']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.bandUpToKm)]}>
                        <InputNumber min={0} step={0.5} suffix="km" aria-label="Up to km" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                      </Form.Item>
                      <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'charge']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.bandCharge)]}>
                        <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" aria-label="Charge" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                      </Form.Item>
                      <Tooltip title="Remove band">
                        <Button
                          type="text"
                          danger
                          icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
                          onClick={() => remove(row.name)}
                          aria-label="Remove band"
                        />
                      </Tooltip>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <Form.ErrorList errors={errors} />
                <Button className="edit-list__add" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => add({ charge: 0 })}>
                  Add distance band
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
          </Form.List>
          <Row gutter={16} className="settings-after-list">
            <Col xs={24} md={8}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Max delivery distance"
                name="maxDistanceKm"
                extra="No delivery beyond this distance. Leave empty to use the farthest band."
                rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.maxDistanceKm)]}
              >
                <InputNumber min={0} step={0.5} suffix="km" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </>
      ) : (
        <Row gutter={16}>
          <Col xs={24} md={8}>
            <Form.Item
              label="Delivery charge"
              name="fixedCharge"
              required
              extra="Added to the bill automatically."
              rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.fixedCharge)]}
            >
              <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
            </Form.Item>
          </Col>
        </Row>
      )}

      <Row gutter={16}>
        <Col xs={24} md={8}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Tax on delivery charge"
            name="taxPercent"
            required
            extra="Worked out and added to the bill automatically."
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.taxPercent)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={0} max={100} suffix="%" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} md={16}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Delivery item ID"
            name="deliveryItemId"
            extra='Only if the delivery charge is a separate item on your bill or POS: the 24-character ID of a "Delivery Charge" item in the catalog.'
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.deliveryItemId)]}
          >
            <Input
              placeholder="e.g. 6a96be40a785dc8cc1535eac"
              maxLength={24}
              className="settings-mono"
              spellCheck={false}
            />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
      </Row>

      <Typography.Text className="settings-subhead">Free delivery</Typography.Text>
      <Row gutter={16}>
        <Col xs={24} md={8}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Delivery charge exemption limit"
            name="exemptionLimit"
            required
            extra="Orders above this amount get free delivery. Use 0 to always charge delivery."
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.exemptionLimit)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} md={16}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Check it against"
            name="exemptionField"
            required
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.exemptionField)]}
          >
            <BillFieldPicker />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
      </Row>
    </>
  );
}

/** The time slots customers choose for delivery. */
function SlotFields() {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<DeliveryFormInput>();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const slots = Form.useWatch('slots', form);

  const slotOptions = (slots ?? [])
    .map((slot) => slot?.displayName?.trim())
    .filter((name): name is string => Boolean(name))
    .map((name) => ({ value: name, label: name }));

  const resetMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: resetDeliveryDateSlots,
    onSuccess: () => void message.success('Every delivery date slot was reset.'),
    onError: () => void message.error('The slots could not be reset. Please try again.'),
  });

  /** A new slot starts where the last one ends, and lasts 2 hours. */
  const nextSlot = () => {
    const last = slots?.at(-1)?.time?.[1];
    const start = dayjs.isDayjs(last) ? last : dayjs().hour(9).minute(0).second(0);
    return { time: [start, start.add(2, 'hour')], maxOrders: 100, leadTimeMinutes: 15 };
  };

  return (
    <>
      <Form.Item
        label="Delivery days customers can choose"
        name="deliveryDateOffsets"
        required
        extra="Today, tomorrow, or up to 7 days ahead."
      >
        <ChipGroup options={dateOffsetOptions} />
      </Form.Item>

      <Row gutter={16} align="bottom">
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} md={6}>
          <Form.Item label="Cut-off time" name="cutOffTime" rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.cutOffTime)]}>
            <TimePicker format={TIME_FORMAT} minuteStep={5} needConfirm={false} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} md={8}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Cut-off slot"
            name="cutOffSlot"
            tooltip="The slot the cut-off time applies to."
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.cutOffSlot)]}
          >
            <Select allowClear placeholder="Select cut-off slot" options={slotOptions} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} md={10}>
          <Form.Item label="Reset all delivery date slots">
            <Popconfirm
              title="Reset every delivery date slot?"
              description="All slots open again for new orders. Orders already placed are not changed."
              okText="Reset all"
              cancelText="Cancel"
              onConfirm={() => resetMutation.mutate()}
            >
              <Button icon={<ReloadOutlined />} loading={resetMutation.isPending}>
                Reset all
              </Button>
            </Popconfirm>
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
      </Row>

      <Typography.Text className="settings-subhead">Time slots</Typography.Text>
      <Form.List name="slots">
        {(rows, { add, remove }, { errors }) => (
          <>
            <div className="edit-list">
              <div className="edit-list__row edit-list__head slot-row">
                <span>Display name</span>
                <span>Time</span>
                <span>Max orders</span>
                <span>Lead time</span>
                <span />
              </div>
              {rows.length === 0 && <div className="edit-list__empty">No slots yet. Add the first one.</div>}
              {rows.map((row) => (
                <div className="edit-list__row slot-row" key={row.key}>
                  <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'displayName']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.slotName)]}>
                    <Input placeholder="Filled in from the time" maxLength={40} aria-label="Display name" />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'time']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.slotTime)]}>
                    <TimePicker.RangePicker
                      format={TIME_FORMAT}
                      minuteStep={15}
                      needConfirm={false}
                      order={false}
                      aria-label="Slot time"
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'maxOrders']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.slotMaxOrders)]}>
                    <InputNumber min={1} precision={0} aria-label="Max orders" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'leadTimeMinutes']} rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.slotLeadTime)]}>
                    <InputNumber
                      min={0}
                      precision={0}
                      suffix="min"
                      aria-label="Lead time in minutes"
                      style={{ width: '100%' }}
                    />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Tooltip title="Remove slot">
                    <Button
                      type="text"
                      danger
                      icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
                      onClick={() => remove(row.name)}
                      aria-label="Remove slot"
                    />
                  </Tooltip>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <Form.ErrorList errors={errors} />
            <Button className="edit-list__add" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => add(nextSlot())}>
              Add slot
            </Button>
          </>
        )}
      </Form.List>
      <Typography.Paragraph className="settings-muted settings-list-note">
        Lead time is how many minutes before a slot starts it stops taking orders. Empty names are filled in from the
        time, for example "9AM to 11AM".
      </Typography.Paragraph>
    </>
  );
}

          <SlotFields />

            <DatePicker
              multiple
              format="DD MMM YYYY"
              placeholder="Pick dates"
              maxTagCount="responsive"
              disabledDate={(date) => date.isBefore(dayjs(), 'day')}
              style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 480 }}
            />
          </Form.Item>
        </SwitchCard>
        <SwitchCard
          name="liveOrderCutOffEnabled"
          title="Enable live order cut off"
          hint="Stop taking live orders every day after a certain time."
        >
          <Form.Item
            label="Stop live orders after"
            name="liveOrderCutOffTime"
            required
            rules={[zodRule(deliveryFields.liveOrderCutOffTime)]}
          >
            <TimePicker format={TIME_FORMAT} minuteStep={5} needConfirm={false} style={{ width: 200 }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </SwitchCard>
      </FormSection>
    </SettingsForm>
  );
}
