===== FILE: src/features/settings/components/EarningPointsTab.tsx =====
import { CrownOutlined, DeleteOutlined, PlusOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';
import {
  Button,
  Card,
  Col,
  Collapse,
  DatePicker,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Popconfirm,
  Row,
  Tag,
  Typography,
} from 'antd';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { FormSection } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { SwitchCard } from '@/components/ui/SwitchCard';
import { SettingsForm } from '@/features/settings/components/SettingsForm';
import { DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_DAYS } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import {
  earningPointsFields as fields,
  earningPointsSchema,
  type EarningPointsFormInput,
} from '@/features/settings/schemas/earning-points.schema';
import { toDateValue } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import type { EarningPointsSettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import { formatCount } from '@/utils/number-format';

type TabProps = { onDirtyChange: (section: 'earning-points', dirty: boolean) => void };

function toFormValues(value: EarningPointsSettings): EarningPointsFormInput {
  return {
    ...value,
    openingPointsDate: toDateValue(value.openingPointsDate),
    openingWalletDate: toDateValue(value.openingWalletDate),
    tiers: value.tiers.map((tier) => ({ ...tier, benefits: tier.benefits.join('\n') })),
  };
}

/** 62 → '1 hour 2 minutes', 525600 → '365 days'. */
function describeMinutes(total: number): string {
  const days = Math.floor(total / 1440);
  const hours = Math.floor((total % 1440) / 60);
  const minutes = total % 60;
  const parts = [
    days ? `${formatCount(days)} ${days === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}` : '',
    hours ? `${hours} ${hours === 1 ? 'hour' : 'hours'}` : '',
    minutes ? `${minutes} ${minutes === 1 ? 'minute' : 'minutes'}` : '',
  ].filter(Boolean);
  return parts.join(' ') || '0 minutes';
}

function ExpiryHint() {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<EarningPointsFormInput>();
  const minutes: unknown = Form.useWatch('expiryMinutes', form);
  const readable = typeof minutes === 'number' && minutes > 0 ? ` That is ${describeMinutes(minutes)}.` : '';
  return <>Earned points expire after this many minutes. The wallet needs at least 61.{readable}</>;
}

/** The three app texts of a perk: the button, the confirmation and the line in the cart. */
function PerkTexts({ base }: { base: (string | number)[] }) {
  return (
    <>
      <Form.Item label="Text 1 (apply button)" name={[...base, 'applyText']} rules={[zodRule(fields.appText)]}>
        <Input placeholder="e.g. Avail 5% off" maxLength={60} />
      </Form.Item>
      <Form.Item label="Text 2 (confirmation)" name={[...base, 'confirmationText']} rules={[zodRule(fields.appText)]}>
        <Input placeholder="e.g. You get 5% off" maxLength={60} />
      </Form.Item>
      <Form.Item label="Text 3 (cart view)" name={[...base, 'cartText']} rules={[zodRule(fields.appText)]}>
        <Input placeholder="e.g. 5% off applied" maxLength={60} />
      </Form.Item>
    </>
  );
}

function PercentPerk({ index, perk, title }: { index: number; perk: 'discount' | 'cashback'; title: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className="tier-perk" size="small" variant="borderless" title={title}>
      <Row gutter={12}>
        <Col span={12}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Percentage"
            name={[index, perk, 'percent']}
            extra="Empty = off"
            rules={[zodRule(fields.percent)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={0} max={100} suffix="%" placeholder="e.g. 5" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col span={12}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Cooldown"
            name={[index, perk, 'cooldownDays']}
            extra={`Empty = ${DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_DAYS} days`}
            rules={[zodRule(fields.cooldownDays)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={0} precision={0} suffix="days" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
      </Row>
      <PerkTexts base={[index, perk]} />
    </Card>
  );
}

function TierFields({ index }: { index: number }) {
  return (
    <>
      <Row gutter={16}>
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
          <Form.Item label="Name" name={[index, 'name']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.tierName)]}>
            <Input placeholder="e.g. Gold" maxLength={40} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
          <Form.Item label="Min points" name={[index, 'minPoints']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.tierMinPoints)]}>
            <InputNumber min={0} precision={0} style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Earn multiplier"
            name={[index, 'earnMultiplier']}
            required
            extra="Birthday month only"
            rules={[zodRule(fields.earnMultiplier)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={1} max={10} step={0.1} suffix="×" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} sm={12} lg={6}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Free delivery above"
            name={[index, 'freeDeliveryMin']}
            extra="Empty or 0 = off"
            rules={[zodRule(fields.freeDeliveryMin)]}
          >
            <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" placeholder="e.g. 299" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
        <Col span={24}>
          <Form.Item
            label="Benefits list"
            name={[index, 'benefits']}
            extra="One benefit per line. Shown to customers in the app."
            rules={[zodRule(fields.benefits)]}
          >
            <Input.TextArea
              autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 6 }}
              placeholder={'e.g. 5% OFF once a week\n1.5x points in birthday month'}
            />
          </Form.Item>
        </Col>
      </Row>

      <div className="tier-perks">
        <PercentPerk index={index} perk="discount" title="Discount" />
        <PercentPerk index={index} perk="cashback" title="Wallet cashback" />
        <Card className="tier-perk" size="small" variant="borderless" title="Free item (every N orders)">
          <Row gutter={12}>
            <Col span={15}>
              <Form.Item label="Item ID" name={[index, 'freeItem', 'itemId']} rules={[zodRule(fields.freeItemId)]}>
                <Input placeholder="Catalog item ID" maxLength={24} className="settings-mono" spellCheck={false} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col span={9}>
              <Form.Item label="Every N" name={[index, 'freeItem', 'everyN']} rules={[zodRule(fields.everyN)]}>
                <InputNumber min={1} precision={0} placeholder="e.g. 4" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
          <PerkTexts base={[index, 'freeItem']} />
        </Card>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

/** The collapsed line of a tier: its name, where it starts, and whether it is the base tier. */
function TierHeader({ index }: { index: number }) {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<EarningPointsFormInput>();
  const name: unknown = Form.useWatch(['tiers', index, 'name'], form);
  const minPoints: unknown = Form.useWatch(['tiers', index, 'minPoints'], form);
  const multiplier: unknown = Form.useWatch(['tiers', index, 'earnMultiplier'], form);
  return (
    <span className="tier-header">
      <CrownOutlined aria-hidden="true" />
      <span className="tier-header__name">{typeof name === 'string' && name.trim() ? name : 'New tier'}</span>
      {typeof minPoints === 'number' && <span className="settings-muted">from {formatCount(minPoints)} points</span>}
      {typeof multiplier === 'number' && <span className="settings-muted">· {multiplier}× birthday points</span>}
      {minPoints === 0 && <Tag color="blue">Base tier</Tag>}
    </span>
  );
}

function TierList() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<string[]>([]);

  return (
    <Form.List name="tiers">
      {(rows, { add, remove }, { errors }) => (
        <>
          <Collapse
            activeKey={open}
            onChange={(keys) => setOpen([keys].flat().map(String))}
            items={rows.map((row) => ({
              key: String(row.name),
              label: <TierHeader index={row.name} />,
              forceRender: true,
              extra: (
                <Popconfirm
                  title="Delete this tier?"
                  description="It is removed when you save. The server refuses this if customers are in the tier."
                  okText="Delete"
                  okButtonProps={{ danger: true }}
                  cancelText="Cancel"
                  onConfirm={() => {
                    setOpen([]);
                    remove(row.name);
                  }}
                  onPopupClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
                >
                  <Button
                    size="small"
                    type="text"
                    danger
                    icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
                    onClick={(event) => event.stopPropagation()}
                    aria-label="Delete tier"
                  />
                </Popconfirm>
              ),
              children: <TierFields index={row.name} />,
            }))}
          />
          <Form.ErrorList errors={errors} />
          <Button
            className="edit-list__add"
            icon={<PlusOutlined />}
            onClick={() => {
              add({ earnMultiplier: 1, benefits: '', discount: {}, cashback: {}, freeItem: {} });
              setOpen([String(rows.length)]);
            }}
          >
            Add tier
          </Button>
        </>
      )}
    </Form.List>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/settings/components/ReferralTab.tsx =====
import { Card, Col, DatePicker, Form, Input, InputNumber, Row, Select, Typography } from 'antd';
import dayjs from 'dayjs';
import { FormSection } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { SwitchCard } from '@/components/ui/SwitchCard';
import { COUPON_TARGET_LABELS, COUPON_TARGETS, COUPON_TYPES, COUPON_VALUE_TYPES } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import { getCouponTypeLabel, getValueTypeLabel } from '@/features/coupons/format';
import { getOrderStatusMeta } from '@/features/orders/format';
import { ORDER_STATUSES } from '@/features/orders/types';
import { SettingsForm } from '@/features/settings/components/SettingsForm';
import {
  bonusFields,
  inviteFields,
  referralEndTime,
  referralSchema,
  type ReferralFormInput,
} from '@/features/settings/schemas/referral.schema';
import type { ReferralSettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { zodRule } from '@/lib/zod-form';

type TabProps = { onDirtyChange: (section: 'referral', dirty: boolean) => void };

const valueTypeOptions = COUPON_VALUE_TYPES.map((value) => ({ value, label: getValueTypeLabel(value) }));
const targetOptions = COUPON_TARGETS.map((value) => ({ value, label: COUPON_TARGET_LABELS.get(value) }));
const typeOptions = COUPON_TYPES.map((value) => ({ value, label: getCouponTypeLabel(value) }));
const statusOptions = ORDER_STATUSES.map((value) => ({ value, label: getOrderStatusMeta(value).label }));

function toFormValues(value: ReferralSettings): ReferralFormInput {
  return { ...value, endTime: value.endTime ? dayjs(value.endTime) : undefined };
}

/** The fields both referral coupons have. */
function CouponFields({ coupon }: { coupon: 'invite' | 'bonus' }) {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<ReferralFormInput>();
  const valueType = Form.useWatch([coupon, 'valueType'], form);
  const fields = coupon === 'invite' ? inviteFields : bonusFields;
  const isPercent = valueType === 'PERCENT';

  return (
    <Row gutter={16}>
      <Col span={24}>
        <Form.Item label="Title" name={[coupon, 'title']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.title)]}>
          <Input maxLength={60} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item label="Value type" name={[coupon, 'valueType']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.valueType)]}>
          <Select options={valueTypeOptions} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item label="Value" name={[coupon, 'value']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.value)]}>
          <InputNumber
            min={0}
            prefix={isPercent ? undefined : '₹'}
            suffix={isPercent ? '%' : undefined}
            style={{ width: '100%' }}
          />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item
          label="Max discount"
          name={[coupon, 'maxDiscount']}
          extra="Leave empty for no cap."
          rules={[zodRule(fields.maxDiscount)]}
        >
          <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item
          label="Minimum bill amount"
          name={[coupon, 'minBillAmount']}
          required
          extra="0 means any amount."
          rules={[zodRule(fields.minBillAmount)]}
        >
          <InputNumber min={0} prefix="₹" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item
          label="Max redemption count"
          name={[coupon, 'maxRedemptions']}
          extra="Leave empty for no limit."
          rules={[zodRule(fields.maxRedemptions)]}
        >
          <InputNumber min={1} precision={0} placeholder="No limit" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
      <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
        <Form.Item label="Target type" name={[coupon, 'target']} required rules={[zodRule(fields.target)]}>
          <Select options={targetOptions} />
        </Form.Item>
      </Col>
    </Row>
  );
}

/** What a friend receives when a customer shares their referral code. */
function SharePreview() {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<ReferralFormInput>();
  const shareMessage: unknown = Form.useWatch(['invite', 'shareMessage'], form);
  const shareLink: unknown = Form.useWatch(['invite', 'shareLink'], form);
  return (
    <>
      <Typography.Text className="settings-subhead">What friends receive</Typography.Text>
      <div className="referral-share-preview" aria-label="Share message preview">
        {typeof shareMessage === 'string' && shareMessage.trim() ? shareMessage.trim() : 'Share message'} RAHUL1234
        {'\n'}
        {typeof shareLink === 'string' && shareLink.trim() ? shareLink.trim() : 'https://…'}
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

/** Referral tab: the coupon a new user gets, and the bonus coupon the person who invited them gets. */
export function ReferralTab({ onDirtyChange }: TabProps) {
  return (
    <SettingsForm
      section="referral"
      label="Referral"
      schema={referralSchema}
      toFormValues={toFormValues}
      onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange}
    >
      <FormSection
        title="Referral program"
        description="Customers share their code. The friend gets the invite coupon; the customer gets the bonus coupon."
      >
        <SwitchCard
          name="enabled"
          title="Enable referral program"
          hint="Customers see their referral code and can share it."
        />
        <Row gutter={16}>
          <Col xs={24} md={8}>
            <Form.Item
              label="Referral end time"
              name="endTime"
              extra="After this, new referrals stop working."
              rules={[zodRule(referralEndTime)]}
            >
              <DatePicker showTime={{ format: 'h:mm A' }} format="DD MMM YYYY, h:mm A" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
            </Form.Item>
          </Col>
        </Row>
      </FormSection>

      <div className="referral-columns">
        <Card className="form-section" variant="borderless" title="Referral invite coupon" extra="For the new user">

            label="Share message"

            required
            extra="Filled in when a customer shares their code. Their code is added at the end."

          >
            <Input.TextArea autoSize={{ minRows: 2, maxRows: 4 }} maxLength={300} showCount />
          </Form.Item>
          <Form.Item
            label="Share link"
            name={['invite', 'shareLink']}
            required
            extra="Sent with the message. A smart link that opens the app or website works best."
            rules={[zodRule(inviteFields.shareLink)]}
          >
            <Input placeholder="https://" inputMode="url" />
          </Form.Item>
          <SharePreview />
        </Card>

        <Card className="form-section" variant="borderless" title="Referral bonus coupon" extra="For the referrer">
          <Form.Item label="Coupon type" name={['bonus', 'type']} required rules={[zodRule(bonusFields.type)]}>
            <Select options={typeOptions} />
          </Form.Item>
          <CouponFields coupon="bonus" />
          <Row gutter={16}>
            <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Referrer validity"
                name={['bonus', 'referrerValidityDays']}
                required
                extra="Days the bonus coupon stays valid."
                rules={[zodRule(bonusFields.referrerValidityDays)]}
              >
                <InputNumber min={1} precision={0} suffix="days" style={{ width: '100%' }} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
            <Col xs={24} sm={12}>
              <Form.Item
                label="Bonus trigger status"
                name={['bonus', 'triggerStatus']}
                required
                extra="The referrer gets the bonus coupon when the friend's order reaches this status."
                rules={[zodRule(bonusFields.triggerStatus)]}
              >
                <Select options={statusOptions} />
              </Form.Item>
            </Col>
          </Row>
        </Card>
      </div>
    </SettingsForm>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/settings/components/SettingsForm.tsx =====
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { App, Button, Form, Skeleton, Typography } from 'antd';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import type { z } from 'zod';
import { FormActions } from '@/components/ui/PageLayout';
import { LoadError } from '@/components/ui/LoadError';
import { settingsQueryKeys } from '@/features/settings/api/query-keys';
import { getSettings, saveSettings } from '@/features/settings/api/settings-api';
import type { SettingsTabKey } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import type { SettingsMap } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { showZodErrorsInForm } from '@/lib/zod-form';
import '@/features/settings/settings.css';

type SettingsFormProps<K extends SettingsTabKey, S extends z.ZodType<SettingsMap[K]>> = {
  section: K;
  /** Short name used in messages, for example "Billing". */
  label: string;
  /** Rules that check the form values and turn them into the saved settings. */
  schema: S;
  /** Turns the saved settings into what the form holds (dates as pickers, lists as text, ...). */
  toFormValues: (value: SettingsMap[K]) => z.input<S>;
  /** Tells the page when this tab has changes that are not saved, so the tab can show a dot. */
  onDirtyChange: (section: K, dirty: boolean) => void;
  /** The fields. They reach the form with `Form.useFormInstance()`. */
  children: ReactNode;
};

type LoadedFormProps<K extends SettingsTabKey, S extends z.ZodType<SettingsMap[K]>> = SettingsFormProps<K, S> & {
  saved: SettingsMap[K];
};

/** What the Ant Design form holds. The tab's schema checks it on save. */
type FormValues = Record<string, unknown>;

function LoadedForm<K extends SettingsTabKey, S extends z.ZodType<SettingsMap[K]>>({
  section,
  label,
  schema,
  toFormValues,
  onDirtyChange,
  children,
  saved,
}: LoadedFormProps<K, S>) {
  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
  const toValues = (value: SettingsMap[K]) => toFormValues(value) as FormValues;
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { message } = App.useApp();
  const [dirty, setDirty] = useState(false);

  const markDirty = (value: boolean) => {
    setDirty(value);
    onDirtyChange(section, value);
  };

  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: saveSettings<K>,
    onSuccess: (value) => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(settingsQueryKeys.section(section), value);
      // Show the saved values as the server keeps them (for example tiers sorted, slot names filled in).
      form.setFieldsValue(toValues(value));
      markDirty(false);
      void message.success(`${label} settings were saved.`);
    },
    onError: () => {
      void message.error(`The ${label.toLowerCase()} settings could not be saved. Please try again.`);
    },
  });

  const handleFinish = (values: FormValues) => {
    const result = schema.safeParse(values);
    if (!result.success) {
      showZodErrorsInForm(form, result.error);
      return;
    }
    mutation.mutate({ section, value: result.data });
  };

  const discard = () => {
    form.resetFields();
    markDirty(false);
  };

  return (
    <Form<FormValues>
      form={form}
      layout="vertical"
      initialValues={toValues(saved)}
      onFinish={handleFinish}
      onValuesChange={() => {
        if (!dirty) markDirty(true);
      }}
      scrollToFirstError={{ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }}
      disabled={mutation.isPending}
      autoComplete="off"
      className="settings-tab"
    >
      {children}

      <FormActions>
        <Typography.Text className={`settings-save-note${dirty ? ' settings-save-note--dirty' : ''}`}>
          {dirty ? 'You have unsaved changes' : 'All changes saved'}
        </Typography.Text>
        <Button onClick={discard} disabled={!dirty || mutation.isPending}>
          Discard changes
        </Button>
        <Button type="primary" htmlType="submit" loading={mutation.isPending} disabled={!dirty}>
          Save {label.toLowerCase()} settings
        </Button>
      </FormActions>
    </Form>
  );
}

/**
 * The frame of every settings tab: loads its settings, shows the fields, and saves them with
 * the bar at the bottom. Each tab is loaded and saved on its own.
 */
export function SettingsForm<K extends SettingsTabKey, S extends z.ZodType<SettingsMap[K]>>(
  props: SettingsFormProps<K, S>,
) {
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: settingsQueryKeys.section(props.section),
    queryFn: () => getSettings(props.section),
  });

  if (query.isError) {
    return <LoadError what={`${props.label.toLowerCase()} settings`} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} />;
  }
  if (query.isPending) return <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 8 }} />;
  return <LoadedForm {...props} saved={query.data} />;
}

import { DeleteOutlined, PlusOutlined, SearchOutlined } from '@ant-design/icons';

// ---------- Fixed OTP ----------

const fixedOtpToForm = (value: FixedOtpSettings): FixedOtpFormInput => value;

function FixedOtpList() {
  const form = Form.useFormInstance<FixedOtpFormInput>();
  const entries = Form.useWatch('entries', form) ?? [];
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const term = search.trim().toLowerCase();

  const matches = (index: number) => {
    if (!term) return true;
    const entry = entries.at(index);
    return `${entry?.phone ?? ''} ${entry?.comment ?? ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(term);
  };

  return (
    <Form.List name="entries">
      {(rows, { add, remove }, { errors }) => (
        <>
          <Row gutter={12} align="middle" className="settings-list-toolbar">
            <Col flex="auto">
              <Input
                allowClear
                prefix={<SearchOutlined />}
                placeholder="Search phone number or comment"
                value={search}
                onChange={(event) => setSearch(event.target.value)}
                aria-label="Search fixed OTP numbers"
                className="settings-list-search"
              />
            </Col>
            <Col>
              <Button
                type="primary"
                ghost
                icon={<PlusOutlined />}
                onClick={() => {
                  setSearch('');
                  add({ phone: '', otp: '123456' }, 0);
                }}
              >
                Add number
              </Button>
            </Col>
          </Row>

          <div className="edit-list">
            <div className="edit-list__row edit-list__head otp-row">
              <span>Phone number</span>
              <span>Fixed OTP</span>
              <span>Comment</span>
              <span />
            </div>
            {rows.length === 0 && <div className="edit-list__empty">No fixed OTP numbers.</div>}
            {/* Rows that don't match the search are hidden, not removed, so they are still saved. */}
            {rows.map((row, index) => (
              <div className="edit-list__row otp-row" key={row.key} hidden={!matches(index)}>
                <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'phone']} rules={[zodRule(fixedOtpFields.phone)]}>
                  <Input placeholder="10-digit number" maxLength={10} inputMode="numeric" aria-label="Phone number" />
                </Form.Item>
                <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'otp']} rules={[zodRule(fixedOtpFields.otp)]}>
                  <Input maxLength={6} inputMode="numeric" className="settings-mono" aria-label="Fixed OTP" />
                </Form.Item>
                <Form.Item name={[row.name, 'comment']} rules={[zodRule(fixedOtpFields.comment)]}>
                  <Input placeholder="Why this number has a fixed OTP" maxLength={100} aria-label="Comment" />
                </Form.Item>
                <Tooltip title="Remove number">
                  <Button
                    type="text"
                    danger
                    icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
                    onClick={() => remove(row.name)}
                    aria-label="Remove number"
                  />
                </Tooltip>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <Form.ErrorList errors={errors} />
          <div className="settings-list-note settings-muted">
            {formatCount(rows.length)} numbers
            {term ? ` · ${formatCount(rows.filter((_, i) => matches(i)).length)} match the search` : ''}
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </Form.List>
  );
}

/** Fixed OTP tab: phone numbers that always sign in with the same OTP. */

    <SettingsForm
      section="fixed-otp"
      label="Fixed OTP"
      schema={fixedOtpSchema}
      toFormValues={fixedOtpToForm}
      onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange}
    >
      <FormSection
        title="Fixed OTP numbers"
        description="These phone numbers always sign in with the OTP set here, without an SMS."
      >
        <Alert
          type="warning"
          showIcon
          className="settings-alert"
          title="Anyone who knows one of these numbers and its OTP can sign in to that account."
          description="Use them only for test accounts and app store review accounts, and remove numbers you no longer need."
        />
        <FixedOtpList />
      </FormSection>
    </SettingsForm>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/settings/constants.ts =====
/** The tabs of the Settings page, in order. The key is also used in the address: /settings?tab=delivery */
export const SETTINGS_TABS = [
  { key: 'billing', label: 'Billing' },
  { key: 'delivery', label: 'Delivery' },
  { key: 'packing', label: 'Packing' },
  { key: 'referral', label: 'Referral' },
  { key: 'fixed-otp', label: 'Fixed OTP' },
  { key: 'order-emails', label: 'Order emails' },
  { key: 'earning-points', label: 'Earning points' },
] as const;

export type SettingsTabKey = (typeof SETTINGS_TABS)[number]['key'];

/** Which total of the bill a minimum-bill or free-delivery rule is checked against. */
export const BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS = ['GROSS', 'BILL', 'TOTAL'] as const;
export type BillAmountField = (typeof BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS)[number];

export const BILL_AMOUNT_FIELD_META = new Map<BillAmountField, { label: string; hint: string }>([
  ['GROSS', { label: 'Gross amount', hint: 'Cost of all items, without tax.' }],
  ['BILL', { label: 'Bill amount', hint: 'Cost of all items, with tax.' }],
  ['TOTAL', { label: 'Total amount', hint: 'Bill amount after any discount.' }],
]);

export const DELIVERY_CHARGE_MODES = ['FIXED', 'KM'] as const;
export type DeliveryChargeMode = (typeof DELIVERY_CHARGE_MODES)[number];

/** Delivery days a customer can pick, counted from today: 0 is today, 1 is tomorrow, up to 7. */
export const DELIVERY_DATE_OFFSETS = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7] as const;

export function getDeliveryDateLabel(offset: number): string {
  if (offset === 0) return 'Today';
  if (offset === 1) return 'Tomorrow';
  return `T + ${offset}`;
}

/** The Delivery Item ID is a 24-character catalog ID. */
export const CATALOG_ID_PATTERN = /^[a-f0-9]{24}$/i;

/** Protein points must expire at least this many minutes after they are earned (the wallet needs over 1 hour). */
export const MIN_POINTS_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 61;

/** Default cooldown for tier discounts and cashback when the box is left empty. */
export const DEFAULT_COOLDOWN_DAYS = 7;

===== FILE: src/features/settings/pages/SettingsPage.tsx =====
import { Card, Tabs, Typography } from 'antd';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { useBeforeUnload, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { BillingTab } from '@/features/settings/components/BillingTab';
import { DeliveryTab } from '@/features/settings/components/DeliveryTab';
import { EarningPointsTab } from '@/features/settings/components/EarningPointsTab';
import { ReferralTab } from '@/features/settings/components/ReferralTab';
import { FixedOtpTab, OrderEmailsTab, PackingTab } from '@/features/settings/components/SimpleTabs';
import { SETTINGS_TABS, type SettingsTabKey } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import '@/features/settings/settings.css';

function isTabKey(value: string | null): value is SettingsTabKey {
  return SETTINGS_TABS.some((tab) => tab.key === value);
}

/**
 * Store settings, one tab per topic. Each tab loads and saves on its own.
 * The open tab is kept in the address (/settings?tab=delivery), so a refresh or a shared link opens it again.
 */
export default function SettingsPage() {
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const requested = searchParams.get('tab');
  const activeTab: SettingsTabKey = isTabKey(requested) ? requested : 'billing';
  const [dirtyTabs, setDirtyTabs] = useState<ReadonlySet<SettingsTabKey>>(new Set());

  const onDirtyChange = (section: SettingsTabKey, dirty: boolean) => {
    setDirtyTabs((current) => {
      if (current.has(section) === dirty) return current;
      const next = new Set(current);
      if (dirty) next.add(section);
      else next.delete(section);
      return next;
    });
  };

  // Closing or reloading the browser tab with unsaved changes asks first.
  useBeforeUnload((event) => {
    if (dirtyTabs.size > 0) event.preventDefault();
  });

  const panels: Record<SettingsTabKey, ReactNode> = {
    billing: <BillingTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    delivery: <DeliveryTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    packing: <PackingTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    referral: <ReferralTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    'fixed-otp': <FixedOtpTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    'order-emails': <OrderEmailsTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
    'earning-points': <EarningPointsTab onDirtyChange={onDirtyChange} />,
  };
  const panelFor = (key: SettingsTabKey) => new Map(Object.entries(panels)).get(key);

  return (
    <div className="settings-page">
      <Card className="settings-tabs" variant="borderless">
        <Typography.Title level={4} className="list-panel__title">
          Store settings
        </Typography.Title>
        <Typography.Text className="settings-muted">
          Charges, delivery, referrals and loyalty for your store. Each tab is saved on its own.
        </Typography.Text>
        <Tabs
          activeKey={activeTab}
          onChange={(key) => setSearchParams({ tab: key }, { replace: true })}
          items={SETTINGS_TABS.map((tab) => ({
            key: tab.key,
            label: (
              <span>
                {tab.label}
                {dirtyTabs.has(tab.key) && (
                  <span className="settings-tab-dirty" role="img" aria-label="has unsaved changes" />
                )}
              </span>
            ),
            children: panelFor(tab.key),
          }))}
        />
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/billing.schema.ts =====
import { z } from 'zod';
import { BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import { amount } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';

const minimumBillRule = z
  .object({
    enabled: z.boolean().default(false),
    amount: amount('Enter the minimum bill amount', 1_000_000),
    field: z.enum(BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS, { error: 'Choose which amount to check' }),
  })
  .superRefine((rule, ctx) => {
    if (rule.enabled && rule.amount <= 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['amount'], message: 'Enter an amount above 0, or switch this off' });
    }
  });

/** Billing tab rules. */
export const billingSchema = z.object({
  customer: minimumBillRule,
  employee: minimumBillRule,
});

export const billingFields = {
  amount: amount('Enter the minimum bill amount', 1_000_000),
  field: z.enum(BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS, { error: 'Choose which amount to check' }),
};

export type BillingFormInput = z.input<typeof billingSchema>;

===== FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/delivery.schema.ts =====
import type { Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { z } from 'zod';
import {
  BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS,
  CATALOG_ID_PATTERN,
  DELIVERY_CHARGE_MODES,
  DELIVERY_DATE_OFFSETS,
} from '@/features/settings/constants';
import {
  amount,
  isValidDayjs,
  optionalTimeOfDay,
  percent,
  wholeNumber,
} from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import type { DeliverySettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { optionalText, optionalWholeNumber } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

const MAX_SLOTS = 24;

const dayjsValue = z.custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick the start and end time' });

/** '09:00' → '9AM', '13:30' → '1:30PM'. Used to name a slot that was left without a name. */
function shortTime(time: string): string {
  const [hour = 0, minute = 0] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  const suffix = hour < 12 ? 'AM' : 'PM';
  const h = hour % 12 === 0 ? 12 : hour % 12;
  return minute ? `${h}:${String(minute).padStart(2, '0')}${suffix}` : `${h}${suffix}`;
}

export const deliveryFields = {
  chargeMode: z.enum(DELIVERY_CHARGE_MODES),
  fixedCharge: amount('Enter the delivery charge', 100_000),
  bandUpToKm: z.number({ error: 'Enter the distance' }).positive('Use more than 0').max(500, 'Use 500 km or less'),
  bandCharge: amount('Enter the charge', 100_000),
  maxDistanceKm: z
    .number()
    .positive('Use more than 0')
    .max(500, 'Use 500 km or less')
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? undefined),
  taxPercent: percent('Enter the tax, or 0 for none'),
  exemptionLimit: amount('Enter the limit, or 0 to always charge delivery'),
  exemptionField: z.enum(BILL_AMOUNT_FIELDS),
  deliveryItemId: optionalText(24).refine(
    (value) => !value || CATALOG_ID_PATTERN.test(value),
    'Use the 24-character ID of the catalog item',
  ),
  deliveryTimeMinutes: wholeNumber('Enter the delivery time', 0, 1440),
  maxCartQuantity: optionalWholeNumber(100_000).transform((value) => value || undefined),
  cutOffTime: optionalTimeOfDay,
  cutOffSlot: optionalText(40),
  slotName: optionalText(40),
  slotTime: z.tuple([dayjsValue, dayjsValue], { error: 'Pick the start and end time' }),
  slotMaxOrders: wholeNumber('Enter the max orders', 1, 100_000),
  slotLeadTime: wholeNumber('Enter the lead time', 0, 1440),
  liveOrderCutOffTime: optionalTimeOfDay,
};

const deliveryFormSchema = z.object({
  chargeEnabled: z.boolean().default(false),
  chargeMode: deliveryFields.chargeMode,
  fixedCharge: deliveryFields.fixedCharge,
  distanceBands: z
    .array(z.object({ upToKm: deliveryFields.bandUpToKm, charge: deliveryFields.bandCharge }))
    .max(20, 'Use 20 bands or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((bands) => [...(bands ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.upToKm - b.upToKm)),
  maxDistanceKm: deliveryFields.maxDistanceKm,
  taxPercent: deliveryFields.taxPercent,
  exemptionLimit: deliveryFields.exemptionLimit,
  exemptionField: deliveryFields.exemptionField,
  deliveryItemId: deliveryFields.deliveryItemId,
  deliveryTimeMinutes: deliveryFields.deliveryTimeMinutes,
  maxCartQuantity: deliveryFields.maxCartQuantity,

  slotsEnabled: z.boolean().default(false),
  cutOffTime: deliveryFields.cutOffTime,
  cutOffSlot: deliveryFields.cutOffSlot,
  deliveryDateOffsets: z
    .array(z.number().refine((value) => (DELIVERY_DATE_OFFSETS as readonly number[]).includes(value)))
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => [...(value ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a - b)),
  slots: z
    .array(
      z.object({
        displayName: deliveryFields.slotName,
        time: deliveryFields.slotTime,
        maxOrders: deliveryFields.slotMaxOrders,
        leadTimeMinutes: deliveryFields.slotLeadTime,
      }),
    )
    .max(MAX_SLOTS, `Use ${MAX_SLOTS} slots or fewer`)
    .nullish()
    .transform((slots) =>
      (slots ?? []).map((slot) => {
        const start = slot.time[0].format('HH:mm');
        const end = slot.time[1].format('HH:mm');
        return {
          displayName: slot.displayName ?? `${shortTime(start)} to ${shortTime(end)}`,
          start,
          end,
          maxOrders: slot.maxOrders,
          leadTimeMinutes: slot.leadTimeMinutes,
        };
      }),
    ),

  nonDeliveryDaysEnabled: z.boolean().default(false),
  nonDeliveryDates: z
    .array(z.custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick a valid date' }))
    .max(366, 'Add 366 dates or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((dates) => [...new Set((dates ?? []).map((date) => date.format('YYYY-MM-DD')))].sort()),

  liveOrderCutOffEnabled: z.boolean().default(false),
  liveOrderCutOffTime: deliveryFields.liveOrderCutOffTime,
});

type DeliveryFormOutput = z.output<typeof deliveryFormSchema>;

function checkDelivery(values: DeliveryFormOutput, ctx: z.RefinementCtx) {
  if (values.chargeEnabled && values.chargeMode === 'KM') {
    if (values.distanceBands.length === 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['distanceBands'], message: 'Add at least one distance band' });
    }
    const distances = values.distanceBands.map((band) => band.upToKm);
    if (new Set(distances).size !== distances.length) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['distanceBands'], message: 'Two bands have the same distance' });
    }
    const farthest = distances.at(-1);
    if (values.maxDistanceKm !== undefined && farthest !== undefined && values.maxDistanceKm < farthest) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        path: ['maxDistanceKm'],
        message: `The farthest band goes up to ${farthest} km, so use at least that`,
      });
    }
  }

  if (values.slotsEnabled) {
    if (values.deliveryDateOffsets.length === 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['deliveryDateOffsets'], message: 'Choose at least one delivery day' });
    }
    if (values.slots.length === 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['slots'], message: 'Add at least one delivery slot' });
    }
    values.slots.forEach((slot, index) => {
      if (slot.end <= slot.start) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['slots', index, 'time'], message: 'The end must be after the start' });
      }
    });
    // Two slots that share time confuse customers and the max-orders count.
    const sorted = values.slots
      .map((slot, index) => ({ ...slot, index }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start));
    sorted.slice(1).forEach((slot, position) => {
      const previous = sorted.at(position);
      if (previous && slot.start < previous.end) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['slots', slot.index, 'time'],
          message: `Overlaps with "${previous.displayName}"`,
        });
      }
    });
    if (values.cutOffSlot && !values.slots.some((slot) => slot.displayName === values.cutOffSlot)) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['cutOffSlot'], message: 'This slot no longer exists. Choose another one' });
    }
  }

  if (values.nonDeliveryDaysEnabled && values.nonDeliveryDates.length === 0) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['nonDeliveryDates'], message: 'Pick at least one date, or switch this off' });
  }
  if (values.liveOrderCutOffEnabled && !values.liveOrderCutOffTime) {
    ctx.addIssue({
      code: 'custom',
      path: ['liveOrderCutOffTime'],
      message: 'Pick the cut-off time, or switch this off',
    });
  }
}

/** Delivery tab rules. Turns the form values into the saved settings. */
export const deliverySchema = deliveryFormSchema
  .superRefine(checkDelivery)
  .transform((values): DeliverySettings => values);

export type DeliveryFormInput = z.input<typeof deliveryFormSchema>;
===== END FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/delivery.schema.ts =====

===== FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/earning-points.schema.ts =====
import { z } from 'zod';
import { CATALOG_ID_PATTERN, MIN_POINTS_EXPIRY_MINUTES } from '@/features/settings/constants';
import { amount, optionalDate, percent, wholeNumber } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import type { EarningPointsSettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { optionalAmount, optionalPercent, optionalText, optionalWholeNumber, requiredText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

const MAX_TIERS = 20;

const appText = optionalText(60);

const perk = z.object({
  percent: optionalPercent(),
  cooldownDays: optionalWholeNumber(365),
  applyText: appText,
  confirmationText: appText,
  cartText: appText,
});

export const earningPointsFields = {
  redeemablePercent: percent('Enter the percentage'),
  pointsPerRupee: wholeNumber('Enter the points for ₹1', 1, 1_000_000),
  itemsMinBill: amount('Enter the amount, or 0 for any bill'),
  redemptionMinBill: amount('Enter the amount, or 0 for any bill'),
  expiryMinutes: wholeNumber('Enter the expiry in minutes', MIN_POINTS_EXPIRY_MINUTES, 5_256_000),
  openingDate: optionalDate,
  tierWindowDays: wholeNumber('Enter the number of days', 1, 3650),
  tierName: requiredText('Enter the tier name', 40),
  tierMinPoints: wholeNumber('Enter the minimum points', 0, 100_000_000),
  earnMultiplier: z.number({ error: 'Enter the multiplier' }).min(1, 'Use 1 or more').max(10, 'Use 10 or less'),
  freeDeliveryMin: optionalAmount(1_000_000).transform((value) => value || undefined),
  benefits: z
    .string()
    .max(2000, 'Use 2000 characters or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((text) =>
      (text ?? '')
        .split('\n')
        .map((line) => line.trim())
        .filter(Boolean),
    ),
  percent: optionalPercent(),
  cooldownDays: optionalWholeNumber(365),
  appText,
  freeItemId: optionalText(24).refine(
    (value) => !value || CATALOG_ID_PATTERN.test(value),
    'Use the 24-character ID of the catalog item',
  ),
  everyN: optionalWholeNumber(1000).transform((value) => value || undefined),
};

const tier = z.object({
  name: earningPointsFields.tierName,
  minPoints: earningPointsFields.tierMinPoints,
  earnMultiplier: earningPointsFields.earnMultiplier,
  freeDeliveryMin: earningPointsFields.freeDeliveryMin,
  benefits: earningPointsFields.benefits,
  discount: perk,
  cashback: perk,
  freeItem: z.object({
    itemId: earningPointsFields.freeItemId,
    everyN: earningPointsFields.everyN,
    applyText: appText,
    confirmationText: appText,
    cartText: appText,
  }),
});

const earningPointsFormSchema = z.object({
  redeemablePercent: earningPointsFields.redeemablePercent,
  pointsPerRupee: earningPointsFields.pointsPerRupee,
  itemsMinBill: earningPointsFields.itemsMinBill,
  includeDeliveryCharges: z.boolean().default(false),
  redemptionMinBill: earningPointsFields.redemptionMinBill,
  restrictMultipleItems: z.boolean().default(false),
  expiryMinutes: earningPointsFields.expiryMinutes,
  openingPointsDate: earningPointsFields.openingDate,
  openingWalletDate: earningPointsFields.openingDate,
  tierWindowDays: earningPointsFields.tierWindowDays,
  tiers: z
    .array(tier)
    .max(MAX_TIERS, `Use ${MAX_TIERS} tiers or fewer`)
    .nullish()
    .transform((tiers) => tiers ?? []),
});

/** Earning points tab rules. Tiers are saved from lowest to highest. */
export const earningPointsSchema = earningPointsFormSchema
  .superRefine((values, ctx) => {
    if (values.tiers.length > 0 && !values.tiers.some((item) => item.minPoints === 0)) {
      ctx.addIssue({
        code: 'custom',
        path: ['tiers'],
        message: 'One tier must start at 0 points: it is the base tier every customer starts in',
      });
    }
    const names = new Set<string>();
    const points = new Set<number>();
    values.tiers.forEach((item, index) => {
      const key = item.name.toLowerCase();
      if (names.has(key)) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['tiers', index, 'name'], message: 'Another tier has this name' });
      }
      if (points.has(item.minPoints)) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['tiers', index, 'minPoints'],
          message: 'Another tier starts at these points',
        });
      }
      names.add(key);
      points.add(item.minPoints);

      const { itemId, everyN } = item.freeItem;
      if (itemId && !everyN) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['tiers', index, 'freeItem', 'everyN'],
          message: 'Enter every how many orders',
        });
      }
      if (everyN && !itemId) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['tiers', index, 'freeItem', 'itemId'],
          message: 'Enter the free item ID',
        });
      }
    });
  })
  .transform((values): EarningPointsSettings => ({
    ...values,
    tiers: [...values.tiers].sort((a, b) => a.minPoints - b.minPoints),
  }));

export type EarningPointsFormInput = z.input<typeof earningPointsFormSchema>;
===== END FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/earning-points.schema.ts =====

import { z } from 'zod';
import { COUPON_TARGETS, COUPON_TYPES, COUPON_VALUE_TYPES } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import { ORDER_STATUSES } from '@/features/orders/types';
import { amount, wholeNumber } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import type { ReferralSettings } from '@/features/settings/types';
import { optionalAmount, optionalDateTime, requiredText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

/** Fields both referral coupons have. */
export const referralCouponFields = {
  title: requiredText('Enter the coupon title', 60),
  value: z.number({ error: 'Enter the coupon value' }).positive('Use more than 0').max(1_000_000),
  valueType: z.enum(COUPON_VALUE_TYPES, { error: 'Choose the value type' }),
  /** Empty means no limit. The API stores no limit as -1. */
  maxRedemptions: z
    .number()
    .int('Use a whole number')
    .min(1, 'Use 1 or more, or leave empty for no limit')
    .max(1_000_000)
    .nullish()
    .transform((value) => value ?? undefined),
  minBillAmount: amount('Enter the minimum bill, or 0 for any amount'),
  target: z.enum(COUPON_TARGETS, { error: 'Choose the target' }),
  maxDiscount: optionalAmount(1_000_000),
};

export const inviteFields = {
  ...referralCouponFields,
  shareMessage: requiredText('Enter the share message', 300),
  shareLink: z
    .string({ error: 'Enter the share link' })
    .trim()
    .pipe(z.url({ protocol: /^https$/, error: 'Enter a full https:// link' })),
};

export const bonusFields = {
  ...referralCouponFields,
  type: z.enum(COUPON_TYPES, { error: 'Choose the coupon type' }),
  referrerValidityDays: wholeNumber('Enter how many days the coupon stays valid', 1, 3650),
  triggerStatus: z.enum(ORDER_STATUSES, { error: 'Choose the order status' }),
};

export const referralEndTime = optionalDateTime('Pick a valid date and time');

function checkPercentValue(
  values: { valueType: string; value: number },
  ctx: z.RefinementCtx,
  path: 'invite' | 'bonus',
) {
  if (values.valueType === 'PERCENT' && values.value > 100) {
    ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: [path, 'value'], message: 'A percent discount can be 100 at most' });
  }
}

/** Referral tab rules. */
export const referralSchema = z
  .object({
    enabled: z.boolean().default(false),
    invite: z.object(inviteFields),
    bonus: z.object(bonusFields),
    endTime: referralEndTime,
  })
  .superRefine((values, ctx) => {
    checkPercentValue(values.invite, ctx, 'invite');
    checkPercentValue(values.bonus, ctx, 'bonus');
    if (values.enabled && !values.endTime) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['endTime'], message: 'Pick when the referral program ends' });
    }
  })
  .transform((values): ReferralSettings => values);

export type ReferralFormInput = z.input<typeof referralSchema>;

===== FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields.ts =====
import dayjs, { type Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { z } from 'zod';

/** Small building blocks shared by the settings tabs. */

export function isValidDayjs(value: unknown): value is Dayjs {
  return dayjs.isDayjs(value) && value.isValid();
}

/** A required amount of money (or any number) from 0 up to `max`. */
export function amount(message: string, max = 10_000_000) {
  return z
    .number({ error: message })
    .min(0, 'Use 0 or more')
    .max(max, `Use ${max.toLocaleString('en-IN')} or less`);
}

/** A required percentage from 0 to 100, such as a tax rate. */
export function percent(message: string) {
  return z.number({ error: message }).min(0, 'Use 0 or more').max(100, 'Use 100 or less');
}

/** A required whole number from `min` up to `max`. */
export function wholeNumber(message: string, min: number, max: number) {
  return z
    .number({ error: message })
    .int('Use a whole number')
    .min(min, `Use ${min} or more`)
    .max(max, `Use ${max.toLocaleString('en-IN')} or less`);
}

/** A time picked in a time picker. The payload gets "13:30"; empty becomes `undefined`. */
export const optionalTimeOfDay = z
  .custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick a time' })
  .nullish()
  .transform((value) => value?.format('HH:mm'));

/** A date picked in a date picker. The payload gets "2026-12-25"; empty becomes `undefined`. */
export const optionalDate = z
  .custom<Dayjs>(isValidDayjs, { error: 'Pick a date' })
  .nullish()
  .transform((value) => value?.format('YYYY-MM-DD'));

/** "13:30" → a dayjs value the time picker can show. */
export function toTimeValue(time: string | undefined): Dayjs | undefined {
  if (!time) return undefined;
  const [hour = 0, minute = 0] = time.split(':').map(Number);
  return dayjs().hour(hour).minute(minute).second(0).millisecond(0);
}

/** "2026-12-25" → a dayjs value the date picker can show. */
export function toDateValue(date: string | undefined): Dayjs | undefined {
  return date ? dayjs(date) : undefined;
}
===== END FILE: src/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields.ts =====

import { z } from 'zod';
import { emailField } from '@/features/users/schemas/user.schema';
import { amount, percent } from '@/features/settings/schemas/shared-fields';
import { optionalText } from '@/lib/zod-fields';

/** Rules for the small tabs: Packing, Fixed OTP and Order emails. */

// ---------- Packing ----------

export const packingSchema = z
  .object({
    enabled: z.boolean().default(false),
    charge: amount('Enter the packing charge', 100_000),
    taxPercent: percent('Enter the tax, or 0 for none'),
  })
  .superRefine((values, ctx) => {
    if (values.enabled && values.charge <= 0) {
      ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['charge'], message: 'Enter a charge above 0, or switch this off' });
    }
  });

export type PackingFormInput = z.input<typeof packingSchema>;

// ---------- Fixed OTP ----------

export const fixedOtpFields = {
  phone: z
    .string({ error: 'Enter the phone number' })
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{10}$/, 'Use a 10-digit phone number'),
  otp: z
    .string({ error: 'Enter the OTP' })
    .trim()
    .regex(/^\d{4,6}$/, 'Use 4 to 6 digits'),
  comment: optionalText(100),
};

export const fixedOtpSchema = z
  .object({
    entries: z
      .array(z.object(fixedOtpFields))
      .max(500, 'Use 500 numbers or fewer')
      .nullish()
      .transform((entries) => entries ?? []),
  })
  .superRefine((values, ctx) => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    values.entries.forEach((entry, index) => {
      if (seen.has(entry.phone)) {
        ctx.addIssue({
          code: 'custom',
          path: ['entries', index, 'phone'],
          message: 'This number is already in the list',
        });
      }
      seen.add(entry.phone);
    });
  });

export type FixedOtpFormInput = z.input<typeof fixedOtpSchema>;

// ---------- Order emails ----------

export const orderEmailsSchema = z.object({
  employeeEmails: z
    .array(emailField, { error: 'Enter valid email addresses' })
    .max(50, 'Use 50 addresses or fewer')
    .nullish()
    .transform((emails) => [...new Set(emails ?? [])]),
});

export type OrderEmailsFormInput = z.input<typeof orderEmailsSchema>;

===== FILE: src/features/settings/types.ts =====
import type { CouponTarget, CouponType, CouponValueType } from '@/features/coupons/constants';
import type { OrderStatus } from '@/features/orders/types';
import type { BillAmountField, DeliveryChargeMode } from '@/features/settings/constants';

/** A time of day such as "13:30", in the merchant's time zone. */
export type TimeOfDay = string;

// ---------- Billing ----------

export type MinimumBillRule = {
  enabled: boolean;
  amount: number;
  field: BillAmountField;
};

export type BillingSettings = {
  /** Orders placed by customers. */
  customer: MinimumBillRule;
  /** Orders placed by an employee on behalf of a customer. */
  employee: MinimumBillRule;
};

// ---------- Delivery ----------

/** One distance band of a KM-based delivery charge: up to `upToKm` km costs `charge`. */
export type DistanceBand = { upToKm: number; charge: number };

export type DeliverySlot = {
  displayName: string;
  start: TimeOfDay;
  end: TimeOfDay;
  maxOrders: number;
  leadTimeMinutes: number;
};

export type DeliverySettings = {
  chargeEnabled: boolean;
  chargeMode: DeliveryChargeMode;
  /** Used when `chargeMode` is FIXED. */
  fixedCharge: number;
  /** Used when `chargeMode` is KM, sorted by distance. */
  distanceBands: DistanceBand[];
  /** No delivery beyond this distance (KM mode). */
  maxDistanceKm?: number;
  taxPercent: number;
  /** Orders above this amount get free delivery. 0 means delivery is always charged. */
  exemptionLimit: number;
  exemptionField: BillAmountField;
  /** Catalog item used to show the delivery charge on the bill, if any. */
  deliveryItemId?: string;
  deliveryTimeMinutes: number;
  /** Most items allowed in the cart. Missing means no limit. */
  maxCartQuantity?: number;

  slotsEnabled: boolean;
  cutOffTime?: TimeOfDay;
  /** Display name of the slot the cut-off applies to. */
  cutOffSlot?: string;
  deliveryDateOffsets: number[];
  slots: DeliverySlot[];

  nonDeliveryDaysEnabled: boolean;
  /** Dates as "2026-12-25". */
  nonDeliveryDates: string[];

  liveOrderCutOffEnabled: boolean;
  liveOrderCutOffTime?: TimeOfDay;
};

// ---------- Packing ----------

export type PackingSettings = {
  enabled: boolean;
  charge: number;
  taxPercent: number;
};

// ---------- Referral ----------

type ReferralCouponBase = {
  title: string;
  value: number;
  valueType: CouponValueType;
  /** Missing means no limit (the API stores -1). */
  maxRedemptions?: number;
  minBillAmount: number;
  target: CouponTarget;
  maxDiscount?: number;
};

/** Given to the new user who signs up with a referral code. */
export type ReferralInviteCoupon = ReferralCouponBase & {
  shareMessage: string;
  shareLink: string;
};

/** Given to the person who referred them, once the new user's order reaches `triggerStatus`. */
export type ReferralBonusCoupon = ReferralCouponBase & {
  type: CouponType;
  referrerValidityDays: number;
  triggerStatus: OrderStatus;
};

export type ReferralSettings = {
  enabled: boolean;
  invite: ReferralInviteCoupon;
  bonus: ReferralBonusCoupon;
  /** ISO date and time. */
  endTime?: string;
};

// ---------- Fixed OTP ----------

/** A phone number that always signs in with the same OTP, for test and app-review accounts. */
export type FixedOtp = {
  phone: string;
  otp: string;
  comment?: string;
};

export type FixedOtpSettings = { entries: FixedOtp[] };

// ---------- Order emails ----------

export type OrderEmailSettings = {
  /** Employees who get an email for every new order. */
  employeeEmails: string[];
};

// ---------- Earning points ----------

/** A tier perk the customer applies in the app: a discount or a wallet cashback. */
export type TierPerk = {
  percent?: number;
  cooldownDays?: number;
  /** Button text in the app ("apply CTA"). */
  applyText?: string;
  confirmationText?: string;
  cartText?: string;
};

export type TierFreeItem = {
  itemId?: string;
  /** Free item on every N-th order. */
  everyN?: number;
  applyText?: string;
  confirmationText?: string;
  cartText?: string;
};

export type LoyaltyTier = {
  name: string;
  minPoints: number;
  /** Earn multiplier in the customer's birthday month. */
  earnMultiplier: number;
  /** Free delivery above this bill amount. Missing means off. */
  freeDeliveryMin?: number;
  benefits: string[];
  discount: TierPerk;
  cashback: TierPerk;
  freeItem: TierFreeItem;
};

export type EarningPointsSettings = {
  redeemablePercent: number;
  pointsPerRupee: number;
  itemsMinBill: number;
  includeDeliveryCharges: boolean;
  redemptionMinBill: number;
  restrictMultipleItems: boolean;
  expiryMinutes: number;
  /** Dates as "2026-09-01". */
  openingPointsDate?: string;
  openingWalletDate?: string;
  tierWindowDays: number;
  tiers: LoyaltyTier[];
};

/** Every settings section, by tab. */
export type SettingsMap = {
  billing: BillingSettings;
  delivery: DeliverySettings;
  packing: PackingSettings;
  referral: ReferralSettings;
  'fixed-otp': FixedOtpSettings;
  'order-emails': OrderEmailSettings;
  'earning-points': EarningPointsSettings;
};
===== END FILE: src/features/settings/types.ts =====
